~~~python
        y = host.act(w, s)
        for (p, x, c) in host.comps(w, y, s):
            if x not in T:
                continue
            c = comp_of(host, w, y, s, p, x)
            if host.orient[x] * c <= eps:
                continue
            ok = False
            for (U, f, w2) in settings(host, w, T, fracs):
                A, D, tot = branches(host, w, w2, s, p, x)
                if abs(tot) > eps and abs(A) > eps:
                    ok = True
                    break
            if not ok:
                continue
            if not plus:
                return (s, p, x, c)
            good = True
            for f_ in [f_ for f_ in fracs if f_ < 1]:
                w2 = setT(host, w, T, f_)
                if not host.orient[x] * total_comp(host, w2, s, p, x) < host.orient[x] * c:
                    good = False
                    break
            if good:
                return (s, p, x, c)
    return None

def net_deep(host, w, protocol, T, eps):
    """实际净加深：施协议后 T 中某位取向深度之增 > eps。返回 [(x, 前, 后)]。"""
    w2 = dict(w)
    for s in protocol:
        w2 = host.step(w2, s)
    return [(x, depth(host, w, x), depth(host, w2, x)) for x in T if depth(host, w2, x) - depth(host, w, x) > eps]

def reach_pairs(host, hists):
    out = []
    for h in hists:
        S = run(host, host.init, h)
        for k, s in enumerate(h):
            out.append((S[k], s))
    return out

class UF:
    def __init__(self, items):
        self.p = {i: i for i in items}
    def f(self, i):
        while self.p[i] != i:
            self.p[i] = self.p[self.p[i]]
            i = self.p[i]
        return i
    def u(self, a, b):
        ra, rb = self.f(a), self.f(b)
        if ra != rb:
            self.p[rb] = ra

def _act_dep(host, w, w2, s, eps):
    y0, y1 = host.act(w, s), host.act(w2, s)
    return [j for j in y0 if abs(y0[j] - y1[j]) > eps]

def org_graph_exact(host, w, E, probes, eps, fracs=FRACS):
    """精确枚举：在 E 的全部非空子集 × 深度倍数（申报的联合设定族）上求依赖。
    共同参与超边：使活动分量 j 改变的极小子集 U（|U| ≥ 2 时为超边，|U| = 1 时并入同一 j 的单位依赖群）。
    历史条件超边：使作用于 x 的总分项改变的极小子集 U，连同 x。返回 (超边列表, 分支)。"""
    E = list(E)
    hyper = set()
    for s in probes:
        y0 = host.act(w, s)
        changed = {}
        for (U, f, w2) in settings(host, w, E, fracs, joint=True):
            for j in _act_dep(host, w, w2, s, eps):
                changed.setdefault(('act', j), set()).add(U)
            for (p, x, c) in host.comps(w, y0, s):
                if x not in E:
                    continue
                tot = total_comp(host, w2, s, p, x) - comp_of(host, w, y0, s, p, x)
                if abs(tot) > eps:
                    changed.setdefault(('w', p, x), set()).add(U)
        for key, Us in changed.items():
            mins = [U for U in Us if not any(set(V) < set(U) for V in Us)]
            if key[0] == 'act':
                singles = sorted({U[0] for U in mins if len(U) == 1})
                if len(singles) >= 2:
                    hyper.add(('共同参与', key[1], tuple(singles)))
                for U in mins:
                    if len(U) >= 2:
                        hyper.add(('共同参与', key[1], tuple(sorted(U))))
            else:
                x = key[2]
                for U in mins:
                    nodes = tuple(sorted(set(U) | {x}))
                    if len(nodes) >= 2:
                        hyper.add(('历史条件', key[1] + '@' + x, nodes))
    uf = UF(E)
    for (_k, _n, nodes) in hyper:
        for a in nodes[1:]:
            uf.u(nodes[0], a)
    comps = {}
    for i in E:
        comps.setdefault(uf.f(i), []).append(i)
    return sorted(hyper), sorted([sorted(v) for v in comps.values()])

def org_graph_sample(host, w, E, probes, eps, tested, fracs=FRACS):
    """有界采样：只在 tested（子集列表）上设定。返回 (见证超边, 覆盖缺口：未被任何已测子集共同覆盖的位对；被覆盖也不等于已识别)。"""
    hyper = set()
    for s in probes:
        y0 = host.act(w, s)
        for U in tested:
            for f in fracs:
                w2 = setT(host, w, U, f)
                if w2 == w:
                    continue
                for j in _act_dep(host, w, w2, s, eps):
                    hyper.add(('共同参与', j, tuple(sorted(U))))
                for (p, x, c) in host.comps(w, y0, s):
                    if x in E and abs(total_comp(host, w2, s, p, x) - comp_of(host, w, y0, s, p, x)) > eps:
                        hyper.add(('历史条件', p + '@' + x, tuple(sorted(set(U) | {x}))))
    covered = set()
    for U in tested:
        for a in U:
            for b in U:
                covered.add((a, b))
    unknown = [(a, b) for a, b in combinations(sorted(E), 2) if (a, b) not in covered]
    return sorted(hyper), unknown

def basins(F, chain):
    """有限全序链 chain 上的映射 F（dict）。返回 {不动点: [其吸引域（极限等于该点的原像）]}。"""
    out = {}
    for d in chain:
        x, seen = d, set()
        while F[x] != x:
            if x in seen:
                x = None
                break
            seen.add(x)
            x = F[x]
        out.setdefault(x, []).append(d)
    return out

def order_rel(a1, a2):
    return (a1 > a2) - (a1 < a2)

def order_conflicts(pairs):
    """返回 q 与 e 的序关系不一致的点对（含等号错配两个方向）。"""
    bad = []
    for i in range(len(pairs)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(pairs)):
            (q1, e1), (q2, e2) = pairs[i], pairs[j]
            if order_rel(q1, q2) != order_rel(e1, e2):
                bad.append((pairs[i], pairs[j]))
    return bad

def fstr(x):
    if isinstance(x, Fr):
        return str(x.numerator) if x.denominator == 1 else '%d/%d' % (x.numerator, x.denominator)
    return str(x)

def avail_at(host, states, k, T, eps, k_form=None):
    """拍 k 的可用承担位：拍 k 的拍前态 states[k] 中取向深度 > eps 的 T 中位。
    states[k] 是此前各拍写入的结果，故其越阈意味着在拍 k 之前已留下；若越阈始自 states[0]（起点即越阈），
    其形成须另有记录（〔封三〕ENT 的对应物），本函数不代证；程序在此只验数值上的「已留下」。
    T 的窗末成员身份不用于此判断。"""
    return [x for x in T if depth(host, states[k], x) > eps]

def hc_status(host, w, s, proc, x, U_av, eps, fracs=FRACS):
    """历史条件在一拍一过程上的三种判值（只改可用位 U_av 的合法设定族，含联合设定）：
    '有效差异已获证'：某设定使总分项差 > eps；'函数支持已立'：某设定使总分项差 ≠ 0 而无一 > eps；
    '有效差异未获证'：所测设定族内总分项差恒为 0（这只是有限设定族内的读数，不是完整否定）。"""
    best = None
    nonzero = False
    for (U, f, w2) in settings(host, w, U_av, fracs, joint=True):
        A, D, tot = branches(host, w, w2, s, proc, x)
        if abs(tot) > eps:
            return '有效差异已获证', (U, f, A, D, tot)
        if tot != 0:
            nonzero = True
            best = (U, f, A, D, tot)
    if nonzero:
        return '函数支持已立', best
    return '有效差异未获证', None

def hdyn_cert(host, states, sigs, T, eps, fracs=FRACS):
    """同拍、同一过程上的 Act 与历史差异：Act = 该拍该过程在 x ∈ T 上的总分项 |c| > eps；
    设定只改该拍的可用承担位（avail_at）。返回 (判值, 见证)；判值 ∈ {'1', '函数支持已立', '未获证'}。
    本函数只验模型的结构依赖与数值差（模型模式）；实际物质途径与作用认证另由申报承担。"""
    status = '未获证'
    wit = None
    for k, s in enumerate(sigs):
        w = states[k]
        Uav = avail_at(host, states, k, T, eps)
        if not Uav:
            continue
        y = host.act(w, s)
        seen = set()
        for (p, x, _c) in host.comps(w, y, s):
            if x not in T or (p, x) in seen:
                continue
            seen.add((p, x))
            c = comp_of(host, w, y, s, p, x)
            if abs(c) <= eps:
                continue
            st, wt = hc_status(host, w, s, p, x, Uav, eps, fracs)
            if st == '有效差异已获证':
                return '1', (k, s, p, x, c, Uav) + wt
            if st == '函数支持已立' and status == '未获证':
                status, wit = '函数支持已立', (k, s, p, x, c, Uav) + wt
    return status, wit

def branches_rev(host, w, w2, s, proc, x):
    """另一申报次序的望远镜分解：先改直读（活动保持原值），再换活动。返回 (D″, A″, 总差)。"""
    y, y2 = host.act(w, s), host.act(w2, s)
    c0 = comp_of(host, w, y, s, proc, x)
    cD = comp_of(host, w2, y, s, proc, x)
    c2 = comp_of(host, w2, y2, s, proc, x)
    return cD - c0, c2 - cD, c2 - c0
~~~

A.2　jhc\_models.py　上游冻结程序：《具化场识别判定式》v1.0 附录 A.2（Fly 等申报模型）

~~~python
"""《具化场识别判定式》v1.0 附录程序：申报宿载模型（全部为本篇构造的最小模型，参数为构造值）。
代表实例（模型层）：
  Ecoli     大肠杆菌趋化的单调代理模型（受体簇共同活性 A；Tar、Tsr 两类甲基化位；CheR、CheB 分项含位点可得因子）。
  Fly       果蝇蘑菇体 KC→MBON 最小模型（γ1pedc 短迹、α3 长迹、PPL1-α3 经 γ1 迹应去抑制；三变体）。
  Physarum  多头绒泡菌管网的 Wheatstone 型五管网（Kirchhoff 守恒、Tero 型 Hill 生长与衰减）。
  Rill      滩面凹痕（脚印或冲沟）的单元模型，冲刷、淤积与混合三区间。
对照（边界检验）：
  SunStone      晒热的石头（吸收与当前温度无关；冷却直读本位）。
  ContactStone  接触加热的石块（热流由温差承担，属活动中介）。
  Scratch       恒定介质中的划痕（扫描读出，无作用于迹应的过程）。
  Register      里程表计数寄存器（单位增量与当前读数无关；满量程回零）。
  Gerry         两个互不耦合的子系统（拼接边界的构造）。
反例模型：Cancel（X1）、NetShallow（X2）、Joint（X3）、Reverse（X4）。
跨记录链的记录模型：FlyRec2、FlyNoY。
数位轮的周期写入另见 jhc_bridge.digit_wheel。
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
from jhc_core import Host
F = Fr

def solve(A, b):
    """有理高斯消元。"""
    n = len(A)
    M = [list(A[i]) + [b[i]] for i in range(n)]
    for c in range(n):
        piv = next(r for r in range(c, n) if M[r][c] != 0)
        M[c], M[piv] = M[piv], M[c]
        pv = M[c][c]
        M[c] = [v / pv for v in M[c]]
        for r in range(n):
            if r != c and M[r][c] != 0:
                fct = M[r][c]
                M[r] = [M[r][k] - fct * M[c][k] for k in range(n + 1)]
    return [M[i][n] for i in range(n)]

class Ecoli(Host):
    """活性代理 A = M²/(M² + K²·Rt·Rs)，M = mTar + mTsr，R(L) = ((1+L)/(1+L/50))²。
    分项：CheR_i = h·kR·(1 − A)·(4 − m_i)/4（加深），CheB_i = −h·kB·A·m_i/4（退浅）。
    无迹基态 m = 2、2（A = 1/2，静置下不动）。kR = 0 为 cheR 缺失变体。"""
    def __init__(self, kR=F(1), kB=F(1), h=F(1, 4), K=F(4), grid=4096):
        self.name = 'Ecoli' if kR != 0 else 'Ecoli_cheR-'
        self.X = ['mTar', 'mTsr']
        self.lo = {x: F(0) for x in self.X}
        self.hi = {x: F(4) for x in self.X}
        self.orient = {x: 1 for x in self.X}
        self.kR, self.kB, self.h, self.K, self.grid = kR, kB, h, K, grid
        if kR != 0:
            g = {'mTar': F(2), 'mTsr': F(2)}
        else:
            g = {'mTar': F(0), 'mTsr': F(0)}
        self.ground = dict(g)
        self.init = dict(g)
        self.signals = ['rest', 'Ltar', 'Ltsr', 'Ltar3']
    @staticmethod
    def R(L):
        return ((1 + L) / (1 + L / 50)) ** 2
    def lig(self, s):
        return {'rest': (F(0), F(0)), 'Ltar': (F(1), F(0)), 'Ltsr': (F(0), F(1)),
                'Ltar3': (F(3), F(0))}[s]
    def act(self, w, s):
        Lt, Ls = self.lig(s)
        M = w['mTar'] + w['mTsr']
        A = M * M / (M * M + self.K ** 2 * self.R(Lt) * self.R(Ls))
        return {'A': A}
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        A = y['A']
        out = []
        for x in self.X:
            out.append(('CheR', x, self.h * self.kR * (1 - A) * (4 - w[x]) / 4))
            out.append(('CheB', x, -self.h * self.kB * A * w[x] / 4))
        return out

class Fly(Host):
    """KC 0..3；K(A) = {0,1}，K(B) = {2,3}（overlap=True 时 K(B) = {1,2}）。
    γ1 位 g_i、α3 位 a_i 的深度 = 压抑比例 ∈ [0,1]。
    活动：MBON_g = Σ_{K(s)} (1 − g_i)；DAN_g = 电击 + fb·(|K(s)| − MBON_g)（fb 只在变体 gfb）；
          PPL1_a3 = 电击 + (−a + b·Σ_{K(s)} g_i)（有气味时）；MBON_a3 = Σ_{K(s)} (1 − a_i)。
    分项：Wg_i = η_g·DAN_g（KC 激活时），Rg_i = −ρ_g·g_i（每拍），Wa_i = η_a·PPL1_a3（KC 激活时，带符号）。
    变体：full（γ1 ∪ α3）、gonly（只 γ1，前馈）、gfb（只 γ1，加 MBON-γ1pedc → PPL1-γ1pedc 去抑制）。"""
    def __init__(self, variant='full', overlap=False, grid=4096):
        self.name = 'Fly_' + variant + ('_ov' if overlap else '')
        self.variant = variant
        self.KA = [0, 1]
        self.KB = [1, 2] if overlap else [2, 3]
        self.X = ['g0', 'g1', 'g2', 'g3']
        if variant == 'full':
            self.X += ['a0', 'a1', 'a2', 'a3']
        self.lo = {x: F(0) for x in self.X}
        self.hi = {x: F(1) for x in self.X}
        self.orient = {x: 1 for x in self.X}
        self.ground = {x: F(0) for x in self.X}
        self.init = dict(self.ground)
        self.grid = grid
        self.eta_g, self.rho_g, self.eta_a = F(1, 2), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)
        self.a, self.b, self.fb = F(1, 2), F(1), F(1, 2)
        self.signals = ['rest', 'A+', 'B+', 'A', 'B']
    def K(self, s):
        if s.startswith('A'):
            return self.KA
        if s.startswith('B'):
            return self.KB
        return []
    def act(self, w, s):
        K = self.K(s)
        shock = F(1) if s.endswith('+') else F(0)
        odor = len(K) > 0
        mg = sum((1 - w['g%d' % i] for i in K), F(0))
        dang = shock
        if self.variant == 'gfb' and odor:
            dang = shock + self.fb * (len(K) - mg)
        y = {'MBON_g': mg, 'DAN_g': dang}
        if self.variant == 'full':
            p = shock + ((-self.a + self.b * sum((w['g%d' % i] for i in K), F(0))) if odor else F(0))
            y['PPL1_a3'] = p
            y['MBON_a3'] = sum((1 - w['a%d' % i] for i in K), F(0))
        return y
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        K = self.K(s)
        out = []
        for i in range(4):
            g = 'g%d' % i
            if i in K:
                out.append(('Wg', g, self.eta_g * y['DAN_g']))
            out.append(('Rg', g, -self.rho_g * w[g]))
            if self.variant == 'full' and i in K:
                out.append(('Wa', 'a%d' % i, self.eta_a * y['PPL1_a3']))
        return out

class Physarum(Host):
    """结点 0（泵送入口，注入 1）、1、2、3（汇，压力 0）；管 01、02、13、23、12，长度 1、2、2、1、1。
    流量 Q_e = (D_e/ℓ_e)(p_u − p_v)。信号 N1：结点 1 另注入 1/2（营养刺激），汇点吸收全部。
    分项：grow_e = h·κ·Q²/(q0² + Q²)（活动中介），decay_e = −h·γ·D_e（直读本位）。
    无迹基态：自 D ≡ 1 起静置 400 拍所得的取格状态（运行时计算并核对静置不动）。"""
    E = [('01', 0, 1, F(1)), ('02', 0, 2, F(2)), ('13', 1, 3, F(2)), ('23', 2, 3, F(1)), ('12', 1, 2, F(1))]
    def __init__(self, grid=4096):
        self.name = 'Physarum'
        self.X = [e[0] for e in self.E]
        self.lo = {x: F(0) for x in self.X}
        self.hi = {x: F(4) for x in self.X}
        self.orient = {x: 1 for x in self.X}
        self.grid = grid
        self.h, self.kap, self.q0, self.gam = F(1, 8), F(2), F(1, 2), F(1)
        self.signals = ['rest', 'N1']
        w = {x: F(1) for x in self.X}
        for _ in range(400):
            w = self.step(w, 'rest')
        self.ground = dict(w)
        self.init = dict(w)
    def act(self, w, s):
        inj = [F(1), F(1, 2) if s == 'N1' else F(0), F(0)]
        L = [[F(0)] * 3 for _ in range(3)]
        for (e, u, v, ln) in self.E:
            c = w[e] / ln
            for (a, b_) in ((u, v), (v, u)):
                if a < 3:
                    L[a][a] += c
                    if b_ < 3:
                        L[a][b_] -= c
        try:
            p = solve(L, inj) + [F(0)]
        except StopIteration:
            p = [F(0)] * 4
        y = {}
        for (e, u, v, ln) in self.E:
            y['Q' + e] = w[e] / ln * (p[u] - p[v])
        for k in range(3):
            y['p%d' % k] = p[k]
        return y
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        out = []
        for e in self.X:
            q = y['Q' + e]
            out.append(('grow', e, self.h * self.kap * q * q / (self.q0 ** 2 + q * q)))
            out.append(('decay', e, -self.h * self.gam * w[e]))
        return out

class Rill(Host):
    """凹痕相对周围滩面的深度 d。tide 拍：汇流 q = q0·(1 + κ·d)（活动）；
    分项（相对周围）：erode = h·α·(max(0, q − qc) − max(0, q0 − qc))（活动中介加深），
                      deposit = −h·β·c·(q − q0)（活动中介退浅）；step（踩踏）：foot = sd（与 d 无关）。
    区间：dep（qc = 100，c = 1/4）；ero（qc = 3/2，c = 0）；mix（qc = 3/2，c = 1/4）。"""
    REG = {'dep': (F(100), F(1, 4)), 'ero': (F(3, 2), F(0)), 'mix': (F(3, 2), F(1, 4))}
    def __init__(self, regime='mix', grid=4096):
        self.name = 'Rill_' + regime
        self.regime = regime
        self.qc, self.c = self.REG[regime]
        self.X = ['d']
        self.lo, self.hi = {'d': F(0)}, {'d': F(4)}
        self.orient = {'d': 1}
        self.ground = {'d': F(0)}
        self.init = {'d': F(0)}
        self.grid = grid
        self.h, self.q0, self.kap, self.al, self.be, self.sd = F(1, 4), F(1), F(2), F(1), F(1), F(1, 4)
        self.signals = ['rest', 'step', 'tide']
    def act(self, w, s):
        if s == 'tide':
            return {'q': self.q0 * (1 + self.kap * w['d'])}
        return {'q': F(0)}
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        if s == 'step':
            return [('foot', 'd', self.sd)]
        if s == 'tide':
            q = y['q']
            er = self.h * self.al * (max(F(0), q - self.qc) - max(F(0), self.q0 - self.qc))
            de = -self.h * self.be * self.c * (q - self.q0)
            return [('erode', 'd', er), ('deposit', 'd', de)]
        return []

class SunStone(Host):
    """晒热的石头：温升 T。sun 拍吸收 h·S（与 T 无关）；冷却 −h·k·T（直读本位）；红外读出 IR = k·T。"""
    def __init__(self):
        self.name = 'SunStone'
        self.X = ['T']
        self.lo, self.hi = {'T': F(0)}, {'T': F(10)}
        self.orient = {'T': 1}
        self.ground, self.init = {'T': F(0)}, {'T': F(0)}
        self.grid = 4096
        self.h, self.S, self.k = F(1, 4), F(1), F(1, 2)
        self.signals = ['rest', 'sun']
    def act(self, w, s):
        return {'IR': self.k * w['T']}
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        out = [('cool', 'T', -self.h * self.k * w['T'])]
        if s == 'sun':
            out.append(('absorb', 'T', self.h * self.S))
        return out

class ContactStone(Host):
    """接触加热的石块：plate 拍热流 J = κ·(Tp − T)（活动），分项 conduct = h·J；冷却 −h·k·T。"""
    def __init__(self):
        self.name = 'ContactStone'
        self.X = ['T']
        self.lo, self.hi = {'T': F(0)}, {'T': F(10)}
        self.orient = {'T': 1}
        self.ground, self.init = {'T': F(0)}, {'T': F(0)}
        self.grid = 4096
        self.h, self.kap, self.Tp, self.k = F(1, 4), F(1), F(2), F(1, 2)
        self.signals = ['rest', 'plate']
    def act(self, w, s):
        J = self.kap * (self.Tp - w['T']) if s == 'plate' else F(0)
        return {'J': J, 'IR': self.k * w['T']}
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        return [('conduct', 'T', self.h * y['J']), ('cool', 'T', -self.h * self.k * w['T'])]

class Scratch(Host):
    """恒定介质中的划痕：scratch 刻深 1；扫描读出 scan = g；无其他过程。"""
    def __init__(self):
        self.name = 'Scratch'
        self.X = ['g']
        self.lo, self.hi = {'g': F(0)}, {'g': F(4)}
        self.orient = {'g': 1}
        self.ground, self.init = {'g': F(0)}, {'g': F(0)}
        self.grid = None
        self.signals = ['rest', 'scratch']
    def act(self, w, s):
        return {'scan': w['g']}
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        return [('cut', 'g', F(1))] if s == 'scratch' else []

class Register(Host):
    """里程表计数寄存器 N ∈ {0..999}：step 增 1（与 N 无关）；N = 999 时另有满量程回零分项 −1000（直读本位）。"""
    def __init__(self):
        self.name = 'Register'
        self.X = ['N']
        self.lo, self.hi = {'N': F(0)}, {'N': F(999)}
        self.orient = {'N': 1}
        self.ground, self.init = {'N': F(0)}, {'N': F(0)}
        self.grid = None
        self.signals = ['rest', 'step']
    def act(self, w, s):
        return {'display': w['N']}
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        if s != 'step':
            return []
        out = [('inc', 'N', F(1))]
        if w['N'] == 999:
            out.append(('wrap', 'N', F(-1000)))
        return out

class Gerry(Host):
    """拼接边界：s 为信号刻下的划痕（扫描读出，无作用过程）；b 为界内续行所留的迹应
    （无迹基态 0，起点 1/2 由入口拍的场内脉冲给出，不是信号），活动 y_b = b，分项 self = h·y_b/2。"""
    def __init__(self):
        self.name = 'Gerry'
        self.X = ['s', 'b']
        self.lo, self.hi = {'s': F(0), 'b': F(0)}, {'s': F(4), 'b': F(4)}
        self.orient = {'s': 1, 'b': 1}
        self.ground = {'s': F(0), 'b': F(0)}
        self.init = {'s': F(0), 'b': F(1, 2)}
        self.grid = 4096
        self.h = F(1, 4)
        self.signals = ['rest', 'scratch']
    def act(self, w, s):
        return {'scan': w['s'], 'yb': w['b']}
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        out = [('self', 'b', self.h * y['yb'] / 2)]
        if s == 'scratch':
            out.append(('cut', 's', F(1)))
        return out

# ---------------- 反例 X1—X4 的模型 ----------------
class Cancel(Host):
    """X1：c = 1 + y − x，y = x。两支相抵，总分项恒为 1。"""
    name = 'X1_Cancel'
    X = ['x']
    lo, hi = {'x': F(0)}, {'x': F(4)}
    ground, orient, init = {'x': F(0)}, {'x': 1}, {'x': F(0)}
    signals = ['rest', 'seed', 'use']
    def act(self, w, s):
        return {'y': w['x']}
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        if s == 'seed':
            return [('seed', 'x', F(1))]
        if s == 'use':
            return [('p', 'x', 1 + y['y'] - w['x'])]
        return []

class NetShallow(Cancel):
    """X2：写入 y/4（活动中介），退浅 −x/2（直读）；作用拍深度乘 3/4。"""
    name = 'X2_NetShallow'
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        if s == 'use':
            return [('grow', 'x', y['y'] / 4), ('decay', 'x', -w['x'] / 2)]
        return super().comps(w, y, s)

class Joint(Host):
    """X3：对 x 的分项 (a−1)(b−1)，a = b = x = 1；单设 a 或 b 无响应，联合设零得 1。"""
    name = 'X3_Joint'
    X = ['a', 'b', 'x']
    lo = {k: F(0) for k in X}
    hi = {k: F(4) for k in X}
    ground = {k: F(0) for k in X}
    orient = {k: 1 for k in X}
    init = {k: F(1) for k in X}
    signals = ['rest', 'use']
    def act(self, w, s):
        return {}
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        return [('p', 'x', (w['a'] - 1) * (w['b'] - 1))]

class Reverse(Cancel):
    """X4：基态 1，写后 1/2，正取向深度 −1/2。"""
    name = 'X4_Reverse'
    init = {'x': F(1)}
    ground = {'x': F(1)}
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        return [('p', 'x', -F(1, 2))] if s == 'use' else []

# ---------------- 跨记录链的记录模型 ----------------
class FlyRec2(Fly):
    """记录二（光遗传分级驱动 PPL1-α3）：PPL1_a3 由外加驱动 u 直接给定，γ1 迹应不参与；
    offset 为通道偏差（〔封十三〕丙b 型：光驱与生理放电之差，常值，|offset| ≤ ζ）。
    co_drive=True 为 C2 反例：驱动同时改写另一调制量 z（真实写入只依 z，不依 PPL1_a3）。"""
    def __init__(self, u, offset=F(0), co_drive=False):
        Fly.__init__(self, 'full')
        self.u, self.offset, self.co_drive = u, offset, co_drive
        self.name = 'FlyRec2'
    def act(self, w, s):
        y = Fly.act(self, w, s)
        if self.K(s):
            y['PPL1_a3'] = self.u + self.offset
            if self.co_drive:
                y['z'] = self.u
        return y
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        out = []
        for (p, x, c) in Fly.comps(self, w, y, s):
            if p == 'Wa' and self.co_drive:
                c = self.eta_a * y['z']
            out.append((p, x, c))
        return out

class FlyNoY(Fly):
    """C2 反例的目标实例：α3 写入只依调制量 z（生理上恒为 z₀），不依 PPL1-α3。"""
    def __init__(self, z0):
        Fly.__init__(self, 'full')
        self.z0 = z0
        self.name = 'FlyNoY'
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        out = []
        for (p, x, c) in Fly.comps(self, w, y, s):
            if p == 'Wa':
                c = self.eta_a * self.z0
            out.append((p, x, c))
        return out
~~~

A.3　jhc\_bridge.py　上游冻结程序：《具化场识别判定式》v1.0 附录 A.3（跨记录链证书）

~~~python
"""《具化场识别判定式》v1.0 附录程序：识别与桥接的专门核验。
  window_geo        三分识别的稳健窗（定理 17-1）：几何自衰减模式的留存分类端点。
  digit_wheel       周期写入的载体（数位轮）：沿形成写入的轨道回到无迹基态（定理 17-3）。
  probe_orders      两探针诱导的深度序冲突（引理 17-3′）。
  cert_continuous   连续类短拍敲除—设定响应及其一阶误差界；cert_short 加读数预算（定理 17-7′）。
  exp_bracket       e^h − 1 的有理上下界（X5）。
  chain_eval        跨记录链接合的反例求值；chain_certificate 为单调律类的链证书（定理 17-8′）。
  cycle_map         周期映射；cyclemap_welldef 为周期映射良定检验（定理 17-6′）。
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
F = Fr

# ---------- 定理 17-1：几何自衰减的稳健窗 ----------
def first_below(A, lam, eps):
    """最小 t ≥ 0 使 A·lam^t ≤ eps。"""
    t, v = 0, A
    while v > eps:
        t += 1
        v = v * lam
    return t

def last_above(a, lam, eps, cap=100000):
    """最大 t ≥ 0 使 a·lam^t > eps（a > eps 时）；a ≤ eps 返回 -1。"""
    if a <= eps:
        return -1
    t, v = 0, a
    while v * lam > eps and t < cap:
        t += 1
        v = v * lam
    return t

def window_geo(lams, a, A, eps):
    """各模式自衰减率 lams，写入幅度 ∈ [a, A]。对每一窗长 t 求「必留存」与「必不留存」的模式集；
    返回 (稳健窗 [t_lo, t_hi]（分类与快慢二分一致的 t 之集的端点）, 逐 t 分类表的摘要)。
    必留存：a·lam^t > eps；必不留存：A·lam^t ≤ eps；其余为「依幅度」。"""
    return {k: (first_below(A, l, eps), last_above(a, l, eps)) for k, l in lams.items()}

# ---------- 定理 17-3：数位轮 ----------
def digit_wheel(n=10):
    """数位轮：载体态 0..n−1，形成写入（走一格）w(k) = (k + 1) mod n。返回自基态 0 出发的轨道。"""
    orb, k = [0], 0
    while True:
        k = (k + 1) % n
        if k == 0:
            break
        orb.append(k)
    return orb

def k3_possible_on_orbit(orb, n):
    """在轨道上是否存在满足 K1（基态深度 0、其余 > 0）与 K3（形成写入不减深度）的深度函数。
    轨道末点写入回到基态：K3 要求 δ(0) ≥ δ(末点) > 0，与 K1 矛盾。返回 (可能?, 矛盾点)。"""
    last = orb[-1]
    if (last + 1) % n == orb[0]:
        return False, (last, orb[0])
    return True, None

# ---------- 引理 17-3′：两探针的序冲突 ----------
def probe_orders(states, e1, e2):
    """返回 e1 与 e2 诱导的序相冲突的一对状态（无冲突返回 None）。"""
    for u in states:
        for v in states:
            if e1(u) < e1(v) and e2(u) > e2(v):
                return (u, v)
    return None

# ---------- 定理 17-7′：连续类短拍证书（多项式系统，敲除差的精确积分） ----------
def pint(coefs, y0, c, h):
    """∫_0^h P(y0 + c s) ds，P 为多项式（系数自低到高）。精确。"""
    # 展开 P(y0 + c s) 为 s 的多项式
    from math import comb
    deg = len(coefs) - 1
    sc = [F(0)] * (deg + 1)
    for k, a in enumerate(coefs):
        for j in range(k + 1):
            sc[j] += a * comb(k, j) * (y0 ** (k - j)) * (c ** j)
    return sum((sc[j] * h ** (j + 1) / (j + 1) for j in range(deg + 1)), F(0))

def pval(coefs, y):
    return sum((a * y ** k for k, a in enumerate(coefs)), F(0))

def pder(coefs):
    return [k * coefs[k] for k in range(1, len(coefs))]

def sup_abs_quad(coefs, lo, hi):
    """二次及以下多项式在 [lo, hi] 上 |P| 的上确界（端点与顶点）。"""
    cands = [lo, hi]
    if len(coefs) == 3 and coefs[2] != 0:
        v = -coefs[1] / (2 * coefs[2])
        if lo <= v <= hi:
            cands.append(v)
    return max(abs(pval(coefs, t)) for t in cands)

def cert_continuous(fm, g, c, y0, delta, h):
    """系统 y' = c，x' = f_m(y) + g(y)；f_m、g 为二次及以下多项式。过程 m 读迹应 y、作用于 x。
    敲除差 Δ_m^h(y0) = x_full(h) − x_{−m}(h) = ∫_0^h f_m(y0 + c s) ds（精确）。
    设定响应 R = Δ_m^h(y0 + δ) − Δ_m^h(y0)。
    误差界 B = h²·(L·M + L'·M')：L、M 为全系统场在凸包盒上的 Lipschitz 常数（最大范数）与场的上确界，
    L'、M' 为敲除系统的。返回 dict。"""
    lo = min(y0, y0 + delta, y0 + c * h, y0 + delta + c * h)
    hi = max(y0, y0 + delta, y0 + c * h, y0 + delta + c * h)
    full = [a + b for a, b in zip(fm + [F(0)] * (3 - len(fm)), g + [F(0)] * (3 - len(g)))]
    gg = g + [F(0)] * (3 - len(g))
    L = sup_abs_quad(pder(full) + [F(0)], lo, hi)
    M = max(abs(c), sup_abs_quad(full, lo, hi))
    L2 = sup_abs_quad(pder(gg) + [F(0)], lo, hi)
    M2 = max(abs(c), sup_abs_quad(gg, lo, hi))
    B = h * h * (L * M + L2 * M2)
    D0 = pint(fm, y0, c, h)
    D1 = pint(fm, y0 + delta, c, h)
    R = D1 - D0
    lin = h * (pval(fm, y0 + delta) - pval(fm, y0))
    return {'R': R, 'lin': lin, 'B': B, 'err': abs(R - lin), 'fires': abs(R) > B,
            'fdiff': pval(fm, y0 + delta) - pval(fm, y0)}

# ---------- 定理 17-8′：跨记录链接合的反例求值 ----------
def chain_eval(h_link, g_link, bypass, T1, T2):
    """目标实例上写入分项 c(T) = g(h(T)) + bypass(T)。返回 (c(T1), c(T2))。"""
    return g_link(h_link(T1)) + bypass(T1), g_link(h_link(T2)) + bypass(T2)

# ---------- 定理 17-6′：周期映射 ----------
def cycle_map(host, d, cycle):
    w = {'d': d}
    for s in cycle:
        w = host.step(w, s)
    return w['d']

def cert_short(fm, g, c, y0, delta, h, r_phi=F(0)):
    """定理 17-7′：误差分报。
    模型误差（流的一阶展开）：E_mod = h²(LM + L′M′)；读数误差：四个原始流读数各 ≤ r_phi，
    两个敲除差各 ≤ 2 r_phi，最终响应 ≤ 4 r_phi。判 1 规则：|R_obs| > E_mod + 4 r_phi（先签有限差异 f(s′) ≠ f(s)）。
    完备性阈：E_mod 的系数 K = LM + L′M′；K = 0 时对一切 h 完备（不作除法）。"""
    r = cert_continuous(fm, g, c, y0, delta, h)
    K = r['B'] / (h * h)
    r['K'] = K
    r['bud_read'] = 4 * r_phi
    r['fires_read'] = abs(r['R']) > r['B'] + 4 * r_phi
    if r['fdiff'] == 0:
        r['h_complete'] = None
    elif K == 0:
        r['h_complete'] = 'all'
    else:
        r['h_complete'] = abs(r['fdiff']) / (2 * K)
    return r

def exp_bracket(h, N=12):
    """e^h − 1 的有理上下界（0 < h ≤ 1/2）：部分和与余项 ≤ 2·h^{N+1}/(N+1)!。"""
    from math import factorial
    s = sum((h ** k / factorial(k) for k in range(1, N + 1)), F(0))
    return s, s + 2 * h ** (N + 1) / factorial(N + 1)

def cyclemap_welldef(host, states_by_depth, cycle, key):
    """周期映射良定检验：同一深度 key(w) 的若干可达状态经一周期后的深度是否相同。"""
    outs = {}
    for w in states_by_depth:
        w2 = dict(w)
        for s in cycle:
            w2 = host.step(w2, s)
        outs.setdefault(key(w), set()).add(key(w2))
    return {k: v for k, v in outs.items() if len(v) > 1}

# ---------- 定理 17-8′：链式非零、越阈差异、实际作用分别证明 ----------
def chain_certificate(y1m, y2m, r1, grid_meas, r2, zeta, eps, full_component=False, bypass=None):
    """单调律类（写入关于中介单调不减）证书，三项分签。
    非零：存在格点 a < b，y2m + r1 ≤ a、b ≤ y1m − r1，使 (ĝ(b) − r2 − ζ) − (ĝ(a) + r2 + ζ) > 0；
    越阈：同一下界 > eps（有效差异已获证）；
    实际作用：目标实际总分项 c(T1) 的认证区间。G(y1) ∈ [ĝ(b) − r2 − ζ, ĝ(b′) + r2 + ζ]，b′ 为 ≥ y1m + r1 的最小格点；
      full_component=True 时申报「G 即完整分项」（须另证：该拍该位无其它分项、无夹限生效）；
      bypass = (下界, 上界) 时总分项区间为 G 区间加旁路区间；二者皆无时实际作用判 ⊥_Act。
      区间下端 > eps 或上端 < −eps 时 Act 已获证（负号即有效退浅，同样是作用）。
    记录一两态区间重叠、或两态区间之间格点不足时提前返回，差异与实际作用都记 ⊥：对充分程序是保守的；
    本函数不穷尽一切可独立认证实际作用的输入，独立取得的 Act 证书另行申报。"""
    lo1, hi1, hi2 = y1m - r1, y1m + r1, y2m + r1
    out = {'nonzero': False, 'over_eps': False, 'margin': None, 'act': '⊥', 'act_ok': False, 'interval': None}
    if hi2 >= lo1:
        out['v'], out['why'] = '⊥', '⊥_Chain(C3′)：记录一两态区间重叠'
        return out
    pts = sorted(u for u in grid_meas if hi2 <= u <= lo1)
    if len(pts) < 2:
        out['v'], out['why'] = '⊥', '⊥_Chain(C3′)：两态区间之间无足够格点'
        return out
    a, b = pts[0], pts[-1]
    margin = (grid_meas[b] - r2 - zeta) - (grid_meas[a] + r2 + zeta)
    out.update({'a': a, 'b': b, 'margin': margin})
    if margin > 0:
        out['nonzero'] = True
    if margin > eps:
        out['over_eps'] = True
        out['v'], out['why'] = '1', '有效差异已获证'
    elif margin > 0:
        out['v'], out['why'] = '⊥', '非零已证，越阈未证'
    else:
        out['v'], out['why'] = '⊥', '⊥_Chain(C3′)：消差裕度不足'
    g_lo = grid_meas[b] - r2 - zeta
    ups = sorted(u for u in grid_meas if u >= hi1)
    g_hi = (grid_meas[ups[0]] + r2 + zeta) if ups else None
    if full_component:
        lo_t, hi_t = g_lo, g_hi
    elif bypass is not None:
        lo_t = g_lo + bypass[0]
        hi_t = (g_hi + bypass[1]) if g_hi is not None else None
    else:
        out['act'] = '⊥_Act：旁路基值未申报，且未证 G 即完整分项'
        return out
    out['interval'] = (lo_t, hi_t)
    if lo_t > eps:
        out['act'], out['act_ok'] = '已获证（正向）', True
    elif hi_t is not None and hi_t < -eps:
        out['act'], out['act_ok'] = '已获证（负向，有效退浅）', True
    else:
        out['act'] = '⊥_Act：认证区间与 [−ε, ε] 相交'
    return out
~~~

A.4　jxs\_core.py　上游冻结程序：《迹应集合变换数理式》v1.0 附录 A.1（宿载、势、Act/Aff、运行与回读）

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
《迹应集合变换数理式》v1.0 附录程序：运行系统核心库。
对应正文第三至五节：完整状态 σ = (d, r)；读出律 Φ；写入分项；残留律 P；两套分辨率语义；
势、受影响集、作用集；精确动力同一；线性定常闭环的永隐判据；F 层编译与两种历史条件读法。
纪律：只用标准库；一切数值为 fractions.Fraction；无随机数。
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
from itertools import product

class Host:
    """申报宿载模型 𝔐（D18-1）。
    positions：带身份标签的结构位列表（X̂，可含存在变量所控的潜在位）。
    read(d, r, s) -> dict：全活动 y（D18-7）。
    comps(d, y, s) -> dict pos -> list[(过程标识, 分项 c)]：写入律的合法逐过程分解（D18-1）。
    post(d_new, d_old) -> dict：申报的后处理（如夹限、存在位生成）；无则恒等。
    resid(r, y) -> r'：残留律；无残留时 r 恒为 None。
    """
    def __init__(self, positions, read, comps, post=None, resid=None):
        self.positions = list(positions)
        self.read = read
        self.comps = comps
        self.post = post or (lambda dn, do: dn)
        self.resid = resid or (lambda r, y: r)

def step(host, d, r, s, eps=F(0), sem="raw"):
    """一拍运行（运行方程）。sem = 'raw'：原机制语义，全部分项入账；'booked'：有效入账语义，只入账 |c| > eps。
    返回 (y, d', r', 记录)；记录含各位分项、Act⁰、Act^ε、Aff⁰、Aff^ε。"""
    y = host.read(d, r, s)
    cs = host.comps(d, y, s)
    dn = dict(d)
    for x, lst in cs.items():
        if sem == "raw":
            dn[x] = d[x] + sum(c for _, c in lst)
        else:
            dn[x] = d[x] + sum(c for _, c in lst if abs(c) > eps)
    dn = host.post(dn, d)
    rn = host.resid(r, y)
    act0 = {x for x, lst in cs.items() if any(c != 0 for _, c in lst)}
    acte = {x for x, lst in cs.items() if any(abs(c) > eps for _, c in lst)}
    aff0 = {x for x in host.positions if dn.get(x, F(0)) != d.get(x, F(0))}
    affe = {x for x in host.positions if abs(dn.get(x, F(0)) - d.get(x, F(0))) > eps}
    return y, dn, rn, {"comps": cs, "Act0": act0, "Acte": acte, "Aff0": aff0, "Affe": affe}

def run(host, d, r, hist, eps=F(0), sem="raw"):
    ys = []
    for s in hist:
        y, d, r, _ = step(host, d, r, s, eps, sem)
        ys.append(y)
    return ys, d, r

def potential(host, d, r, signals):
    """势 Φ_σ（D18-6）：带标签信号类上的全活动响应。"""
    return {s: host.read(d, r, s) for s in signals}

def exact_dyn_same(host, s1, s2, alphabet, L, eps=F(0), sem="raw"):
    """精确动力同一（D18-14）在长度 ≤ L 的全部史上的有限核验；返回 (是否, 史数)。
    eps 与 sem 贯穿运行与比较：缺省为原机制语义（sem = "raw"）；有效入账语义须显式传入 sem = "booked" 与 ε。"""
    n = 0
    for k in range(L + 1):
        for h in product(alphabet, repeat=k):
            n += 1
            y1, _, _ = run(host, s1[0], s1[1], h, eps, sem)
            y2, _, _ = run(host, s2[0], s2[1], h, eps, sem)
            if y1 != y2:
                return False, n
    return True, n

def booked_sum(lst, eps):
    """纯数值筛后写入 W^ε_num 的分项和：只计 |c| > ε 的分项（step 的 sem = "booked" 即此算子）。"""
    return sum(c for _, c in lst if abs(c) > eps)

def booked_sum_F(lst, eps, cert):
    """F 层认证筛后写入 W^ε_F 的分项和：只计 |c| > ε 且 cert_eff = 1 的分项（〔封二〕4.2）。cert：{过程: 1/0/'⊥'}，缺省 1。"""
    return sum(c for rho, c in lst if abs(c) > eps and cert.get(rho, 1) == 1)

def pairing_ok(cs_a, cs_b, pi):
    """过程身份配对的充分条件（定理 18-2（一）有效入账款）：对每一位 x，状态 a 在 x 上的 (过程, 分项) 多重集
    与状态 b 在 π(x) 上的相同（逐分项保数值；阈值 ε 两侧同一）。这保证纯数值筛后写入 W^ε_num 交换；
    F 层认证筛后写入 W^ε_F 另须配对两侧的认证状态相同（cert_pairing_ok）。"""
    keys = set(cs_a) | {k for k in pi}
    for x in keys:
        la = sorted(cs_a.get(x, []), key=lambda t: (str(t[0]), t[1]))
        lb = sorted(cs_b.get(pi.get(x, x), []), key=lambda t: (str(t[0]), t[1]))
        if la != lb:
            return False
    return True

def cert_pairing_ok(cs_a, cert_a, cert_b, pi):
    """配对两侧的作用认证状态相同：位 x 上过程 ρ 的 cert_eff 等于位 π(x) 上同名过程的 cert_eff。cert：{(过程, 位): 值}。"""
    for x, lst in cs_a.items():
        for rho, _ in lst:
            if cert_a.get((rho, x), 1) != cert_b.get((rho, pi.get(x, x)), 1):
                return False
    return True

def mat_mul(A, B):
    return [[sum(A[i][k] * B[k][j] for k in range(len(B))) for j in range(len(B[0]))] for i in range(len(A))]

def hidden_forever_lti(A, C, delta):
    """线性定常闭环 Δ' = AΔ、读数 CΔ：Cayley–Hamilton，检 k = 0..n−1 即为全称判据（定理 18-7（四））。"""
    v = [[x] for x in delta]
    for _ in range(len(A)):
        if any(row[0] != 0 for row in mat_mul(C, v)):
            return False
        v = mat_mul(A, v)
    return True

# ---- F 层编译与两种历史条件读法（定理 18-4） ----
def f_component(law, s):
    """〔封二〕D12 的三类数值律：('const', b) / ('lin', b, k, R) / ('bil', b, k, j1, j2)。"""
    if law[0] == "const":
        return law[1]
    if law[0] == "lin":
        _, b, k, R = law
        return b + k * sum(s[j] for j in R)
    _, b, k, j1, j2 = law
    return b + k * s[j1] * s[j2]

def f_supp_act(law, s):
    """〔封二〕D15 的函数支持（数学非零，与 ε 无关）。"""
    if law[0] == "const":
        return set()
    if law[0] == "lin":
        return set(law[3]) if law[2] != 0 else set()
    _, b, k, j1, j2 = law
    if k == 0:
        return set()
    out = set()
    if s[j2] != 0:
        out.add(j1)
    if s[j1] != 0:
        out.add(j2)
    return out

def histcond_F(law, s, T, eps, formed):
    """〔封二〕D17、D18 的 HistCond_F（路径认证取 1、cert_read 取 1）：可用位 = Supp_act ∩ T 中 s > ε 且已形成者。"""
    avail = {i for i in f_supp_act(law, s) & set(T) if s[i] > eps and i in formed}
    return 1 if avail else 0

def histcond_diff(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts):
    """单设对照的数值差读法：存在只改 T 中一个已形成、深度 > ε 的位的合法对照，总分项差 > ε（返回 1/0）。
    这是〔十七〕定义 6′（丙）款的数值部分在单设子协议上的检验，不是完整证书。contrasts：合法取值表 {位: [值…]}。"""
    base = f_component(law, s)
    pos = [i for i in T if i in formed and s[i] > eps]
    for i in pos:
        for v in contrasts.get(i, []):
            s2 = dict(s); s2[i] = v
            if abs(f_component(law, s2) - base) > eps:
                return 1
    return 0

# ---- 带路径与读取认证的 HistCond_F；联合对照；两列原始判断；F 层入账（含 cert_eff） ----
def histcond_F_full(law, s, T, eps, formed, path_from, cert_read=1):
    """〔封二〕D18：HistCond_F = [Avail_act ≠ ∅] ∧ cert_read ∧ Path({(i,t) : i ∈ Avail_act}, m)。
    path_from(起点集) -> 1/0/'⊥'：申报证据路径图上自该起点集到本机制事件的路径状态（D14）。"""
    avail = {i for i in f_supp_act(law, s) & set(T) if s[i] > eps and i in formed}
    if not avail:
        return 0
    if cert_read == 0:
        return 0
    p = path_from(frozenset(avail))
    if p == 0:
        return 0
    if cert_read == "⊥" or p == "⊥":
        return "⊥"
    return 1

def histcond_diff_joint(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts):
    """笛卡尔闭包枚举（显式申报联合合法对照族为逐位候选值的笛卡尔闭包时使用）：对 T 中已形成、深度 > ε 的位的任意非空子集同时取合法值。
    返回 '数值差越阈' 或 '数值差未越阈'（只是〔十七〕定义 6′（丙）款的数值部分；后者亦不是否定）。"""
    from itertools import combinations, product as prod
    base = f_component(law, s)
    pos = [i for i in T if i in formed and s[i] > eps]
    for k in range(1, len(pos) + 1):
        for sub in combinations(pos, k):
            for vals in prod(*[contrasts.get(i, []) for i in sub]):
                s2 = dict(s)
                for i, v in zip(sub, vals):
                    s2[i] = v
                if abs(f_component(law, s2) - base) > eps:
                    return "数值差越阈"
    return "数值差未越阈"

def readback_hist(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts, path_from, cert_read=1):
    """两列原始判断：(F 层函数支持之值, 联合对照下的数值差判断)。"""
    hf = histcond_F_full(law, s, T, eps, formed, path_from, cert_read)
    hd = histcond_diff_joint(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts)
    return ("函数支持已立" if hf == 1 else ("函数支持未定" if hf == "⊥" else "函数支持未立")), hd

def f_layer_update(comps, eps):
    """〔封二〕4.2 的 F 层入账：只计 Act_F = [|c| > ε] ∧ cert_eff = 1 的分项。comps：[(c, cert_eff)]。"""
    return sum(c for c, ce in comps if abs(c) > eps and ce == 1)

def raw_update(comps):
    return sum(c for c, _ in comps)

# ---- D18 三值合取；回读原子与三栏摘要；实际方程下的 F 层对照 ----
def kleene_and(vals):
    """三值合取：任一项为 0 得 0；无 0 而有 ⊥ 得 ⊥；全为 1 得 1。"""
    if any(v == 0 for v in vals):
        return 0
    if any(v == "⊥" for v in vals):
        return "⊥"
    return 1

def max_contrast_diff(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts):
    """笛卡尔闭包上的最大差：只在申报的联合合法对照族恰为逐位候选值的笛卡尔闭包时使用（显式申报，不作缺省）。"""
    from itertools import combinations, product as prod
    base = f_component(law, s)
    pos = [i for i in T if i in formed and s[i] > eps]
    best = F(0)
    for k in range(1, len(pos) + 1):
        for sub in combinations(pos, k):
            for vals in prod(*[contrasts.get(i, []) for i in sub]):
                s2 = dict(s)
                for i, v in zip(sub, vals):
                    s2[i] = v
                best = max(best, abs(f_component(law, s2) - base))
    return best

def kleene_or(vals):
    """三值析取：任一项为 1 得 1；无 1 而有 ⊥ 得 ⊥；全为 0（或空）得 0。"""
    if any(v == 1 for v in vals):
        return 1
    if any(v == "⊥" for v in vals):
        return "⊥"
    return 0

def contrast_family(contrasts):
    """规范化对照族（定理 18-4 引言）。
    contrasts 为 {位: [值…]}：单设简写，每位每值各成一条单设对照，不自动生成联合对照；申报覆盖记「完整」。
    contrasts 为 {"对照": [...], "覆盖": "完整"/"不完整"}：冻结的对照集合，每条为 {"id": 名, "set": {位: 值}, "证据": {...}}；
      联合对照须作为一条显式列出（或由联合合法性谓词生成后列出）。
    返回 (对照列表, 覆盖)。"""
    if "对照" in contrasts:
        fam = [dict(id=k.get("id", "对照%d" % n), set=dict(k["set"]), 证据=dict(k.get("证据", {})))
               for n, k in enumerate(contrasts["对照"])]
        ids = [k["id"] for k in fam]
        if len(set(ids)) != len(ids):
            raise ValueError("对照身份重复")
        return fam, contrasts.get("覆盖", "完整")
    fam = []
    for i in sorted(contrasts, key=str):
        for v in contrasts[i]:
            fam.append(dict(id="%s→%s" % (i, v), set={i: v}, 证据={}))
    return fam, "完整"

def numeric_diff_family(law, s, T, eps, formed, fam, cover):
    """数值差项：只计合于〔十七〕定义 6′（乙）款的对照（只改 T 中已形成、深度 > ε 的位）。
    取值「数值差越阈」「数值差非零未越阈」「数值差为零」；无一越阈而申报覆盖不完整时记「对照域覆盖不足」。"""
    base = f_component(law, s)
    m = F(0)
    for k in fam:
        act_set = {i: v for i, v in k["set"].items() if v != s[i]}
        if all(i in T and i in formed and s[i] > eps for i in act_set):
            s2 = dict(s); s2.update(act_set)
            m = max(m, abs(f_component(law, s2) - base))
    if m > eps:
        return "数值差越阈"
    if cover != "完整":
        return "对照域覆盖不足"
    return "数值差非零未越阈" if m > 0 else "数值差为零"

def numeric_diff(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts):
    """数值差项：〔十七〕定义 6′（丙）款的数值部分，不是完整证书（对照族按 contrast_family 规范化）。"""
    fam, cover = contrast_family(contrasts)
    return numeric_diff_family(law, s, T, eps, formed, fam, cover)

def readback_atoms(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts, path_from, cert_read=1, cert_eff=1, evidence=None,
                   independent=None):
    """定理 18-4 的回读原子与三栏摘要。
    对照族：按 contrast_family 规范化；以同一拍、同一过程、同一目标与对照身份逐条计算差与认证，再对完整见证取析取；
    认证绑定到取得越阈差的那一条对照，不跨对照拼接。
    原子：数值支持 Avail、读取认证 cert_read、路径 Path、HistCond_F（〔封二〕D18 原合取）、Act_F（含 cert_eff）、
    数值差（numeric_diff_family），逐条对照的明细，以及〔十七〕定义 6′ 四款的逐项证据（同拍、承担位、途径、作用）。
    evidence：同一 F 事件的共同证据 {键: 1/0/'⊥'}，申报为对各条对照共同适用；对照自带的「证据」逐款优先。各款缺省：
    同拍由对照的构造给出，记 1；承担位按（乙）款逐条判定（只改 T 中已形成、深度 > ε 的位记 1，否则 0）；途径绑定该条
    对照实际改动的承担位——自这些起点到本事件的路径为 1 时记 1，否则 '⊥'（路径为 0 不排除另外的物质途径；另申报的
    合法实际途径须独立带证书补足）；聚合 Path 只作独立原子，不替代逐条途径；作用取 Act_F。
    对照明细逐条记 (对照身份, |Δ|, 该条见证, 该条实际四款)；「定义6′四款」取取证对照的四款（无取证对照时取首条）。
    「证书状态」：有取证对照时记其身份；否则记已列对照证书析取之值，申报覆盖不完整时另记「申报域尚未覆盖」。
    作用一款的合并：F 原子 Act_F 是同一事件的作用证据。
      Act_F = 0（明确否证）：外部声称不得覆盖；外部声称 1 时记「证据冲突」并按原否证阻断，该款取 0。
      Act_F = 1：外部声称 0 时同记「证据冲突」，该款取 0（保守阻断）；外部声称 1 或未给时取 1。
      Act_F = ⊥（未证）：外部显式认证可补足（取其值，来源记为「显式认证」）；外部显式给 ⊥ 时来源记「显式未证」；
      未给时来源记「未证」，该款为 ⊥。
    independent：不同机制或独立 R 层见证，须为 {事件身份, 模式, 认证依据, 作用}；单列于原子「独立见证」，
      不并入本 F 事件的四款、完整证书与摘要；字段不全时记「独立见证不完整」。
    完整证书 = 对各条对照的 [|Δ| > ε ∧ 四款] 取三值析取。摘要按〔十七〕定义 6′ 的判值三分：完整证书为 1 记
    「有效差异已获证」；否则数值差非零未越阈（申报覆盖完整），或 HistCond_F = 1，记「函数支持已立」；其余记
    「有效差异未获证」。摘要不覆盖原子；冲突、未证与否证分别见原子「证据冲突」「作用来源」。"""
    evidence = dict(evidence or {})
    fam, cover = contrast_family(contrasts)
    avail = {i for i in f_supp_act(law, s) & set(T) if s[i] > eps and i in formed}
    path = path_from(frozenset(avail)) if avail else 0
    hf = histcond_F_full(law, s, T, eps, formed, path_from, cert_read)
    c = f_component(law, s)
    act = kleene_and([1 if abs(c) > eps else 0, cert_eff])
    nd = numeric_diff_family(law, s, T, eps, formed, fam, cover)
    conflicts = []
    ext = evidence.get("作用")
    if act == 0:
        act_item, src = 0, "F 原子否证"
        if ext == 1:
            conflicts.append("作用：F 原子 Act_F = 0（明确否证）与外部声称 1 冲突，按原否证阻断")
    elif act == 1:
        if ext == 0:
            act_item, src = 0, "外部否证"
            conflicts.append("作用：F 原子 Act_F = 1 与外部声称 0 冲突，保守阻断")
        else:
            act_item, src = 1, "F 原子"
    else:
        if ext is None:
            act_item, src = "⊥", "未证"
        elif ext == "⊥":
            act_item, src = "⊥", "显式未证"
        else:
            act_item, src = ext, "显式认证"
    detail, certs, witness, wfour, first_four = [], [], None, None, None
    for k in fam:
        ev = k["证据"]
        # 实际差集：只保留取值确与拍前值不同的位（设回拍前值的设置不算改动）
        act_set = {i: v for i, v in k["set"].items() if v != s[i]}
        legal = all(i in T and i in formed and s[i] > eps for i in act_set)
        s2 = dict(s); s2.update(act_set)
        dk = abs(f_component(law, s2) - c)
        # 途径绑定该条对照实际改动的承担位：自这些起点到本事件的路径；聚合 Path 不替代
        bearers = frozenset(i for i in act_set if i in avail)
        p_k = path_from(bearers) if bearers else 0
        route_k = ev.get("途径", evidence.get("途径", 1 if p_k == 1 else "⊥"))
        four_k = {"同拍": ev.get("同拍", evidence.get("同拍", 1)),
                  "承担位": ev.get("承担位", evidence.get("承担位", 1)) if legal else 0,
                  "途径": route_k, "作用": act_item}
        ck = kleene_and([1 if dk > eps else 0] + list(four_k.values()))
        detail.append((k["id"], dk, ck, dict(four_k)))
        if first_four is None:
            first_four = dict(four_k)
        certs.append(ck)
        if ck == 1 and witness is None:
            witness, wfour = k["id"], dict(four_k)
    cert = kleene_or(certs)
    four = wfour if witness is not None else (first_four if first_four is not None else
           {"同拍": evidence.get("同拍", 1), "承担位": 0, "途径": "⊥", "作用": act_item})
    status = ("取证对照「%s」" % witness if witness is not None else
              "已列对照证书析取为 %s" % cert + ("；申报域尚未覆盖" if cover != "完整" else ""))
    atoms = {"Avail": 1 if avail else 0, "cert_read": cert_read, "Path": path, "HistCond_F": hf,
             "Act_F": act, "数值差": nd, "定义6′四款": four, "作用来源": src, "证据冲突": conflicts,
             "对照明细": detail, "取证对照": witness, "完整证书": cert, "覆盖": cover, "证书状态": status}
    if independent is not None:
        need = ("事件身份", "模式", "认证依据", "作用")
        atoms["独立见证"] = dict(independent) if all(k in independent for k in need) else "独立见证不完整"
    if cert == 1:
        col = "有效差异已获证"
    elif hf == 1 or nd == "数值差非零未越阈":
        col = "函数支持已立"
    else:
        col = "有效差异未获证"
    return atoms, col

def f_post_match(post, d, raw_sum, f_sum):
    """定理 18-4（四）（甲）的实际方程比较：原机制一拍 post(d + Σ原) 与 F 层一拍 d + ΣF。"""
    return post(d + raw_sum) == d + f_sum

# ---- J₁₈：经〔封二〕F 层求值器的校验、轨迹复现与资格（定理 18-4（四）） ----
def _tri(v):
    return {1: True, 0: False, "⊥": None, True: True, False: False, None: None}[v]

def _untri(v):
    return {True: 1, False: 0, None: "⊥"}[v]

def j18_spec(sites, initial, window, eps, T, events, links=(), identity=1, trace_history=1, closed_graph=None):
    """把本篇一次运行的申报事件编译为〔封二〕附录 A.1 的 Spec（原始事件、认证、时间与来源显式传入，不压缩）。
    events：每条为
      {"类": "脉冲", "名": 事件标识, "拍": t, "目标": 位, "量": a, "界外": True/False, "发生": 1/0/'⊥'}，或
      {"类": "过程", "名": 事件标识, "拍": t, "目标": 位, "载体在内": True/False, "律": 'constant'/'linear'/'bilinear',
       "读取": (位…), "系数": k, "偏置": b, "cert_eff": 1/0/'⊥', "cert_read": 1/0/'⊥'}。
    links：[(源结点, 目标结点, 认证)]，结点为 'state:位:拍' 或事件标识。
    closed_graph：证据图是否闭合，须由证据协议申报（True/False）；未申报（None）时不编译。"""
    if closed_graph not in (True, False):
        raise ValueError("证据图是否闭合未申报")
    import field_final as FF
    pulses = tuple(FF.Pulse(e["名"], e["拍"], e["目标"], e["量"], e.get("界外", True), _tri(e.get("发生", 1)))
                   for e in events if e["类"] == "脉冲")
    procs = tuple(FF.Process(e["名"], e["拍"], e["目标"], e["载体在内"], e["律"], tuple(e.get("读取", ())),
                             e.get("系数", F(0)), e.get("偏置", F(0)), _tri(e.get("cert_eff", 1)), _tri(e.get("cert_read", 1)))
                  for e in events if e["类"] == "过程")
    lk = tuple(FF.Link(a, b, _tri(c)) for a, b, c in links)
    return FF.Spec(tuple(sites), tuple(initial), tuple(window), pulses, procs, lk, _tri(identity), _tri(trace_history),
                   closed_graph, eps, tuple(T))

def j18_raw_run(sites, initial, window, events):
    """本篇原机制语义下的同一运行（post 恒等）：每拍各过程按其律读拍前态求分项，发生的脉冲计入，全部分项入账。
    参与计算的一切数值先按〔封二〕的 exact 规范化（十进制小数按其十进制表示取精确有理数），与上游求值器同一解释。"""
    from field_final import exact
    st = {x: exact(v) for x, v in zip(sites, initial)}
    traj = []
    for t in range(window[0] - 1, window[-1] + 1):
        before = dict(st)
        add = {x: F(0) for x in sites}
        for e in events:
            if e["拍"] != t:
                continue
            if e["类"] == "脉冲":
                if e.get("发生", 1) == 1:
                    add[e["目标"]] += exact(e["量"])
            else:
                rd = e.get("读取", ()); k = exact(e.get("系数", F(0))); b = exact(e.get("偏置", F(0)))
                v = b if e["律"] == "constant" else (b + k * sum(before[q] for q in rd) if e["律"] == "linear"
                                                     else b + k * before[rd[0]] * before[rd[1]])
                add[e["目标"]] += v
        st = {x: before[x] + add[x] for x in sites}
        if t in window:
            traj.append(dict(st))
    return traj

def j18_check(sites, initial, window, eps, T, events, links=(), identity=1, trace_history=1, closed_graph=None):
    """定理 18-4（四）的三条件，经〔封二〕求值器（field_final.check、run）判值：
    （甲）可生成 F 候选事件表：j18_spec 编译成功（证据图是否闭合由证据协议申报），且 field_final.check 按 D20 接受原始认证与原始事件；
         D20 拒绝时（如作用认证未定，零分项亦然）不运行 F，另报 R 层待定；
    （乙）复现原运行数值轨迹：原机制同一运行的各拍后态与 F 运行报告的各拍后态逐位相等；
    （丙）保见证与资格：由 field_final.run 求 D19 的 Trace_F、Part_F、HDyn_F、Q_F 与逐事件原子。"""
    import field_final as FF
    try:
        spec = j18_spec(sites, initial, window, eps, T, events, links, identity, trace_history, closed_graph)
    except ValueError as exc:
        return {"D20": "拒绝：%s" % exc, "R": "须由证据协议申报后再求值", "事件表": 0, "轨迹": "未运行",
                "Trace_F": "未运行", "Part_F": "未运行", "HDyn_F": "未运行", "Q_F": "未运行", "事件": []}
    try:
        FF.check(spec)
    except ValueError as exc:
        return {"D20": "拒绝：%s" % exc, "R": "R 层待定（另按 R 层事件见证求值）", "事件表": 0, "轨迹": "未运行",
                "Trace_F": "未运行", "Part_F": "未运行", "HDyn_F": "未运行", "Q_F": "未运行", "事件": []}
    try:
        out = FF.run(spec)
    except ValueError as exc:
        return {"D20": "拒绝（运行中越出定义域）：%s" % exc, "R": "R 层待定（另按 R 层事件见证求值）", "事件表": 1,
                "轨迹": "未运行", "Trace_F": "未运行", "Part_F": "未运行", "HDyn_F": "未运行", "Q_F": "未运行", "事件": []}
    f_traj = [rep["after"] for rep in out["reports"]]
    raw = j18_raw_run(sites, initial, window, events)
    traj = 1 if [{x: F(v) for x, v in a.items()} for a in f_traj] == raw else 0
    evs = [dict(id=e["id"], c=e["delta"], Act_F=_untri(e["act"]), Cond_F=_untri(e["cond"]), HistCond_F=_untri(e["histcond"]))
           for rep in out["reports"] for e in rep["events"]]
    return {"D20": "接受", "R": "—", "事件表": 1, "轨迹": traj, "Trace_F": _untri(out["trace"]), "Part_F": _untri(out["part"]),
            "HDyn_F": _untri(out["hdyn"]), "Q_F": _untri(out["Q"]), "事件": evs, "F 轨迹": f_traj, "原机制轨迹": raw}

def nonneg_encode(d):
    """签名深度进入 F 层的非负编码：d = d⁺ − d⁻，d⁺、d⁻ ≥ 0；返回 (编码, 回读函数)。"""
    enc = {}
    for x, v in d.items():
        enc[(x, "+")] = v if v > 0 else F(0)
        enc[(x, "−")] = -v if v < 0 else F(0)
    return enc, (lambda e: {x: e[(x, "+")] - e[(x, "−")] for x in d})
~~~

A.5　jxs\_models.py　上游冻结程序：《迹应集合变换数理式》v1.0 附录 A.2（FLY、FLYC、LOOP、CHAIN 模型）

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
《迹应集合变换数理式》v1.0 附录程序：申报宿载模型（本篇构造，参数为构造值，不是测量值）。
FLY：果蝇蘑菇体型最小模型（KC×区室突触深度；MBON 驱动与 PPL1-α3 反应为全活动）。
FLYC：FLY 加 α3 巩固态（律层迹应，定理 18-8）。
TERO：Tero 型流网（导率为迹应，流量与压力为活动）。
CHAIN：带存在变量的一维容纳链（边沁的表示，定理 18-9）。
LOOP：γ1 自环玩具（局部环路增益的符号律，定理 18-5）。
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
# ---------------- FLY ----------------
KC = [0, 1, 2, 3]
COMP = ("g1", "a3", "P", "N")
ODOR = {"A": (0, 1), "B": (2, 3), "C": (1, 2)}
A_INN, B_DIS, ETA, R1 = F(3, 4), F(1), F(1, 4), F(9, 10)
FLY_POS = [(i, c) for i in KC for c in COMP]

def fly_zero():
    return {p: F(0) for p in FLY_POS}

def fly_read(d, r, s):
    """全活动：各区室 MBON 驱动与 PPL1-α3 反应；静置 R 无活动读数（返回空）。"""
    if s == "R":
        return {}
    y = {"MBON_" + c: sum(1 - d[(i, c)] for i in ODOR[s]) for c in COMP}
    y["PPL1_a3"] = -A_INN + B_DIS * sum(d[(i, "g1")] for i in ODOR[s])
    return y

def fly_comps(d, y, s):
    """写入分项：呈现 s 时 α3 列（K(s)）受 η·PPL1-α3；每拍 γ1 弛豫 −(1 − r1)·d。"""
    cs = {}
    if s != "R":
        for i in ODOR[s]:
            cs.setdefault((i, "a3"), []).append(("W_a3", ETA * y["PPL1_a3"]))
    for i in KC:
        cs.setdefault((i, "g1"), []).append(("RELAX_g1", -(1 - R1) * d[(i, "g1")]))
    return cs

def fly_post(dn, do):
    """夹限：α3 位限于 [0, 1]。"""
    out = dict(dn)
    for i in KC:
        out[(i, "a3")] = min(F(1), max(F(0), out[(i, "a3")]))
    return out

def make_fly():
    from jxs_core import Host
    return Host(FLY_POS, fly_read, fly_comps, fly_post)

# ---------------- FLYC：巩固态 ----------------
# 每一 α3 位另有巩固态 c ∈ {0, 1}（律层迹应）：α3 压抑的每拍保留比 ρ(c)：c = 0 → 1/2，c = 1 → 9/10。
# 巩固写入（历史版）：呈现 s 且 PPL1-α3 > θ_c 时，K(s) 的 α3 位 c ← 1。钟控版：自首次写入起满 k_c 拍，c ← 1。
RHO = {0: F(1, 2), 1: F(9, 10)}
THETA_C = F(1, 8)

def flyc_positions():
    return FLY_POS + [("c", i) for i in KC]

def flyc_zero():
    z = fly_zero()
    for i in KC:
        z[("c", i)] = F(0)
    return z

def flyc_read(d, r, s):
    return fly_read({p: d[p] for p in FLY_POS}, r, s)

def flyc_comps_history(d, y, s):
    cs = fly_comps({p: d[p] for p in FLY_POS}, y, s)
    for i in KC:
        rho = RHO[int(d[("c", i)])]
        cs.setdefault((i, "a3"), []).append(("RELAX_a3", -(1 - rho) * d[(i, "a3")]))
    if s != "R" and y["PPL1_a3"] > THETA_C:
        for i in ODOR[s]:
            if d[("c", i)] == 0:
                cs.setdefault(("c", i), []).append(("CONSOL", F(1)))
    return cs

def make_flyc(clock=None, block_dan=False):
    """clock=None：历史版；clock=k：钟控版（r 记首次 α3 写入后的拍数）。block_dan：巩固期阻断 DAN（PPL1-α3 写入与巩固写入都不发生）。"""
    from jxs_core import Host
    def comps(d, y, s):
        if block_dan and s != "R":
            y = dict(y); y["PPL1_a3"] = F(0)
        cs = flyc_comps_history(d, y, s) if clock is None else None
        if clock is not None:
            cs = fly_comps({p: d[p] for p in FLY_POS}, y, s)
            for i in KC:
                rho = RHO[int(d[("c", i)])]
                cs.setdefault((i, "a3"), []).append(("RELAX_a3", -(1 - rho) * d[(i, "a3")]))
        if block_dan and s != "R":
            for i in ODOR[s]:
                cs[(i, "a3")] = [p for p in cs.get((i, "a3"), []) if p[0] != "W_a3"]
        return cs
    def resid(r, y):
        if clock is None:
            return r
        return r  # 钟控版的计时由 post 处理
    def post(dn, do):
        out = fly_post(dn, do)
        return out
    return Host(flyc_positions(), flyc_read, comps, post, resid)

def flyc_clock_run(d, hist, k_c, block_dan=False):
    """钟控巩固版的显式运行：c_i 在其 α3 位首次越 0 后满 k_c 拍置 1，与 DAN 是否在巩固期活动无关。"""
    from jxs_core import step
    host = make_flyc(clock=k_c, block_dan=block_dan)
    since = {i: None for i in KC}
    ys = []
    for s in hist:
        y, dn, _, _ = step(host, d, None, s)
        for i in KC:
            if since[i] is None and dn[(i, "a3")] > 0:
                since[i] = 0
            elif since[i] is not None:
                since[i] += 1
                if since[i] >= k_c:
                    dn[("c", i)] = F(1)
        d = dn
        ys.append(y)
    return ys, d

# ---------------- TERO ----------------
def solve(nodes, edges, D, src, snk, I):
    adj = {n: set() for n in nodes}
    for (u, v), dd in zip(edges, D):
        if dd > 0:
            adj[u].add(v); adj[v].add(u)
    comp, stack = {snk}, [snk]
    while stack:
        x = stack.pop()
        for yv in adj[x]:
            if yv not in comp:
                comp.add(yv); stack.append(yv)
    if src not in comp:
        raise ValueError("源与汇不连通")
    unk = [n for n in nodes if n in comp and n != snk]
    idx = {n: k for k, n in enumerate(unk)}
    m = len(unk)
    A = [[F(0)] * m for _ in range(m)]
    b = [F(0)] * m
    for (u, v), dd in zip(edges, D):
        if dd <= 0 or u not in comp:
            continue
        for x, yv in ((u, v), (v, u)):
            if x in idx:
                A[idx[x]][idx[x]] += dd
                if yv in idx:
                    A[idx[x]][idx[yv]] -= dd
    b[idx[src]] = F(I)
    for col in range(m):
        piv = next(r for r in range(col, m) if A[r][col] != 0)
        A[col], A[piv] = A[piv], A[col]; b[col], b[piv] = b[piv], b[col]
        for r in range(m):
            if r != col and A[r][col] != 0:
                fct = A[r][col] / A[col][col]
                for k in range(col, m):
                    A[r][k] -= fct * A[col][k]
                b[r] -= fct * b[col]
    p = {n: None for n in nodes}
    p[snk] = F(0)
    for n in unk:
        p[n] = b[idx[n]] / A[idx[n]][idx[n]]
    return p

def flows(nodes, edges, D, src, snk, I):
    p = solve(nodes, edges, D, src, snk, I)
    Q = []
    for (u, v), dd in zip(edges, D):
        if dd <= 0 or p[u] is None or p[v] is None:
            Q.append(F(0))
        else:
            Q.append(dd * (p[u] - p[v]))
    return p, Q

def f_tero2(q):
    return q * q / (1 + q * q)

def make_tero(nodes, edges, src, snk, I, h, gam, eps_rm, grid=None, f=f_tero2):
    """位 = 管段序号；d 为导率（D⁰ = 0 的申报）；活动 = 各管流量与结点压力；写入 h(f(|Q|) − γD)；
    post：网格化与移除阈（导率低于 eps_rm 置 0：退浅极限，派生关联改变，定理 18-9）。信号 s 为注入强度倍数（'I' 为常注入）。"""
    from jxs_core import Host
    pos = list(range(len(edges)))
    def read(d, r, s):
        D = [d[k] for k in pos]
        p, Q = flows(nodes, edges, D, src, snk, I)
        return {"Q": tuple(Q), "p": tuple(p[n] for n in nodes)}
    def comps(d, y, s):
        return {k: [("ADAPT", h * (f(abs(y["Q"][k])) - gam * d[k]))] for k in pos if d[k] > 0}
    def post(dn, do):
        out = dict(dn)
        for k in pos:
            v = out[k]
            if grid is not None:
                v = F(round(v * grid), grid)
            out[k] = v if v >= eps_rm else F(0)
        return out
    return Host(pos, read, comps, post)

# ---------------- CHAIN：边沁的表示 ----------------
# 潜在位 X̂ = {0, 1, 2, 3}；存在变量 e_x ∈ {0,1} 与深度 d_x ∈ [0, CAP]；信号 'S' 向现存链的首个未满位注入 1/2；
# 位 x 满（d_x = CAP）且信号继续时，若 x+1 不存在则生成（e_{x+1} ← 1）——满后沿边生成新结构位（《场数理式》方程 10 的最小表示）。
CAP = F(1)

def chain_positions():
    return [("d", x) for x in range(4)] + [("e", x) for x in range(4)]

def chain_zero():
    z = {p: F(0) for p in chain_positions()}
    z[("e", 0)] = F(1)
    return z

def chain_read(d, r, s):
    present = [x for x in range(4) if d[("e", x)] == 1]
    # 活动：信号 S 的通行深度（进入第几位）与各现存位的出流读数（d_x 越深出流越易：读数 = d_x）
    if s == "R":
        return {"out": tuple(d[("d", x)] if x in present else None for x in range(4))}
    tgt = next((x for x in present if d[("d", x)] < CAP), None)
    return {"tgt": tgt, "out": tuple(d[("d", x)] if x in present else None for x in range(4))}

def chain_comps(d, y, s):
    cs = {}
    if s == "S":
        tgt = y["tgt"]
        if tgt is not None:
            room = CAP - d[("d", tgt)]
            cs[("d", tgt)] = [("FILL", min(F(1, 2), room))]
        else:
            last = max(x for x in range(4) if d[("e", x)] == 1)
            if last + 1 < 4:
                cs[("e", last + 1)] = [("BIRTH", F(1))]
    return cs

def make_chain():
    from jxs_core import Host
    return Host(chain_positions(), chain_read, chain_comps)

# ---------------- LOOP：γ1 自环玩具（符号律） ----------------
# 单一 γ1 子集深度 s ∈ [0, 1]；MBON 驱动 m = 2(1 − s)，M 发放 ⇔ m ≥ θ_M；DAN 驱动 = c + u·[M 发放]，D 发放 ⇔ ≥ θ_D；
# 非强化 CS+ 拍：D 发放则写入 +A（压抑加深，夹限 1），否则只退浅 ×(1 − ρ)。
def loop_step(s, u, c=F(1, 2), thM=F(1), thD=F(1, 2), A=F(1, 4), rho=F(1, 10)):
    m = 2 * (1 - s)
    gM = 1 if m >= thM else 0
    gD = 1 if c + u * gM >= thD else 0
    s2 = (1 - rho) * s + (A if gD else 0)
    return min(F(1), s2), gM, gD

# ---------------- 一般边界的导率网络（定理 18-9（二）） ----------------
def components(nodes, edges, D):
    adj = {n: set() for n in nodes}
    for (u, v), dd in zip(edges, D):
        if dd > 0:
            adj[u].add(v); adj[v].add(u)
    seen, out = set(), []
    for n in nodes:
        if n in seen:
            continue
        comp, stack = {n}, [n]
        while stack:
            x = stack.pop()
            for yv in adj[x]:
                if yv not in comp:
                    comp.add(yv); stack.append(yv)
        seen |= comp
        out.append(comp)
    return out

def solve_bc(nodes, edges, D, fixed, inj):
    """导率网络在一般边界下的稳态：fixed = {结点: 定势}，inj = {结点: 注入}（只计自由结点）。
    逐连通分量求解：含定势结点的分量唯一可解；不含者须分量内注入之和为 0（守恒），否则 ValueError；
    不含定势结点的分量取其最小结点势为 0（流量与此规范无关）。"""
    p = {}
    for comp in components(nodes, edges, D):
        fx = [n for n in comp if n in fixed]
        if not fx:
            if sum(F(inj.get(n, 0)) for n in comp) != 0:
                raise ValueError("分量源汇不守恒，稳态无解")
            ref = min(comp, key=str)
            known = {ref: F(0)}
        else:
            known = {n: F(fixed[n]) for n in fx}
        unk = sorted([n for n in comp if n not in known], key=str)
        idx = {n: k for k, n in enumerate(unk)}
        m = len(unk)
        A = [[F(0)] * m for _ in range(m)]
        b = [F(inj.get(n, 0)) for n in unk]
        for (u, v), dd in zip(edges, D):
            if dd <= 0 or u not in comp:
                continue
            for x, yv in ((u, v), (v, u)):
                if x in idx:
                    A[idx[x]][idx[x]] += dd
                    if yv in idx:
                        A[idx[x]][idx[yv]] -= dd
                    else:
                        b[idx[x]] += dd * known[yv]
        for col in range(m):
            piv = next(r for r in range(col, m) if A[r][col] != 0)
            A[col], A[piv] = A[piv], A[col]; b[col], b[piv] = b[piv], b[col]
            for r in range(m):
                if r != col and A[r][col] != 0:
                    fct = A[r][col] / A[col][col]
                    for k in range(col, m):
                        A[r][k] -= fct * A[col][k]
                    b[r] -= fct * b[col]
        p.update(known)
        for n in unk:
            p[n] = b[idx[n]] / A[idx[n]][idx[n]]
    return p

def flows_bc(nodes, edges, D, fixed, inj):
    p = solve_bc(nodes, edges, D, fixed, inj)
    return [dd * (p[u] - p[v]) if dd > 0 else F(0) for (u, v), dd in zip(edges, D)]

def removal_response(nodes, edges, D, fixed, k):
    """同一边界下的增量传递：去掉第 k 条边 e = (u, v) 后，定势结点增量保持为 0（齐次边界），
    自由结点中 u 注入单位流、v 抽出单位流（定势端的注入由边界吸收，不计）时各边的流量。"""
    u, v = edges[k]
    Dc = list(D); Dc[k] = F(0)
    inj = {}
    if u not in fixed:
        inj[u] = F(1)
    if v not in fixed:
        inj[v] = inj.get(v, F(0)) - 1
    return flows_bc(nodes, edges, Dc, {n: F(0) for n in fixed}, inj)
~~~

A.6　field\_final.py　上游冻结程序：《迹应集合变换数理式》v1.0 附录 A.4，与《场具化数理式》v1.0 附录 A.1 逐字节相同（F 层求值器）

~~~python
"""场具化数理式 v1.0 附录 A：F 层（有限可分层）求值器。
形式模拟，不是物质因果认证。
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
from math import isfinite
from collections import defaultdict
from fractions import Fraction
Tri = bool | None
def exact(v):
    """输入数值按其十进制表示取精确有理数；F 层全部运算在有理数上精确进行。"""
    return Fraction(repr(v)) if isinstance(v, float) else Fraction(v)
def tri_and(*xs):
    if False in xs:return False
    if None in xs:return None
    return True
def tri_or(xs):
    xs=list(xs)
    if True in xs:return True
    if None in xs:return None
    return False
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Pulse:
    name:str; step:int; target:str; amount:float; external:bool=True; certified:Tri=True
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Process:
    name:str; step:int; target:str; carrier_inside:bool; law:str
    reads:tuple[str,...]=(); coefficient:float=0.; bias:float=0.
    certified_effect:Tri=True; certified_read:Tri=True
    # rule: constant, linear (sum of read sites), bilinear (two sites)
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Link:
    source:str; target:str; certified:Tri=True
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Spec:
    sites:tuple[str,...]; initial:tuple[float,...]; window:tuple[int,...]
    pulses:tuple[Pulse,...]; processes:tuple[Process,...]
    links:tuple[Link,...]=(); identity:Tri=True; trace_history:Tri=True
    closed_graph:bool=True; epsilon:float=1e-9
    declared_T:tuple[str,...]|None=None

def _num(v, nonneg=False):
    # 合法数值：整数、有理数（Fraction）或有限的十进制浮点数
    if isinstance(v, bool) or not isinstance(v, (int, float, Fraction)): return False
    if isinstance(v, float) and not isfinite(v): return False
    return (not nonneg) or v >= 0
def check(x):
    if not x.sites or len(set(x.sites))!=len(x.sites):raise ValueError('sites')
    if x.declared_T is None or not x.declared_T or len(set(x.declared_T))!=len(x.declared_T) or any(s not in x.sites for s in x.declared_T):raise ValueError('declared T')
    if not x.window or tuple(range(x.window[0],x.window[-1]+1))!=x.window:raise ValueError('window')
    if len(x.initial)!=len(x.sites) or not all(_num(z,True) for z in x.initial):raise ValueError('initial')
    if not _num(x.epsilon,True):raise ValueError('epsilon')
    if x.identity not in (True,False,None) or x.trace_history not in (True,False,None):raise ValueError('identity')
    names=set()
    for e in (*x.pulses,*x.processes):
        if not e.name or e.name in names:raise ValueError('event id')
        names.add(e.name)
        if e.step<x.window[0]-1 or e.step>x.window[-1]:raise ValueError('event time')
        if e.target not in x.sites:raise ValueError('target')
        if isinstance(e,Pulse):
            if not _num(e.amount,True):raise ValueError('pulse amount')
            # certified 在 F 中是脉冲「是否实际发生」的认证：1 发生，0 不发生；⊥ 不属 F 单轨迹定义域，须在 R 中按分支枚举
            if e.certified is None:raise ValueError('F_UNDECIDED_PULSE: enumerate occurrence branches in R')
            if e.certified not in (True,False):raise ValueError('pulse evidence')
        else:
            if e.law not in ('constant','linear','bilinear'):raise ValueError('law')
            if any(s not in x.sites for s in e.reads):raise ValueError('read sites')
            if e.law=='bilinear' and len(e.reads)!=2:raise ValueError('bilinear arity')
            if e.law=='linear' and not e.reads:raise ValueError('linear reads')
            if e.law=='constant' and e.reads:raise ValueError('constant reads')
            if not _num(e.coefficient) or not _num(e.bias):raise ValueError('params')
            # D20：作用认证须已决定；作用未定的机制不属 F 定义域（零分项亦然）
            if e.certified_effect not in (True,False):raise ValueError('undecided mechanism effect')
            if e.certified_read not in (True,False,None):raise ValueError('evidence')
    # All trace nodes are snapshots at integer t; process nodes at integer t+0.5.
    # Strict temporal order prevents fabricated same-step read-after-write.
    times={f'state:{s}:{t}':float(t) for s in x.sites for t in range(x.window[0],x.window[-1]+2)}
    times.update({e.name:float(e.step)+0.5 for e in (*x.pulses,*x.processes)})
    for link in x.links:
        if link.source not in times or link.target not in times:raise ValueError('link endpoint')
        if times[link.source]>=times[link.target]:raise ValueError('noncausal link')
        if link.certified not in (True,False,None):raise ValueError('link evidence')
    return times
def path_status(links,starts,target,closed=True):
    adjacency=defaultdict(list)
    for l in links:adjacency[l.source].append(l)
    def reachable(allow):
        seen=set(starts);queue=list(starts)
        while queue:
            u=queue.pop()
            for l in adjacency[u]:
                if allow(l.certified) and l.target not in seen:
                    seen.add(l.target);queue.append(l.target)
        return target in seen
    if reachable(lambda w:w is True):return True
    if reachable(lambda w:w is not False):return None
    return False if closed else None
def run(x):
    check(x)
    eps=exact(x.epsilon)
    state={s:exact(v) for s,v in zip(x.sites,x.initial)}
    # For each site, certified external history must be identified as event, not inferred from nonzero state.
    formation=defaultdict(list); history_snapshots=defaultdict(list)
    signal_marks=defaultdict(list)  # 界外成因：已认证、有效的界外作用分项（脉冲或界外载体过程）在该位留下的改变
    part=[];hdyn=[];reports=[]
    for t in range(x.window[0]-1,x.window[-1]+1):
        before=dict(state); contributions=defaultdict(list);events=[]
        # Pulse first as an event, but processes read the prior snapshot, not pulse-modified value.
        for p in x.pulses:
            if p.step!=t:continue
            # 只计已认证发生且有效的脉冲；确认不发生者不计贡献、不登记形成与成因
            a=exact(p.amount)
            if p.certified is True and a>eps:
                contributions[p.target].append((p.name,a))
                formation[p.target].append((p.name,t,p.external))
                if p.external:signal_marks[p.target].append((p.name,t))
        for m in x.processes:
            if m.step!=t:continue
            k=exact(m.coefficient); b=exact(m.bias)
            if m.law=='constant':value=b
            elif m.law=='linear':value=b+k*sum(before[s] for s in m.reads)
            else:value=b+k*before[m.reads[0]]*before[m.reads[1]]
            is_action=tri_and(abs(value)>eps,m.certified_effect)
            # Declared reads do not establish a genuine dependence: compute the law's support.
            support=()
            # 组织条件的支持：数值律依赖读取位，或纯读取过程（分项为零）；读取认证另作合取，未定不预先截为假
            # 函数支持按数学上的非零偏导判定（系数与伙伴读数精确不为零），不与有效改变阈值 ε 混用
            if m.law=='linear' and (k!=0 or abs(value)<=eps): support=m.reads
            elif m.law=='bilinear' and k!=0:
                support=tuple(s for i,s in enumerate(m.reads) if before[m.reads[1-i]]!=0)
            # For every element in the declared T, historical formation must precede the read.
            active_T=set(x.declared_T)
            available=[s for s in support if s in active_T and before[s]>eps and any(a[1]<t for a in formation[s])]
            is_cond=tri_and(bool(available),m.certified_read)
            # No automatic path from a read-site to an act. Explicit event links are required.
            old_nodes=[f'state:{s}:{t}' for s in available]
            route=path_status(x.links,old_nodes,m.name,x.closed_graph) if old_nodes else False
            # Action provenance must be supported by a nonzero functional dependence,
            # not merely a certified read that has no effect on the action.
            action_support=()
            if m.law=='linear' and k!=0:action_support=m.reads
            elif m.law=='bilinear' and k!=0:
                action_support=tuple(s for i,s in enumerate(m.reads) if before[m.reads[1-i]]!=0)
            action_available=[s for s in action_support if s in active_T and before[s]>eps and any(a[1]<t for a in formation[s])]
            action_nodes=[f'state:{s}:{t}' for s in action_available]
            action_route=path_status(x.links,action_nodes,m.name,x.closed_graph) if action_nodes else False
            is_hist=tri_and(bool(action_available),m.certified_read,action_route)
            if is_action is True:
                contributions[m.target].append((m.name,value))
                # 新元素的形成：B 内载体者为场内续行形成，界外载体者为信号形成
                if value>eps and before[m.target]<=eps:
                    formation[m.target].append((m.name,t,not m.carrier_inside))
                # 界外载体过程的有效分项，不论新旧元素、不论正负，都是信号留下的改变
                if not m.carrier_inside:
                    signal_marks[m.target].append((m.name,t))
            elif is_action is None: # 防御性检查：作用认证未定已由 check() 排除于定义域之外
                raise ValueError('unresolved effect cannot determine numeric trajectory')
            if t in x.window:
                if m.carrier_inside:part.append(is_cond)
                hdyn.append(tri_and(m.target in active_T,is_action,is_hist))
            events.append({'id':m.name,'delta':value,'act':is_action,'cond':is_cond,'histcond':is_hist,'route':route})
        for s in x.sites:
            new=before[s]+sum(v for _,v in contributions[s])
            # 深度不得为负：精确更新值小于零者越出定义域，不截零、不设容差
            if new < 0:raise ValueError('negative trace depth')
            state[s]=new
            if state[s]>eps and formation[s]:history_snapshots[s].append(t+1)
        if t in x.window:reports.append({'step':t,'before':before,'after':dict(state),'contributions':dict(contributions),'events':events})
    ts=x.declared_T
    eligible=all(state[s]>eps and formation[s] for s in ts)
    sources={origin for site in ts for origin,t in signal_marks[site]}
    trace=tri_and(bool(sources),eligible,x.identity,x.trace_history)
    pw=tri_or(part); hw=tri_or(hdyn)
    return {'trace':trace,'part':pw,'hdyn':hw,'Q':tri_and(trace,pw,hw),'reports':reports,'physical_certificate':False}
~~~

A.7　k17\_core.py　本篇的库：二值类 𝒦\_B 的一拍转移、事件与读取集

~~~python
"""《区室化第三因子回路迹应变换区分式》v1.0 附录库 k17_core.py：二值类 𝒦_B 的一拍转移、R 层事件与读取集、实际事件图路径。
只用标准库；一切数值为 fractions.Fraction。
𝒦_B（正文定义 19-0）：在《物质场具化判定式》v1.0 定义 5、7（𝒦_PA^3、𝒦_PA^≺）之上，
  （一）弛豫系数按结构位申报 ρ_ij（𝒦_PA^≺ 取全局 ρ）；
  （二）增强与抑制的门控单元按列分别申报 gp[j]（PLUS 用）与 gm[j]（MINUS 用），gp = gm 即 𝒦_PA^3 的 gate_of；
  其余（入口、门控单元、同拍有向无环发放输入 U、按拍顺序、夹限）不变。
按拍顺序：（1）读入 s；（2—3）按申报拓扑次序 order 逐单元 vt_j = λ_j v_j + Σ_i w_ij s_i + Σ_{k≺j} U_kj g_k + I_j，g_j = [vt_j ≥ θ_j]；
  （4）复位；（5）x′ = d_x x + s，y′ = d_y y + g；（6）P⁺_ij = A₊ x′_i 当 plastic ∧ bap ∧ g_{gp[j]}，P⁻_ij = −A₋ y′_{gm[j]} 当 plastic ∧ bap ∧ s_i，
  P^r_ij = −ρ_ij (w_ij − b_ij) 当 plastic；（7）夹限 [lo, hi]；（8）读出 g。
事件与读取集（正文定义 19-0 的读取约定）：门控读取集取 ≺ 闭包 gr_j = {v_j} ∪ {w_ij : s_i = 1} ∪ ⋃_{k: U_kj ≠ 0} gr_k。
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
ZERO = Fr(0)
ONE = Fr(1)

def F(v):
    return v if isinstance(v, Fr) else Fr(v)

def clip(a, lo, hi):
    return min(hi, max(lo, a))

class Member:
    """申报成员。units：门控单元名表；order：拓扑次序（单元下标）；U：{(k, j): 系数}；gp、gm：每列的增强、抑制门控单元；
    rho、b：{(i, j): 值}；pat：{字母: 0/1 元组}；Bu、Bi：边界内的门控单元与入口单元下标集。"""
    def __init__(self, n_in, units, order, U, gp, gm, theta, lam, vres, dx, dy, Ap, Am, rho, lo, hi, b, pat,
                 plastic=True, bap=True, Bu=None, Bi=None, name=''):
        self.n_in = n_in
        self.units = list(units)
        self.n_out = len(units)
        self.order = list(order)
        self.U = {k: F(v) for k, v in U.items() if F(v) != 0}
        self.gp = list(gp)
        self.gm = list(gm)
        self.theta = [F(t) for t in theta]
        self.lam = [F(t) for t in lam]
        self.vres = [F(t) for t in vres]
        self.dx = [F(t) for t in dx]
        self.dy = [F(t) for t in dy]
        self.Ap = F(Ap)
        self.Am = F(Am)
        self.rho = {(i, j): F(rho.get((i, j), 0)) for i in range(n_in) for j in range(self.n_out)}
        self.lo = F(lo)
        self.hi = F(hi)
        self.b = {(i, j): F(b.get((i, j), 0)) for i in range(n_in) for j in range(self.n_out)}
        self.pat = {a: tuple(p) for a, p in pat.items()}
        self.plastic = plastic
        self.bap = bap
        self.Bu = set(range(self.n_out)) if Bu is None else set(Bu)
        self.Bi = set(range(n_in)) if Bi is None else set(Bi)
        self.name = name
        self.validate()
    def validate(self):
        pos = {j: n for n, j in enumerate(self.order)}
        assert sorted(self.order) == list(range(self.n_out)), 'order'
        for (k, j) in self.U:
            assert pos[k] < pos[j], '发放输入须沿拓扑次序（有向无环）'
        for j in range(self.n_out):
            assert 0 <= self.lam[j] <= 1 and 0 <= self.dy[j] <= 1
            assert 0 <= self.gp[j] < self.n_out and 0 <= self.gm[j] < self.n_out
        for i in range(self.n_in):
            assert 0 <= self.dx[i] <= 1
        assert self.Ap >= 0 and self.Am >= 0
        for key, r in self.rho.items():
            assert 0 <= r <= 1
            assert self.lo <= self.b[key] <= self.hi
        assert 'none' in self.pat and all(v == 0 for v in self.pat['none'])
    def init_state(self):
        w = {(i, j): self.b[(i, j)] for i in range(self.n_in) for j in range(self.n_out)}
        return (w, [ZERO] * self.n_out, [ZERO] * self.n_in, [ZERO] * self.n_out)

def copy_state(st):
    w, v, x, y = st
    return (dict(w), list(v), list(x), list(y))

def gate_reads(M, s):
    """≺ 闭包的门控读取集（只由字母与申报 U 决定）。"""
    gr = {}
    for j in M.order:
        r = {('v', j)} | {('w', i, j) for i in range(M.n_in) if s[i]}
        for (k, jj), u in M.U.items():
            if jj == j:
                r |= gr[k]
        gr[j] = frozenset(r)
    return gr

def car_of_unit(M, j):
    return 'B' if j in M.Bu else 'EXT'

def car_of_pos(M, i, j):
    return 'B' if (j in M.Bu and i in M.Bi) else 'EXT'

def step(M, st, letter, I):
    """一拍。I：长度 n_out 的注入表。返回 (新态, g, vt, ev)。ev[(种类, 下标)] = dict(occ, c, reads, tgt, car)。"""
    w, v, x, y = st
    s = M.pat[letter]
    I = [F(t) for t in I]
    vt = [None] * M.n_out
    g = [None] * M.n_out
    for j in M.order:
        val = M.lam[j] * v[j] + sum((w[(i, j)] for i in range(M.n_in) if s[i]), ZERO) + I[j]
        for (k, jj), u in M.U.items():
            if jj == j and g[k]:
                val += u
        vt[j] = val
        g[j] = 1 if val >= M.theta[j] else 0
    v2 = [M.vres[j] if g[j] else vt[j] for j in range(M.n_out)]
    x2 = [M.dx[i] * x[i] + s[i] for i in range(M.n_in)]
    y2 = [M.dy[j] * y[j] + g[j] for j in range(M.n_out)]
    gr = gate_reads(M, s)
    ev = {}
    def add(kind, idx, occ, c, reads, tgt, car):
        ev[(kind,) + tuple(idx)] = dict(occ=bool(occ), c=F(c) if occ else ZERO, reads=frozenset(reads), tgt=tgt, car=car)
    for j in range(M.n_out):
        cu = car_of_unit(M, j)
        add('LEAK', (j,), True, (M.lam[j] - 1) * v[j], {('v', j)}, ('v', j), cu)
        for i in range(M.n_in):
            if s[i]:
                add('SYN', (i, j), True, w[(i, j)], {('w', i, j)}, ('v', j), car_of_pos(M, i, j))
        if I[j] != 0:
            add('INJ', (j,), True, I[j], set(), ('v', j), 'EXT')
        if g[j]:
            add('RESET', (j,), True, M.vres[j] - vt[j], gr[j], ('v', j), cu)
            add('YIN', (j,), True, ONE, gr[j], ('y', j), cu)
        add('YDEC', (j,), True, (M.dy[j] - 1) * y[j], {('y', j)}, ('y', j), cu)
        for (k, jj), u in M.U.items():
            if jj == j:
                add('FIN', (k, j), g[k], u, gr[k], ('v', j), cu)
    for i in range(M.n_in):
        ci = 'B' if i in M.Bi else 'EXT'
        add('XDEC', (i,), True, (M.dx[i] - 1) * x[i], {('x', i)}, ('x', i), ci)
        if s[i]:
            add('XIN', (i,), True, ONE, set(), ('x', i), ci)
    w2 = {}
    pl, bp = M.plastic, M.bap
    for i in range(M.n_in):
        for j in range(M.n_out):
            cp = car_of_pos(M, i, j)
            S = w[(i, j)]
            op = bool(pl and bp and g[M.gp[j]])
            pp = M.Ap * x2[i] if op else ZERO
            om = bool(pl and bp and s[i])
            pm = -M.Am * y2[M.gm[j]] if om else ZERO
            rr = M.rho[(i, j)]
            orr = bool(pl and rr != 0)
            pr = -rr * (w[(i, j)] - M.b[(i, j)]) if pl else ZERO
            S = S + pp + pm + pr
            w2[(i, j)] = clip(S, M.lo, M.hi)
            cc = w2[(i, j)] - S
            tgt = ('w', i, j)
            if op:
                add('PLUS', (i, j), True, pp, gr[M.gp[j]] | {('x', i)}, tgt, cp)
            if om:
                add('MINUS', (i, j), True, pm, gr[M.gm[j]] | {('y', M.gm[j])}, tgt, cp)
            if orr:
                add('RELAX', (i, j), True, pr, {('w', i, j)}, tgt, cp)
            if cc != 0:
                add('CLIP', (i, j), True, cc, gr[M.gp[j]] | gr[M.gm[j]] | {('w', i, j), ('x', i), ('y', M.gm[j])}, tgt, cp)
    return (w2, v2, x2, y2), g, vt, ev

def run(M, st0, seq, u=None, setting=None):
    """seq：[(letter, I)]。拍 u 开始前把 setting（{(i, j): 值}）施于结构位。返回逐拍记录。"""
    st = copy_state(st0)
    out = []
    for t, (a, I) in enumerate(seq):
        if u is not None and t == u:
            w, v, x, y = copy_state(st)
            for k, val in (setting or {}).items():
                w[k] = F(val)
            st = (w, v, x, y)
        pre = copy_state(st)
        st, g, vt, ev = step(M, st, a, I)
        out.append(dict(pre=pre, g=g, vt=vt, ev=ev, post=copy_state(st), letter=a, I=list(I)))
    return out

def r1_check(M, rec):
    """引理 R1′ 型闭合：拍前值 + 指向该结点的分项和 = 拍后值。返回不一致数。"""
    bad = 0
    pre, post = rec['pre'], rec['post']
    tot = {}
    for e in rec['ev'].values():
        if e['occ']:
            tot[e['tgt']] = tot.get(e['tgt'], ZERO) + e['c']
    w, v, x, y = pre
    w2, v2, x2, y2 = post
    for (i, j) in w:
        if w[(i, j)] + tot.get(('w', i, j), ZERO) != w2[(i, j)]:
            bad += 1
    for j in range(M.n_out):
        if v[j] + tot.get(('v', j), ZERO) != v2[j]:
            bad += 1
        if y[j] + tot.get(('y', j), ZERO) != y2[j]:
            bad += 1
    for i in range(M.n_in):
        if x[i] + tot.get(('x', i), ZERO) != x2[i]:
            bad += 1
    return bad

def path_act(trace, sources, t_e, reads):
    """实际事件图（只含实际发生的事件；读边、写边、结构位持存边）上自 sources = {(结点, 拍)} 是否可达读取集 reads（拍 t_e）。
    与《候选宿载场具化判定式》附录 decision.path_act 同一规则。"""
    if not sources:
        return 0
    reach = set()
    t0 = min(u for _, u in sources)
    for t in range(t0, t_e + 1):
        reach |= {k for (k, u) in sources if u == t}
        if t == t_e:
            return 1 if (reads & reach) else 0
        nxt = {k for k in reach if k[0] == 'w'}
        for e in trace[t]['ev'].values():
            if e['occ'] and (e['reads'] & reach):
                nxt.add(e['tgt'])
        reach = nxt
    return 0

def slot_reads_tick(M, letter):
    """槽位超图：给定字母，各后态结点所依的拍前结点（一切可能发生的事件槽位的读取集之并；结构位另加持存）。"""
    s = M.pat[letter]
    gr = gate_reads(M, s)
    dep = {}
    def addd(n, r):
        dep.setdefault(n, set()).update(r)
    for j in range(M.n_out):
        addd(('v', j), {('v', j)} | gr[j])
        addd(('y', j), {('y', j)} | gr[j])
    for i in range(M.n_in):
        addd(('x', i), {('x', i)})
        for j in range(M.n_out):
            r = {('w', i, j)}
            if M.plastic and M.bap:
~~~
