~~~python
                r |= gr[M.gp[j]] | {('x', i)}
                if s[i]:
                    r |= gr[M.gm[j]] | {('y', M.gm[j])}
            addd(('w', i, j), r)
    return dep

def path_slot(M, seq, sources, t_e, reads):
    """槽位超图上自 sources 是否可达拍 t_e 的读取集。"""
    if not sources:
        return 0
    reach = set()
    t0 = min(u for _, u in sources)
    for t in range(t0, t_e + 1):
        reach |= {k for (k, u) in sources if u == t}
        if t == t_e:
            return 1 if (reads & reach) else 0
        dep = slot_reads_tick(M, seq[t][0])
        reach = {n for n, r in dep.items() if r & reach}
    return 0
~~~

A.8　k17\_models.py　本篇的库：𝒦\_B 的冻结申报成员 Φ\_γ、Φ\_γα3^0、Φ\_γα3^±、Φ\_α、Φ\_sr

~~~python
"""《区室化第三因子回路迹应变换区分式》v1.0 附录库 k17_models.py：二值类 𝒦_B 的冻结申报成员与申报史。参数为构造值（无量纲、有理数），不是物质测量值。
共同约定：4 个 KC 入口（CS+ 激活 A = {0, 1}，CS− 激活 B = {2, 3}）；字母 'A'、'B'、'none'；
λ = 0、v_reset = 0、d_x = d_y = 0（粗拍：一拍为一个气味呈现槽，长于膜与资格时间常数）；
KC→MBON 位取压抑深度读法 D⁻：基线 b = hi = 1/2，深度 s = b − w ∈ [0, 1/2]；
KC→DAN 位为静态驱动（其列的门控单元为永不发放的 Z，故无增强、抑制分项为 0、ρ = 0）。
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
from k17_core import Member
h = Fr(1, 2)
EPS = Fr(1, 100)
PAT = {'A': (1, 1, 0, 0), 'B': (0, 0, 1, 1), 'none': (0, 0, 0, 0)}
A = (0, 1)
KC = range(4)

def _b(cols):
    """cols：{单元: KC→该单元的基线值}。"""
    return {(i, j): Fr(v) for j, v in cols.items() for i in KC}

def gamma(rho1=Fr(1, 8), cD=Fr(1, 16), u=Fr(-1), thM=Fr(3, 4), thD=h, Am=Fr(1, 8)):
    """Φ_γ：γ1pedc 单区室。单元 M = MBON-γ1pedc>α/β（0）、D = PPL1-γ1pedc（1）、Z（2）；U_MD = u < 0（GABA 能抑制，Pavlowsky 等 2018）。"""
    b = _b({0: h, 1: cD, 2: 0})
    rho = {(i, 0): rho1 for i in KC}
    return Member(4, ['MBON-g1', 'PPL1-g1', 'Z'], [0, 1, 2], {(0, 1): u}, gp=[1, 2, 2], gm=[1, 2, 2],
                  theta=[thM, thD, 1], lam=[0, 0, 0], vres=[0, 0, 0], dx=[0] * 4, dy=[0] * 3,
                  Ap=0, Am=Am, rho=rho, lo=0, hi=h, b=b, pat=PAT, name='Φ_γ')

def selfrenew(rho1=Fr(1, 8), cD=Fr(1, 4), u=Fr(-1), thM=Fr(3, 4), thD=h, Am=Fr(1, 8)):
    """Φ_sr：抑制性自环、去抑制驱动足以使 DAN 发放（c_A ≥ θ_D > c_A + u）。定理 19-5 的模型层见证；不申报为任一果蝇区室。"""
    M = gamma(rho1=rho1, cD=cD, u=u, thM=thM, thD=thD, Am=Am)
    M.name = 'Φ_sr'
    return M

def gamma_alpha3(rho1=Fr(1, 8), rho2=Fr(0), c1=Fr(1, 16), c2=Fr(1, 4), u11=Fr(-1), u12=Fr(-1),
                 signed=False, Ap=Fr(1, 16), cm=Fr(1, 8), um=Fr(1), Am=Fr(1, 8), block=False, Bu=None):
    """Φ_γα3：M1 = MBON-γ1pedc>α/β（0）、D1 = PPL1-γ1pedc（1）、M2 = MBON-α3（2）、D2 = PPL1-α3（3）[、D2m（4）]、Z。
    U_{M1,D1} = u11、U_{M1,D2} = u12（Huang 等 2024 补充表 1 的拟合权重为负）。signed=True 时加 D2m：
    「PPL1-α3 低于基线」的二值读法（M1 发放且有气味输入时发放），门控 MBON-α3 列的增强（A₊ 分项，即 T₂ 的削弱）。
    block=True：M1 的全部发放输出置 0（阻断 MBON-γ1pedc>α/β 传递，律改写）。"""
    if not signed:
        units = ['MBON-g1', 'PPL1-g1', 'MBON-a3', 'PPL1-a3', 'Z']
        Z = 4
        U = {(0, 1): u11, (0, 3): u12}
        gp = [Z, Z, Z, Z, Z]
        gm = [1, Z, 3, Z, Z]
        theta = [Fr(3, 4), h, Fr(3, 4), h, 1]
        b = _b({0: h, 1: c1, 2: h, 3: c2, 4: 0})
    else:
        units = ['MBON-g1', 'PPL1-g1', 'MBON-a3', 'PPL1-a3', 'PPL1-a3-dip', 'Z']
        Z = 5
        U = {(0, 1): u11, (0, 3): u12, (0, 4): um}
        gp = [Z, Z, 4, Z, Z, Z]
        gm = [1, Z, 3, Z, Z, Z]
        theta = [Fr(3, 4), h, Fr(3, 4), h, 1, 1]
        b = _b({0: h, 1: c1, 2: h, 3: c2, 4: cm, 5: 0})
    if block:
        U = {k: Fr(0) for k in U}
    rho = {}
    for i in KC:
        rho[(i, 0)] = rho1
        rho[(i, 2)] = rho2
    n = len(units)
    return Member(4, units, list(range(n)), U, gp=gp, gm=gm, theta=theta, lam=[0] * n, vres=[0] * n,
                  dx=[0] * 4, dy=[0] * n, Ap=(Ap if signed else 0), Am=Am, rho=rho, lo=0, hi=h, b=b, pat=PAT,
                  Bu=Bu, name='Φ_γα3' + ('^±' if signed else '^0') + ('[阻断]' if block else ''))

def alpha1(regime='I', rho1=Fr(1, 32), cD=Fr(1, 16), u=h, thM=Fr(3, 4), thD=Fr(1), Am=Fr(1, 8)):
    """Φ_α：α1 单区室。M = MBON-α1（0）、D = PAM-α1（1）、Z（2）；U_MD = u > 0（谷氨酸经 NMDA 受体为兴奋的申报读法，Ichinose 等 2015）。
    regime 'I'：教学单独不足以使 DAN 发放，须与 MBON 发放相合（J + c_A < θ_D ≤ J + c_A + u）；'II'：教学单独足够。"""
    b = _b({0: h, 1: cD, 2: 0})
    rho = {(i, 0): rho1 for i in KC}
    M = Member(4, ['MBON-a1', 'PAM-a1', 'Z'], [0, 1, 2], {(0, 1): u}, gp=[1, 2, 2], gm=[1, 2, 2],
               theta=[thM, thD, 1], lam=[0, 0, 0], vres=[0, 0, 0], dx=[0] * 4, dy=[0] * 3,
               Ap=0, Am=Am, rho=rho, lo=0, hi=h, b=b, pat=PAT, name='Φ_α^' + regime)
    return M

def US(n_out, j, J):
    I = [Fr(0)] * n_out
    I[j] = Fr(J)
    return I

def Z0(n_out):
    return [Fr(0)] * n_out

def hist_gamma(n_out=3, J=Fr(3, 2)):
    """χ_γ 的申报史：配对 ×3（间隔 1 拍静置），静置 2 拍，CS+ 测试、静置、CS− 测试、静置、CS+ 测试、静置。W = 拍 6—12。"""
    P = ('A', US(n_out, 1, J))
    R = ('none', Z0(n_out))
    seq = [P, R, P, R, P, R, R, ('A', Z0(n_out)), R, ('B', Z0(n_out)), R, ('A', Z0(n_out)), R]
    return seq, (6, 12)

def hist_gamma_alpha3(n_out, J1=Fr(3, 2), late=14):
    """χ_γα3 的申报史：配对 ×3（CS+ 配电击 I_{D1} = J1；I_{D2} = 0），静置 1 拍；早期非强化 CS+（拍 6）、静置、CS−、静置；
    长静置后晚期非强化 CS+（拍 9 + late）、静置。W = 拍 5 至末拍。"""
    P = ('A', US(n_out, 1, J1))
    R = ('none', Z0(n_out))
    seq = [P, R, P, R, P, R, ('A', Z0(n_out)), R, ('B', Z0(n_out))] + [R] * late + [('A', Z0(n_out)), R]
    return seq, (5, len(seq) - 1)

def hist_alpha1(n_out=3, J=h):
    """χ_α 的申报史：配对 ×4（间隔 1 拍），静置 2 拍，CS+ 测试、静置、CS− 测试、静置。W = 拍 0—12（含训练，申报 W 覆盖训练至测试）。"""
    P = ('A', US(n_out, 1, J))
    R = ('none', Z0(n_out))
    seq = [P, R, P, R, P, R, P, R, R, ('A', Z0(n_out)), R, ('B', Z0(n_out)), R]
    return seq, (0, 12)

def hist_selfrenew(n_out=3, J=Fr(3, 2), gaps=(2, 2, 2, 2)):
    """Φ_sr 的申报史：配对 ×3，其后 CS+ 再暴露，间隔为 gaps 所列的静置拍数。W = 第一个再暴露至末拍。"""
    P = ('A', US(n_out, 1, J))
    R = ('none', Z0(n_out))
    seq = [P, R, P, R, P]
    t0 = None
    for gp in gaps:
        seq += [R] * gp
        if t0 is None:
            t0 = len(seq)
        seq.append(('A', Z0(n_out)))
    seq.append(R)
    return seq, (t0, len(seq) - 1)
~~~

A.9　qs19\_models.py　本篇的库：分级类 𝒩₃F（Graded3F）、小脑对照（Cereb）、巩固窗（Consol）、KCAdapter、to\_jxs、定理 19-D 的冻结候选（WindowCand）

~~~python
"""《区室化第三因子回路迹应变换区分式》v1.0 附录库 qs19_models.py：本篇的申报宿载模型。参数一律为构造值（无量纲有理数），不是物质测量值。
只用标准库；一切数值为 fractions.Fraction；无随机数（随机成员由 qs19_run.py 以固定种子生成并作为参数传入）。
接口：〔十七〕附录 jhc_core.Host（X、lo、hi、ground、orient、init、grid、act(w, s)、comps(w, y, s) -> [(过程, 位, 分项)]），
      用于〔十七〕的三分、深度、组织超图、定义 6′ 证书与两支分报；
      to_jxs(host) 把同一模型改写为〔十八〕附录 jxs_core.Host（positions、read、comps -> {位: [(过程, 分项)]}、post），
      用于〔十八〕的势、Act/Aff、两套分辨率语义与运行。两种接口读同一组律函数，不各写一份律。
模型（正文对应条款见各类的说明）：
  Graded3F      区室化第三因子回路的分级类 𝒩₃F（正文定义 19-1、定理 19-4、19-7′、19-H）：
                MBON 截断线性读出、DAN 截断线性（含回返 U）、写入 = η·DAN（无符号）或 η·(DAN − θ*)（带符号），逐位弛豫，夹限 [0, 1]。
  Cereb         小脑平行纤维—浦肯野突触 LTD 的对照成员（正文 4.7 跨宿载对照）：PC 截断线性、PC ⊣ DCN ⊣ IO（攀缘纤维），
                LTD ∝ CF、平行纤维单独呈现时 LTP。只作跨宿载的结构对照。
  Consol        巩固窗模型（正文定理 19-10）：α 区室深度 a_i、律层分量 c_i（巩固态）；窗内 DAN 活动为
                'perm'（与迹应无关的放行型）、'inh'（经抑制性回返读迹应）、'exc'（经兴奋性回返读迹应）。
  KCAdapter     把二值类 𝒦_B 成员（k17_core.Member，粗拍 λ = 0、d_x = d_y = 0）改写为〔十八〕运行系统的宿载
                （正文 3.3 的对拍）：完整状态只含 KC→单元位的深度 d = b − w。
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
from jhc_core import Host
F = Fr
ZERO = Fr(0)

def relu(x):
    return x if x > 0 else ZERO

class Graded3F(Host):
    """𝒩₃F 的申报成员。comps_names：区室名表；KC：入口数；odors：{信号前缀: KC 子集}；
    theta[c]：MBON 截断阈；beta[c]：DAN 基础驱动；U[(c', c)]：MBON_c' → DAN_c 的回返系数；us[c]：强化信号对 DAN_c 的注入；
    eta[c]：写入率；rho[c]：每拍弛豫比；signed[c]：带符号写入时的零点 θ*_c（None 为无符号写入）；
    orient：'dep'（压抑型：读出随深度降）或 'pot'（增强型：读出随深度升）；bg：背景信号 'bg' 下每个 KC 的激活度（无气味期的自发活动）。
    信号：'rest'、气味名、气味名 + '+'（配强化）、'bg'。位名为 '区室:KC'。"""
    def __init__(self, comps_names, KC, odors, theta, beta, U, us, eta, rho, signed=None, orient='dep', bg=ZERO,
                 name='Graded3F', grid=None):
        self.name = name
        self.C = list(comps_names)
        self.KC = list(range(KC))
        self.odors = {k: tuple(v) for k, v in odors.items()}
        self.theta = {c: F(theta[c]) for c in self.C}
        self.beta = {c: F(beta[c]) for c in self.C}
        self.U = {k: F(v) for k, v in U.items()}
        self.us = {c: F(us.get(c, 0)) for c in self.C}
        self.eta = {c: F(eta[c]) for c in self.C}
        self.rho = {c: F(rho[c]) for c in self.C}
        self.signed = {c: (None if (signed is None or signed.get(c) is None) else F(signed[c])) for c in self.C}
        self.orient_kind = orient
        self.bgv = F(bg)
        self.X = ['%s:%d' % (c, i) for c in self.C for i in self.KC]
        self.lo = {x: ZERO for x in self.X}
        self.hi = {x: F(1) for x in self.X}
        self.orient = {x: 1 for x in self.X}
        self.ground = {x: ZERO for x in self.X}
        self.init = dict(self.ground)
        self.grid = grid
    def kc_act(self, s):
        """各 KC 在信号 s 下的激活度（0、1 或背景值）。"""
        if s == 'bg':
            return {i: self.bgv for i in self.KC}
        base = s[:-1] if s.endswith('+') else s
        K = self.odors.get(base, ())
        return {i: (F(1) if i in K else ZERO) for i in self.KC}
    def act(self, w, s):
        a = self.kc_act(s)
        y = {}
        for c in self.C:
            if self.orient_kind == 'dep':
                drive = sum((a[i] * (1 - w['%s:%d' % (c, i)]) for i in self.KC), ZERO)
            else:
                drive = sum((a[i] * (1 + w['%s:%d' % (c, i)]) for i in self.KC), ZERO)
            y['MBON_' + c] = relu(drive - self.theta[c])
        shock = s.endswith('+')
        for c in self.C:
            v = self.beta[c] + (self.us[c] if shock else ZERO)
            for (c1, c2), u in self.U.items():
                if c2 == c:
                    v += u * y['MBON_' + c1]
            y['DAN_' + c] = relu(v)
        return y
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        a = self.kc_act(s)
        out = []
        for c in self.C:
            dan = y['DAN_' + c]
            drive = dan if self.signed[c] is None else dan - self.signed[c]
            for i in self.KC:
                x = '%s:%d' % (c, i)
                if a[i] != 0:
                    out.append(('W_' + c, x, self.eta[c] * a[i] * drive))
                if self.rho[c] != 0:
                    out.append(('R_' + c, x, -self.rho[c] * w[x]))
        return out

class Cereb(Host):
    """小脑对照成员：位 'pf:i' 为平行纤维 i → 浦肯野细胞突触的 LTD 深度（压抑型）。
    PC = max(0, Σ_{K(s)} (1 − d_i) + p0 − θ_P)；DCN = max(0, n0 − κ·PC)；CF（下橄榄）= max(0, o0 − λ·DCN + us·[强化])；
    写入：K(s) 中各位 LTD 分项 η·CF，平行纤维单独呈现的 LTP 分项 −η_p（深度减），无弛豫。
    有效回返符号 u_eff = (−κ)·(−λ) = κλ > 0（经两级抑制的净兴奋）。"""
    def __init__(self, KC=4, odors=None, p0=F(0), thP=F(0), n0=F(2), kappa=F(1, 2), o0=F(1, 2), lam=F(1, 2), us=F(1),
                 eta=F(1, 8), eta_p=F(1, 32), name='Cereb'):
        self.name = name
        self.KC = list(range(KC))
        self.odors = odors or {'A': (0, 1), 'B': (2, 3)}
        self.p0, self.thP, self.n0, self.kappa, self.o0, self.lam, self.us = p0, thP, n0, kappa, o0, lam, us
        self.eta, self.eta_p = eta, eta_p
        self.X = ['pf:%d' % i for i in self.KC]
        self.lo = {x: ZERO for x in self.X}
        self.hi = {x: F(1) for x in self.X}
        self.orient = {x: 1 for x in self.X}
        self.ground = {x: ZERO for x in self.X}
        self.init = dict(self.ground)
        self.grid = None
    def K(self, s):
        return self.odors.get(s[:-1] if s.endswith('+') else s, ())
    def act(self, w, s):
        K = self.K(s)
        pc = relu(sum((1 - w['pf:%d' % i] for i in K), ZERO) + self.p0 - self.thP)
        dcn = relu(self.n0 - self.kappa * pc)
        cf = relu(self.o0 - self.lam * dcn + (self.us if s.endswith('+') else ZERO))
        return {'PC': pc, 'DCN': dcn, 'CF': cf}
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        out = []
        for i in self.K(s):
            x = 'pf:%d' % i
            out.append(('LTD', x, self.eta * y['CF']))
            out.append(('LTP', x, -self.eta_p))
        return out

class Consol(Host):
    """巩固窗模型：位 'a:i'（α 区室 KC i 的压抑深度）与 'c:i'（律层分量：巩固态，0 或 1）。
    读出：MBON_α = Σ_i act_i·(1 − a_i)；窗内 DAN_w：
      'perm'：DAN_w = κ（与迹应无关；例如由与 T 无关的振荡或外源驱动维持）；
      'inh'：DAN_w = max(0, κ + u·MBON_α)，u < 0（抑制性回返：迹应越深、MBON 越弱、DAN 越强）；
      'exc'：DAN_w = max(0, κ + u·MBON_α)，u > 0（兴奋性回返）。
    背景信号 'bg' 下每个 KC 的激活度为 bg；训练信号 'A+' 只写 a（η·[i ∈ K(A)]），不写 c。
    巩固写入 CONS@c:i：窗信号 'bg' 下 DAN_w ≥ θ_c、a_i > tag 且 c_i = 0 时分项 +1（律层置位）。
    弛豫 R@a:i：−(1 − ρ(c_i))·a_i，ρ(0) = rho0、ρ(1) = rho1（巩固态改写退浅律，承〔十八〕D18-11）。
    block=True：窗内阻断 DAN 输出（DAN_w 读作 0）。
    theta_M：MBON_α 的截断阈，MBON_α = max(0, Σ_i act_i·(1 − a_i) − θ_M)；默认 0（Σ 恒 ≥ 0，截断不起作用）。"""
    def __init__(self, mode='perm', KC=4, K_A=(0, 1), kappa=F(1, 2), u=ZERO, bg=F(1, 4), theta_c=F(1, 2), tag=F(1, 20),
                 eta=F(1, 2), rho0=F(1, 2), rho1=F(9, 10), block=False, name=None, theta_M=ZERO):
        self.mode, self.KC, self.K_A = mode, list(range(KC)), tuple(K_A)
        self.kappa, self.u, self.bgv, self.theta_c, self.tag = F(kappa), F(u), F(bg), F(theta_c), F(tag)
        self.eta, self.rho0, self.rho1, self.block = F(eta), F(rho0), F(rho1), block
        self.theta_M = F(theta_M)
        self.name = name or ('Consol_' + mode + ('_阻断' if block else ''))
        self.X = ['a:%d' % i for i in self.KC] + ['c:%d' % i for i in self.KC]
        self.lo = {x: ZERO for x in self.X}
        self.hi = {x: F(1) for x in self.X}
        self.orient = {x: 1 for x in self.X}
        self.ground = {x: ZERO for x in self.X}
        self.init = dict(self.ground)
        self.grid = None
    def kc(self, s):
        if s == 'bg':
            return {i: self.bgv for i in self.KC}
        if s in ('A', 'A+'):
            return {i: (F(1) if i in self.K_A else ZERO) for i in self.KC}
        return {i: ZERO for i in self.KC}
    def act(self, w, s):
        k = self.kc(s)
        m = relu(sum((k[i] * (1 - w['a:%d' % i]) for i in self.KC), ZERO) - self.theta_M)
        if s != 'bg':
            dw = ZERO
        elif self.mode == 'perm':
            dw = self.kappa
        else:
            dw = relu(self.kappa + self.u * m)
        if self.block:
            dw = ZERO
        return {'MBON_a': m, 'DAN_w': dw}
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        out = []
        for i in self.KC:
            a, c = 'a:%d' % i, 'c:%d' % i
            if s == 'A+' and i in self.K_A:
                out.append(('W', a, self.eta))
            rho = self.rho1 if w[c] >= 1 else self.rho0
            out.append(('R', a, -(1 - rho) * w[a]))
            if s == 'bg' and y['DAN_w'] >= self.theta_c and w[a] > self.tag and w[c] < 1:
                out.append(('CONS', c, F(1)))
        return out

def to_jxs(host):
    """把 jhc 接口的宿载改写为〔十八〕附录 jxs_core.Host：位相同；read 即 act；comps 按位聚为 {位: [(过程, 分项)]}；
    post 为 [lo, hi] 夹限（逐位后处理 LP）。"""
    from jxs_core import Host as JHost
    def read(d, r, s):
        return host.act(d, s)
    def comps(d, y, s):
        cs = {}
        for (p, x, c) in host.comps(d, y, s):
            cs.setdefault(x, []).append((p, c))
        return cs
    def post(dn, do):
        return {x: min(host.hi[x], max(host.lo[x], dn[x])) for x in dn}
    return JHost(host.X, read, comps, post)

class KCAdapter:
    """二值类 𝒦_B 粗拍成员（λ = 0、v_reset = 0、d_x = d_y = 0）在〔十八〕运行系统中的独立改写：
    完整状态 d[(i, j)] = b_ij − w_ij；信号为 (字母, 注入元组)；读出按拓扑次序求各单元门控 g_j；
    写入分项按〔十八〕方程 4.2 逐过程给出：PLUS（深度 −A₊·x′_i，门控 g_{gp(j)}）、MINUS（深度 +A₋·y′_{gm(j)}，KC 激活时）、
    RELAX（深度 −ρ_ij·d_ij）；post 为把 w 夹于 [lo, hi]（即 d 夹于 [b − hi, b − lo]）。"""
    def __init__(self, M):
        self.M = M
        for j in range(M.n_out):
            assert M.lam[j] == 0 and M.dy[j] == 0
        for i in range(M.n_in):
            assert M.dx[i] == 0
        self.positions = [(i, j) for i in range(M.n_in) for j in range(M.n_out)]
    def gates(self, d, s):
        M = self.M
        letter, I = s
        pat = M.pat[letter]
        g = {}
        for j in M.order:
            val = sum(((M.b[(i, j)] - d[(i, j)]) for i in range(M.n_in) if pat[i]), ZERO) + F(I[j])
            for (k, jj), u in M.U.items():
                if jj == j and g[k]:
                    val += u
            g[j] = 1 if val >= M.theta[j] else 0
        return g
    def host(self):
        from jxs_core import Host as JHost
        M = self
        def read(d, r, s):
            g = M.gates(d, s)
            return {('g', j): g[j] for j in g}
        def comps(d, y, s):
            Mm = M.M
            pat = Mm.pat[s[0]]
            cs = {}
            for (i, j) in M.positions:
                lst = []
                if Mm.plastic and Mm.bap and y[('g', Mm.gp[j])]:
                    lst.append(('PLUS', -Mm.Ap * pat[i]))
                if Mm.plastic and Mm.bap and pat[i]:
                    lst.append(('MINUS', Mm.Am * y[('g', Mm.gm[j])]))
                if Mm.plastic and Mm.rho[(i, j)] != 0:
                    lst.append(('RELAX', -Mm.rho[(i, j)] * d[(i, j)]))
                if lst:
                    cs[(i, j)] = lst
            return cs
        def post(dn, do):
            Mm = M.M
            return {k: min(Mm.b[k] - Mm.lo, max(Mm.b[k] - Mm.hi, v)) for k, v in dn.items()}
        return JHost(self.positions, read, comps, post)
    def zero(self):
        return {p: ZERO for p in self.positions}

class WindowCand:
    """区分定理 19-D 的冻结候选（巩固窗内 DAN 读数）。同一宿载、同一窗信号 bg、同一背景激活向量 b（每 KC 一值）。
    候选 'P'（放行型）：DAN_w(d) = Q(κ)；候选 'R'（回返型，回返系数 u ≠ 0）：DAN_w(d) = Q(κ + u·R(Σ_i b_i(1 − d_i) − θ_M))。
    R、Q 为截断线性 max(0, ·)。观测：两臂（训练 d^T、假训练 d^S）之差 Δ = DAN_w(d^T) − DAN_w(d^S)。"""
    def __init__(self, kind, kappa, u=ZERO, b=(), theta_M=ZERO):
        assert kind in ('P', 'R')
        self.kind, self.kappa, self.u, self.b, self.theta_M = kind, F(kappa), F(u), tuple(F(x) for x in b), F(theta_M)
    def mbon(self, d):
        return relu(sum((bi * (1 - di) for bi, di in zip(self.b, d)), ZERO) - self.theta_M)
    def dan_input(self, d):
        return self.kappa if self.kind == 'P' else self.kappa + self.u * self.mbon(d)
    def dan(self, d):
        return relu(self.dan_input(d))
    def delta(self, dT, dS):
        return self.dan(dT) - self.dan(dS)
~~~

A.10　qs19\_run.py　运行入口：核验 Q0—Q12

~~~python
"""《区室化第三因子回路迹应变换区分式》v1.0 附录程序 qs19_run.py：运行入口。
运行：python3 qs19_run.py（与附录 A 的其他文件置于同一目录）；逐项输出「通过」或「失败」，末尾汇总；任一项失败时退出码为 1。
节：Q0 程序文件与上游冻结程序的 SHA-256；Q1 找迹应；Q2 单个迹应；Q3 集合组织；Q4 势的形成；Q5 势实行为活动；
    Q6 活动再作用于集合；Q7 动力类型与核验；Q8 二值类 𝒦_B 上的定理 19-5、19-6、19-7；Q9 定量预测的模型内核验；
    Q10—Q12 各条款的边界例（正反两侧）。
纪律：只用标准库；一切数值为 fractions.Fraction（小数只用于显示）；随机成员由固定种子的 random.Random 生成；
      输出不含用时与绝对路径，逐字节可复现。有限核验只覆盖生成域；全称结论以正文的证明为准。
上游冻结程序（jhc_core、jhc_models、jhc_bridge、jxs_core、jxs_models、field_final）与上游正文附录逐字节相同，本程序只调用。"""
import hashlib, os, random, sys
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
from fractions import Fraction as F
RES = []

def check(tag, cond):
    RES.append(bool(cond))
    print(('  通过  ' if cond else '  失败  ') + tag, flush=True)

def sec(t):
    print('\n' + t, flush=True)

def fs(x):
    x = F(x)
    return str(x.numerator) if x.denominator == 1 else '%d/%d' % (x.numerator, x.denominator)

def dec(x, n=4):
    return ('%.' + str(n) + 'f') % float(x)

def sha(p):
    with open(p, 'rb') as f:
        return hashlib.sha256(f.read()).hexdigest()

print('《区室化第三因子回路迹应变换区分式》v1.0 附录程序 qs19_run.py')
# ======================================================================
sec('Q0　程序文件的 SHA-256；上游冻结程序与其上游正文附录所列值相符')
UPSTREAM = {
    'jhc_core.py': ('1666528897308c0047e58b24ac531bc0e4edefa7417bb905a997a0940ffd3deb', '《具化场识别判定式》v1.0 附录 A.1'),
    'jhc_models.py': ('d3878bfbcefa485ada692714ed5a5a0ccdfe76f0934596b797c55c09fe820e6d', '《具化场识别判定式》v1.0 附录 A.2'),
    'jhc_bridge.py': ('9a366d5151cfcda63efc327944190f2b2e1d38f58994b896a0856644d0e53605', '《具化场识别判定式》v1.0 附录 A.3'),
    'jxs_core.py': ('a38202d683cff68946908735b88a3acccb430eb03d24ee66e2acf6d8b33322f7', '《迹应集合变换数理式》v1.0 附录 A.1'),
    'jxs_models.py': ('87cb0ab0058ecd5ac77d7dce527deed3264c40137cb0d50696c5140129ffd0fb', '《迹应集合变换数理式》v1.0 附录 A.2'),
    'field_final.py': ('890a7a73eb45766c23980a39825626a48f5086abeceec6ad03477d57373aa1da', '《迹应集合变换数理式》v1.0 附录 A.4 ＝《场具化数理式》v1.0 附录 A.1'),
}
for fn, (h, where) in UPSTREAM.items():
    got = sha(os.path.join(HERE, fn))
    check('%s（%s）SHA-256 %s…%s' % (fn, where, got[:8], '相符' if got == h else '不符'), got == h)
for fn in ('k17_core.py', 'k17_models.py', 'qs19_models.py'):
    print('  %s SHA-256 %s' % (fn, sha(os.path.join(HERE, fn))))
import jhc_core as JC
import jhc_models as JM
import jhc_bridge as JB
import jxs_core as XC
import jxs_models as XM
import k17_core as KC
import k17_models as KM
import qs19_models as BM
EPS = F(1, 100)
# ======================================================================
sec('Q1　步骤一　找迹应：信号、介质、留存的改变、承担载体、留存尺度与集合边界（〔十七〕定义 1、2′、4′，定理 17-1，引理 17-1′）')
Fy = JM.Fly('full')
w = dict(Fy.ground)
w['g0'], w['a0'] = F(1, 2), F(1, 2)
sg, sa = [], []
for t in range(300):
    sg.append(w['g0'])
    sa.append(w['a0'])
    w = Fy.step(w, 'rest')

def stepG(u):
    v = dict(Fy.ground)
    v['g0'] = u
    return Fy.step(v, 'rest')['g0']

band = [F(k, 4096) for k in range(0, 41)]
rg, fg = JC.retention(sg, EPS, tail=('不变集', stepG, band))
ra, fa = JC.retention(sa, EPS)
~~~

&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(rg, fg) == (29, '完整') and (ra, fa) == (300, '下界'))

~~~text
Mg = KM.gamma_alpha3()
seq = [('A', KM.US(Mg.n_out, 1, F(3, 2))), ('none', KM.Z0(Mg.n_out))] * 3
seq += [('none', KM.Z0(Mg.n_out))] * 60
tr = KC.run(Mg, Mg.init_state(), seq)
k_end = 5
u1 = [Mg.b[(0, 0)] - r['post'][0][(0, 0)] for r in tr[k_end:]]
u2 = [Mg.b[(0, 2)] - r['post'][0][(0, 2)] for r in tr[k_end:]]
~~~

~~~python
# ======================================================================
sec('Q2　步骤二　单个迹应：加深、退浅、零变化、饱和、越过基线与新增（〔十七〕定义 3′、定理 17-2、17-3；〔十八〕D18-8；定理 19-2）')
grid = [F(k, 16) for k in range(17)]

def at(host, vals):
    v = dict(host.ground)
    v.update(vals)
    return v

pairs_g = [(q, abs(Fy.act(at(Fy, {'g0': q}), 'A')['MBON_g'] - Fy.act(Fy.ground, 'A')['MBON_g'])) for q in grid]
pairs_a = [(q, abs(Fy.act(at(Fy, {'a0': q}), 'A')['MBON_a3'] - Fy.act(Fy.ground, 'A')['MBON_a3'])) for q in grid]
hid = [q for q in grid if q > 0 and abs(Fy.act(at(Fy, {'g0': q}), 'B')['MBON_g'] - Fy.act(Fy.ground, 'B')['MBON_g']) == 0]
~~~

check('Q2a K2（相对探针 CS+、读出 MBON 驱动）：γ1 与 α3 单突触压抑深度与参与效应序关系冲突 %d、%d 对；对探针 CS−（不激活该 KC）%d 个非基态深度皆隐（须申报探针类）' %  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(len(JC.order\_conflicts(pairs\_g)), len(JC.order\_conflicts(pairs\_a)), len(hid)),  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;not JC.order\_conflicts(pairs\_g) and not JC.order\_conflicts(pairs\_a) and len(hid) == 16)  
k3\_pair = all(Fy.step(at(Fy, {'g0': q}), 'A+')\['g0'\] \>= q for q in grid)  
k3\_unp = \[q for q in grid if Fy.step(at(Fy, {'g0': q}), 'A')\['g0'\] \< q\]  
a3\_sign = \[(gq, Fy.step(at(Fy, {'g0': gq, 'g1': gq, 'a0': F(1, 2)}), 'A')\['a0'\] - F(1, 2)) for gq in grid\]  
k3\_a3\_ok = \[gq for (gq, dlt) in a3\_sign if dlt \>= 0\]  
check('Q2b K3 只对申报写入条件：γ1 在 𝒲 = {CS+ 配强化} 下重复不擦（全格成立 %s）；在非强化 CS+ 下 %d 个非零深度退浅；'  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;'α3 在非强化 CS+ 下的 K3 取决于 γ1 深度（γ1 共同深度 ≥ %s 时成立）——α3 的写入条件须连同其组织申报' %  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(k3\_pair, len(k3\_unp), fs(min(k3\_a3\_ok))), k3\_pair and len(k3\_unp) == 16 and min(k3\_a3\_ok) == F(4, 16))  
g\_sat = at(Fy, {'g0': F(1), 'g1': F(1)})  
check('Q2c 饱和：γ1 位在深度 1（夹限上端）再配对，后态 %s——零变化由夹限承担（分项非零而后态不变）' % fs(Fy.step(g\_sat, 'A+')\['g0'\]),

~~~text
      Fy.step(g_sat, 'A+')['g0'] == 1)
first_off = next(t for t, v in enumerate(sg) if v <= EPS)
check('Q2d 退浅至迹应现象不发生：γ1 位自深度 1/2 静置 %d 拍后不越 ε，此后由正向不变阈下集保持（Q1a 的尾部证据）' % first_off, first_off == 29)
# 对拍：二值类 𝒦_B 粗拍成员在〔十八〕运行系统中的独立改写（KCAdapter）与 k17_core.step 逐拍一致
rng = random.Random(19001)
mism, beats, gate_mism = 0, 0, 0
for trial in range(120):
    kind = rng.choice(['g', 'ga3', 'ga3s', 'a1'])
    if kind == 'g':
        M = KM.gamma(rho1=rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 2)]), cD=rng.choice([F(1, 16), F(1, 4)]),
                     u=rng.choice([F(-1), F(-1, 2)]), Am=rng.choice([F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)]))
    elif kind == 'ga3':
        M = KM.gamma_alpha3(rho1=rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)]), rho2=rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 64), F(1, 2), F(1)]),
                            Am=rng.choice([F(1, 16), F(1, 8)]))
    elif kind == 'ga3s':
        M = KM.gamma_alpha3(signed=True, rho2=rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 8)]), Ap=rng.choice([F(1, 16), F(1, 8)]))
    else:
        M = KM.alpha1(regime=rng.choice(['I', 'II']), rho1=rng.choice([F(1, 32), F(1, 8)]))
    AD = BM.KCAdapter(M)
    H = AD.host()
    st = M.init_state()
    d = AD.zero()
    for t in range(25):
        letter = rng.choice(['A', 'A', 'B', 'none'])
        J = rng.choice([F(0), F(0), F(1, 2), F(1), F(3, 2)])
        I = [F(0)] * M.n_out
        I[1] = J
        st2, g, vt, ev = KC.step(M, st, letter, I)
        y, d2, _, rec = XC.step(H, d, None, (letter, tuple(I)))
        beats += 1
        if any(M.b[k] - st2[0][k] != d2[k] for k in AD.positions):
            mism += 1
        if any(y[('g', j)] != g[j] for j in range(M.n_out)):
            gate_mism += 1
        st, d = st2, d2
check('Q2e 对拍：二值类 𝒦_B 粗拍成员 120 个 × 25 拍（%d 拍），〔十八〕step（原机制语义、LP 夹限）的后态与 k17_core.step 不一致 %d 拍、门控不一致 %d 拍' %
      (beats, mism, gate_mism), mism == 0 and gate_mism == 0)
# 定理 19-2（单迹应三分，按〔十八〕D18-8 陈述）：写入分项发生 ⇔ x ∈ Act^ε(MINUS)；较无写入对照更深；实际净增深 ⇔ Δd_x > 0
rng = random.Random(19002)
n_beats, bad, cnt = 0, 0, {'写入': 0, '较对照深': 0, '净增深': 0, '写入而未净增深': 0}
for trial in range(400):
    rho2 = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 64), F(1, 8), F(1, 2), F(1)])
    Am = rng.choice([F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)])
    M = KM.gamma_alpha3(rho1=rng.choice([F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)]), rho2=rho2, Am=Am)
    AD = BM.KCAdapter(M)
    H = AD.host()
    d = AD.zero()
    hist = [('A', (0, F(3, 2), 0, 0, 0))] * rng.choice([2, 3, 4]) + [('none', (0,) * 5)] * rng.randrange(0, 6)
    hist += [('A', (0,) * 5)]
    for s in hist[:-1]:
        _, d, _, _ = XC.step(H, d, None, s)
    s = hist[-1]
    y, d2, _, rec = XC.step(H, d, None, s)
    x = (0, 2)
    lst = rec['comps'].get(x, [])
    minus = sum(c for p, c in lst if p == 'MINUS')
    others = sum(c for p, c in lst if p != 'MINUS')
    post = H.post
    with_w = post({x: d[x] + minus + others}, None)[x]
    without = post({x: d[x] + others}, None)[x]
    s2, hi = d[x], M.b[x] - M.lo
    n_beats += 1
    wr = minus != 0
    deeper = with_w > without
    net = d2[x] > d[x]
    pred_deeper = wr and (1 - rho2) * s2 < hi
    pred_net = wr and s2 < hi and Am > rho2 * s2
    if (wr != (x in rec['Act0'] and minus != 0)) or deeper != pred_deeper or net != pred_net or net != (x in rec['Aff0'] and d2[x] > d[x]):
        bad += 1
    cnt['写入'] += wr
    cnt['较对照深'] += deeper
    cnt['净增深'] += net
    cnt['写入而未净增深'] += (wr and not net)

      (cnt['写入'], cnt['较对照深'], cnt['净增深'], cnt['写入而未净增深'], bad), bad == 0 and cnt['写入而未净增深'] > 0)
~~~

~~~python
sec('Q3　步骤三　集合组织：交叠、区室、汇聚与子集间干预（〔十七〕定理 17-4′；〔十八〕D18-4、D18-12、定理 18-9；命题 19-3）')

def org_after(host, hist, probes):
    S_, R_, pos_, neg_, sig_ = JC.trace_candidates(host, hist, EPS)
    hy_, cps_ = JC.org_graph_exact(host, S_[-1], pos_, probes, EPS)
    return pos_, hy_, cps_
~~~

~~~text
sec('Q4　步骤四　历史动力形成势：本位所受之势、方向律与跨区室承担（〔十八〕D18-6、D18-13、定理 18-5（三）；定理 19-4）')
# 定理 19-4（分级符号律）：随机单区室成员，比较非强化 CS+ 拍上两共同深度 s < s′ 的写入分项
rng = random.Random(19004)
viol, strict = 0, {('dep', -1): 0, ('dep', 1): 0, ('pot', -1): 0, ('pot', 1): 0}
plateau = 0
for trial in range(600):
    orient = rng.choice(['dep', 'pot'])
    u = rng.choice([F(-1), F(-1, 2), F(-1, 4), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)])
    th = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)])
    be = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)])
    sg_ = rng.choice([None, F(1, 8), F(1, 4)])
    Gm = BM.Graded3F(['c'], 4, {'A': (0, 1)}, {'c': th}, {'c': be}, {('c', 'c'): u}, {'c': F(1)},
                     {'c': rng.choice([F(1, 8), F(1, 4)])}, {'c': F(0)}, signed={'c': sg_}, orient=orient)
    a_, b_ = sorted(rng.sample([F(k, 16) for k in range(17)], 2))
    wa, wb = at(Gm, {'c:0': a_, 'c:1': a_}), at(Gm, {'c:0': b_, 'c:1': b_})
    ca = JC.total_comp(Gm, wa, 'A', 'W_c', 'c:0')
    cb = JC.total_comp(Gm, wb, 'A', 'W_c', 'c:0')
    dirn = (cb > ca) - (cb < ca)
    expect = (-1 if u > 0 else 1) if orient == 'dep' else (1 if u > 0 else -1)
    if dirn == 0:
        plateau += 1
    elif dirn != expect:
        viol += 1
    else:
        strict[(orient, 1 if u > 0 else -1)] += 1
check('Q4a 定理 19-4（分级符号律）：600 对随机成员与深度对，写入随深度的方向 = m(ψ)·m(w)；与「压抑型 −sgn u、增强型 +sgn u」相反者 %d；'
      '严格见证（压抑 u<0 续写 %d、压抑 u>0 自限 %d、增强 u<0 自限 %d、增强 u>0 续写 %d），平台（零变化）%d' %
      (viol, strict[('dep', -1)], strict[('dep', 1)], strict[('pot', -1)], strict[('pot', 1)], plateau),
      viol == 0 and all(v > 0 for v in strict.values()))
# 二值极限与〔十八〕γ1 自环玩具 LOOP 的对拍（三种实现同向）
agree = True
for u in (F(-1), F(1, 2)):
    for s_lo, s_hi in ((F(0), F(3, 4)), (F(1, 10), F(9, 10))):
        _, gM_lo, gD_lo = XM.loop_step(s_lo, u)
        _, gM_hi, gD_hi = XM.loop_step(s_hi, u)
        loop_dir = gD_hi - gD_lo
        if (u < 0 and loop_dir < 0) or (u > 0 and loop_dir > 0):
            agree = False
for (M, sgn) in ((KM.selfrenew(), -1), (KM.alpha1('I'), 1)):
    AD = BM.KCAdapter(M)
    H = AD.host()
    shallow = AD.zero()
    deep = AD.zero()
    for i in (0, 1):
        deep[(i, 0)] = F(1, 4)
    I0 = (0,) * M.n_out
    g_sh = AD.gates(shallow, ('A', I0))[1]
    g_dp = AD.gates(deep, ('A', I0))[1]
    if (sgn < 0 and not (g_dp >= g_sh and g_dp == 1)) or (sgn > 0 and not (g_dp <= g_sh)):
        agree = False
check('Q4b 二值极限与玩具对拍：〔十八〕LOOP（u = −1 续写、u = 1/2 自限）、二值成员 Φ_sr（抑制性，深则 DAN 发放）与 Φ_α^I（兴奋性，深则 DAN 不增）三处方向与定理 19-4 一致', agree)
# α3 位所受之势只经 γ1 子集承担：本位深度不进入 ψ（活动中介环路增益为 0）；I(α3 ← γ1) ≠ 0、I(γ1 ← α3) = 0
~~~

~~~text
# 无气味期：背景 KC 活动为零时回返对 T 无作用；有背景活动时现有类即可承载经迹应的内部续行（第（乙）类）
Gi0 = BM.Graded3F(['g1'], 4, {'A': (0, 1)}, {'g1': F(1, 4)}, {'g1': F(2)}, {('g1', 'g1'): F(-1)}, {'g1': F(1)},
                  {'g1': F(1, 2)}, {'g1': F(1, 16)}, bg=F(0), name='Graded3F_抑制回返_无背景')
Gi1 = BM.Graded3F(['g1'], 4, {'A': (0, 1)}, {'g1': F(1, 4)}, {'g1': F(2)}, {('g1', 'g1'): F(-1)}, {'g1': F(1)},
                  {'g1': F(1, 2)}, {'g1': F(1, 16)}, bg=F(1, 2), name='Graded3F_抑制回返_有背景')
res = {}
for Gm in (Gi0, Gi1):
    w_ = dict(Gm.init)
    for s in ['A+', 'A+']:
        w_ = Gm.step(w_, s)
    states = [w_]
    sigs = ['bg', 'bg']
    for s in sigs:
        states.append(Gm.step(states[-1], s))
    st_, wit = JC.hdyn_cert(Gm, states, sigs, ['g1:0', 'g1:1'], EPS)
    wproc = None
    if st_ == '1':
        wproc = wit[2]
    sw = JC.sed(Gm, list(zip(states[:-1], sigs)), ['g1:0', 'g1:1'], EPS)
    res[Gm.name] = (st_, wproc, sw)
check('Q5c 无气味期（定理 19-11）：无背景 KC 活动时窗内只有弛豫承担历史条件（证书 %s，见证过程 %s，活动中介正分项台账 SED_W %s）；'
      '有背景 KC 活动（每 KC 1/2）时现有类即给出经迹应、经抑制性回返的写入（证书 %s，见证过程 %s，SED_W %s）' %
      (res[Gi0.name][0], res[Gi0.name][1], '有' if res[Gi0.name][2] else '无', res[Gi1.name][0], res[Gi1.name][1], '有' if res[Gi1.name][2] else '无'),
~~~

check('Q6a 〔十七〕定义 6′ 证书（模型模式）：Fly(full) 训练后，拍 %d 信号 %s 过程 %s 作用于 %s（分项 %s），对照 %s×%s 的总差 %s；两支 A = %s、D = %s（先换活动后改直读）' %

~~~python
      (k6, s6, p6, x6, dec(c6), list(Uset), fs(f6), dec(tot6), dec(A6), dec(D6)), st6 == '1')
# 〔十七〕8.2.4 的跨记录链证书（R7）计算
target = JM.Fly('full')

def trained(k):
    w_ = dict(target.ground)
    for s in ['A+'] + ['rest'] * k:
        w_ = target.step(w_, s)
    return w_

r1, r2, zeta, off = F(1, 20), F(1, 100), F(1, 40), F(1, 50)
ggrid = [F(k, 20) for k in range(-10, 11)]

def record2(kw):
    out = {}
    for j, u in enumerate(ggrid):
        Mh = JM.FlyRec2(u, **kw)
        w_ = dict(Mh.ground)
        w_['a0'] = w_['a1'] = F(1, 2)
        before = Mh.act(w_, 'A')['MBON_a3']
        w2_ = Mh.step(w_, 'A')
        after = Mh.act(w2_, 'A')['MBON_a3']
        out[u] = (before - after) / 2 + r2 * ((j % 3) - 1) / 2
    return out

gmeas = record2({'offset': off})
chain = {}
for k in (2, 6, 12):
    wT_ = trained(k)
    y1 = target.act(wT_, 'A')['PPL1_a3']
    y2 = target.act(JC.setT(target, wT_, ['g0', 'g1']), 'A')['PPL1_a3']
    chain[k] = JB.chain_certificate(y1 + r1 / 2, y2 - r1 / 3, r1, gmeas, r2, zeta, EPS, full_component=True)
check('Q6b 〔十七〕定理 17-8′ 的链证书：延迟 2 拍「%s」实际作用「%s」；延迟 6 拍「%s」实际作用「%s」；延迟 12 拍「%s」' %
      (chain[2]['why'], chain[2]['act'], chain[6]['why'], chain[6]['act'], chain[12]['why']),
      chain[2]['v'] == '1' and chain[2]['act_ok'] and chain[6]['v'] == '1' and not chain[6]['act_ok'] and chain[12]['v'] == '⊥')

# 定理 19-7′（分级跨区室门控）：再暴露写入关于 γ1 深度的斜率与带符号写入的翻转延迟
def two_comp(eta2, U12, beta2, th1, rho1, sgn2=None):
    return BM.Graded3F(['g1', 'a3'], 4, {'A': (0, 1), 'B': (2, 3)}, {'g1': th1, 'a3': F(1, 2)}, {'g1': F(0), 'a3': beta2},
                       {('g1', 'a3'): U12}, {'g1': F(1)}, {'g1': F(1, 2), 'a3': eta2}, {'g1': rho1, 'a3': F(0)},
                       signed={'a3': sgn2}, name='Graded3F_γ1α3')

rng = random.Random(19007)
slope_bad, slope_n, flip_bad, flip_inf, flip_fin = 0, 0, 0, 0, 0
for trial in range(300):
    eta2 = rng.choice([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)])
    U12 = rng.choice([F(-1, 2), F(-1), F(-2)])
    th1 = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)])
    beta2 = rng.choice([F(2), F(5, 2), F(3)])
    rho1 = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 2)])
    Gm = two_comp(eta2, U12, beta2, th1, rho1)
    s_a, s_b = sorted(rng.sample([F(k, 32) for k in range(0, 25)], 2))
    wa = at(Gm, {'g1:0': s_a, 'g1:1': s_a})
    wb = at(Gm, {'g1:0': s_b, 'g1:1': s_b})
    ca = JC.total_comp(Gm, wa, 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0')
    cb = JC.total_comp(Gm, wb, 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0')
    m_a, m_b = Gm.act(wa, 'A')['MBON_g1'], Gm.act(wb, 'A')['MBON_g1']
    dan_a, dan_b = Gm.act(wa, 'A')['DAN_a3'], Gm.act(wb, 'A')['DAN_a3']
    if m_a > 0 and m_b > 0 and dan_a > 0 and dan_b > 0:
        slope_n += 1
        if cb - ca != eta2 * (-U12) * 2 * (s_b - s_a):
            slope_bad += 1
    elif cb < ca:
        slope_bad += 1
    # 带符号写入：零点 θ* 取在 DAN 的可达区间内；翻转延迟 = MBON-γ1 回到 m* 的首拍
    sgn2 = rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(1), F(3, 2)])
    Gs2 = two_comp(eta2, U12, beta2 - 2, th1, rho1, sgn2=sgn2)
    sigma = rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(3, 4), F(7, 8)])
    m_star = (Gs2.beta['a3'] - sgn2) / (-U12)
    k_alpha, k_gamma = None, None
    for k in range(0, 200):
        sk = sigma * (1 - rho1) ** k
        wk = at(Gs2, {'g1:0': sk, 'g1:1': sk})
        yk = Gs2.act(wk, 'A')
        ck = JC.total_comp(Gs2, wk, 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0')
        if k_alpha is None and ck <= 0:
            k_alpha = k
        if k_gamma is None and yk['MBON_g1'] >= m_star:
            k_gamma = k
    if k_alpha != k_gamma:
        flip_bad += 1
    if k_alpha is None:
        flip_inf += 1
    else:
        flip_fin += 1
check('Q6c 定理 19-7′（一）：300 个分级两区室成员，线性区内再暴露写入对 γ1 共同深度的斜率恒为 η₂·|U₁₂|·n_A（%d 例，不符 %d）；全域单调不减' %
      (slope_n, slope_bad), slope_bad == 0 and slope_n > 0)
check('Q6d 定理 19-7′（二）：带符号写入下 α3 效应由加深转为不加深的首拍与 MBON-γ1 驱动回到 m* = (β₂ − θ*)/|U₁₂| 的首拍相同（不符 %d；有限 %d、200 拍内不出现 %d，后者含 ρ₁ = 0 的永不翻转支）' %
      (flip_bad, flip_fin, flip_inf), flip_bad == 0 and flip_inf > 0 and flip_fin > 0)

def nstar(D):
    n_ = 0
    while -XM.A_INN + 2 * XM.B_DIS * D * XM.R1 ** n_ > 0:
        n_ += 1
    return n_

check('Q6e 〔十八〕FLY 的首达区间（承〔十八〕PN-1、T19b）：n*(D) 与区间式 a/(2b r1^(n−1)) < D ≤ a/(2b r1^n) 在 D = 0.40..1.00 逐点一致——分级读法下翻转延迟是 γ1 初始深度的阶梯函数',
      all((XM.A_INN / (2 * XM.B_DIS * XM.R1 ** (nstar(D) - 1)) if nstar(D) >= 1 else F(0)) < D <= XM.A_INN / (2 * XM.B_DIS * XM.R1 ** nstar(D))
          for D in [F(k, 200) for k in range(80, 201)]))
# 读回：〔十八〕定理 18-4 的原子与完整证书；J₁₈ 经〔封二〕求值器
ETA, AI, BD = XM.ETA, XM.A_INN, XM.B_DIS
law = ('lin', -ETA * AI, ETA * BD, ((0, 'g1'), (1, 'g1')))
s_early = {(0, 'g1'): F(3, 4), (1, 'g1'): F(3, 4)}
atoms_e, col_e = XC.readback_atoms(law, s_early, {(0, 'g1'), (1, 'g1')}, EPS, {(0, 'g1'), (1, 'g1')},
                                   {(0, 'g1'): [F(0)], (1, 'g1'): [F(0)]}, lambda _: 1)
s_late = {(0, 'g1'): F(1, 64), (1, 'g1'): F(1, 64)}
atoms_l, col_l = XC.readback_atoms(law, s_late, {(0, 'g1'), (1, 'g1')}, EPS, {(0, 'g1'), (1, 'g1')},
                                   {(0, 'g1'): [F(0)], (1, 'g1'): [F(0)]}, lambda _: 1)
check('Q6f 〔十八〕定理 18-4 回读：早期再暴露（γ1 深 3/4）α3 写入的完整证书 %s、摘要「%s」；晚期（γ1 深 1/64）单设对照差 %s ≤ ε，摘要「%s」，HistCond_F = %s（不读作无历史条件）' %
      (atoms_e['完整证书'], col_e, dec(max(dk for (_i, dk, _c, _f) in atoms_l['对照明细'])), col_l, atoms_l['HistCond_F']),
      col_e == '有效差异已获证' and col_l == '函数支持已立' and atoms_l['HistCond_F'] == 1)
sites = ('g0', 'g1', 'a0', 'a1')
ev = [{'类': '脉冲', '名': 'US_g%d' % i, '拍': 0, '目标': 'g%d' % i, '量': F(3, 4), '界外': True, '发生': 1} for i in (0, 1)]
ev += [{'类': '脉冲', '名': 'US_a%d' % i, '拍': 0, '目标': 'a%d' % i, '量': F(1, 8), '界外': True, '发生': 1} for i in (0, 1)]
ev += [{'类': '过程', '名': 'W_a3_%d' % i, '拍': 1, '目标': 'a%d' % i, '载体在内': True, '律': 'linear', '读取': ('g0', 'g1'),
        '系数': ETA * BD, '偏置': -ETA * AI, 'cert_eff': 1, 'cert_read': 1} for i in (0, 1)]
ev += [{'类': '过程', '名': 'R_g%d' % i, '拍': 1, '目标': 'g%d' % i, '载体在内': True, '律': 'linear', '读取': ('g%d' % i,),
        '系数': -(1 - XM.R1), '偏置': F(0), 'cert_eff': 1, 'cert_read': 1} for i in (0, 1)]
links = [('state:g0:1', 'W_a3_0', 1), ('state:g1:1', 'W_a3_0', 1), ('state:g0:1', 'W_a3_1', 1), ('state:g1:1', 'W_a3_1', 1),
         ('state:g0:1', 'R_g0', 1), ('state:g1:1', 'R_g1', 1)]
jr = XC.j18_check(sites, (F(0),) * 4, (1,), EPS, sites, ev, links, closed_graph=True)
hist_w = [e['HistCond_F'] for e in jr['事件'] if e['id'].startswith('W_a3')]
~~~

check('Q6g J₁₈ 经〔封二〕求值器（field\_final）：训练拍界外脉冲形成 γ1、α3 位，再暴露拍 α3 写入（线性律读 γ1 两位）；D20「%s」，轨迹复现 %s，'

~~~text
# 定理 19-10：巩固窗 DAN 的三种读法与两支分报
cons = {'perm': BM.Consol('perm', kappa=F(1, 2), theta_c=F(1, 4)),
        'inh': BM.Consol('inh', kappa=F(1), u=F(-1), theta_c=F(1, 4)),
~~~

~~~python
check('Q6l 定理 19-10（四）兴奋性回返下的自限巩固：θ_c = 3/8 时只有训练深度 D ≤ %s 的迹应在窗内被巩固（深度 %s 皆巩固，更深者不巩固）' %

def retain(Cm, w0_, nwin, nrest):
    w_ = dict(w0_)
    for _ in range(nwin):
        w_ = Cm.step(w_, 'bg')
    for _ in range(nrest):
        w_ = Cm.step(w_, 'rest')
    return w_['a:0']

blk = {k_: (retain(Cm, w_tr, 2, 20), retain(BM.Consol(Cm.mode, kappa=Cm.kappa, u=Cm.u, theta_c=Cm.theta_c, block=True), w_tr, 2, 20))
       for k_, Cm in cons.items()}
check('Q6m 定理 19-10（五）窗内阻断 DAN：三读法的 22 拍后深度（不阻断对阻断）%s——三者都给「阻断损长时留存」，此读数不区分三读法（Ichinose 等 2015 的阻断结果与三者都相容）' %
      '；'.join('%s %s 对 %s' % (k_, dec(a_), dec(b_)) for k_, (a_, b_) in blk.items()),
      all(a_ > b_ for (a_, b_) in blk.values()))
# 延迟读数的检测条件在〔十七〕定义 2′ 下：延迟读数 = MBON-α3 驱动之降 n_A·(1 − ρ₂)^N·s_end，可检当且仅当 > τ_det；
# 以几何包络为尾部证据（定义 2′（丙）），读数的自留存（相对 τ_det）即首个不可检拍
rng = random.Random(19009)
r03_bad, r03_n, r03_lb = 0, 0, 0
for trial in range(120):
    rho2 = rng.choice([F(1, 64), F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 2), F(1)])
    tau = rng.choice([F(1, 100), F(1, 20), F(1, 8)])
    M = KM.gamma_alpha3(rho2=rho2)
    AD = BM.KCAdapter(M)
    H = AD.host()
    d = AD.zero()
    for s_ in [('A', (0, F(3, 2), 0, 0, 0))] * 3:
        _, d, _, _ = XC.step(H, d, None, s_)
    s_end = d[(0, 2)]
    readout = []
    for N in range(80):
        readout.append(d[(0, 2)] + d[(1, 2)])
        _, d, _, _ = XC.step(H, d, None, ('none', (0,) * 5))
    lb, stt = JC.retention(readout, tau, tail=('包络', readout[-1], 1 - rho2) if rho2 < 1 else ('包络', readout[-1], F(0)))
    closed = next((N for N in range(80) if 2 * (1 - rho2) ** N * s_end <= tau), None)
    r03_n += 1
    if closed is None:
        r03_lb += 1
        if not (stt == '下界' and lb == 80):
            r03_bad += 1
    elif stt != '完整' or lb != closed:
        r03_bad += 1
check('Q6o 延迟读数的检测条件（〔十七〕定义 2′ 的包络尾部证据）：120 个成员上延迟读数的可检拍数与闭式 min{N : n_A(1 − ρ₂)^N s_end ≤ τ_det} 不符 %d；'
      '记录末仍可检者 %d 个只记下界（不签完整）' % (r03_bad, r03_lb), r03_bad == 0 and r03_lb > 0)
# 平行对立迹应与同突触改写（〔十八〕定理 18-7（五）；定理 19-12）
yN = lambda n, dN, rN, dP, rP: 2 * (dN * rN ** n - dP * rP ** n)
eq = all(yN(n, F(1, 2), F(9, 10), F(1, 2), F(9, 10)) == 0 for n in range(30))
rec_ = [yN(n, F(1, 2), F(99, 100), F(1, 2), F(4, 5)) for n in range(30)]
Gsig = two_comp(F(1, 4), F(-1), F(1, 2), F(1, 2), F(1, 8), sgn2=F(1))
w_ = at(Gsig, {'a3:0': F(1, 2), 'a3:1': F(1, 2)})
w_ = Gsig.step(w_, 'A')
seq_a = []
for _ in range(30):
    w_ = Gsig.step(w_, 'rest')
    seq_a.append(w_['a3:0'])
check('Q6n 定理 19-12：平行对立层等率等深时读数恒为 0（隐）%s；不等率（对立层快衰）时消退后读数自发转正（第 29 拍 %s）；同一突触带符号削弱后长静置深度不回升（%s → %s）' %
      (eq, dec(rec_[-1]), dec(seq_a[0]), dec(seq_a[-1])), eq and rec_[0] == 0 and rec_[-1] > 0 and all(seq_a[i + 1] <= seq_a[i] for i in range(29)))
# ======================================================================
sec('Q7　步骤七　动力类型与核验：类层条件资格、跨宿载对照（〔十七〕定义 6′、7′；〔封十六〕词表；定理 19-H）')
rng = random.Random(19008)
cls_n, cls_ok, ctl_n, ctl_ok = 0, 0, 0, 0
for trial in range(60):
    eta2 = rng.choice([F(1, 8), F(1, 4)])
    U12 = rng.choice([F(-1, 2), F(-1)])
    rho1 = rng.choice([F(1, 16), F(1, 8)])
    Gm = two_comp(eta2, U12, F(5, 2), F(1, 4), rho1)
    hist_ = ['A+', 'rest', 'A', 'rest']
    S_ = JC.run(Gm, Gm.init, hist_)
    T_ = ['g1:0', 'g1:1', 'a3:0', 'a3:1']
    ts = JC.trace_candidates(Gm, hist_[:2], EPS)
    pw = JC.part(Gm, S_[2], T_, ['A'], EPS)
    stc, witc = JC.hdyn_cert(Gm, S_, hist_, T_, EPS)
    sw = JC.sed(Gm, [(S_[2], 'A')], T_, EPS)
    cls_n += 1
    if stc == '1' and pw is not None and sw is not None and sw[1] == 'W_a3' and set(ts[2]) == set(T_):
        cls_ok += 1
    G0 = two_comp(eta2, F(0), F(5, 2), F(1, 4), rho1)
    S0 = JC.run(G0, G0.init, hist_)
    st0_, wit0 = JC.hdyn_cert(G0, S0, hist_, T_, EPS)
    sw0 = JC.sed(G0, [(S0[2], 'A')], T_, EPS)
    ctl_n += 1
    if st0_ == '1' and wit0[2] == 'R_g1' and sw0 is None:
        ctl_ok += 1
check('Q7a 定理 19-H 的有限核验（A 栏，模型模式）：前件 N1—N5 成立的分级两区室成员 %d 个，TraceSet、Part、定义 6′ 证书与活动中介正分项台账（α3 写入，SED_W）俱成立 %d 个；'
      '对照 U₁₂ = 0（回返去除）%d 个中主判词仍为 1 而只由 γ1 弛豫直读承担、SED_W 为无者 %d 个' % (cls_n, cls_ok, ctl_n, ctl_ok),
      cls_ok == cls_n and ctl_ok == ctl_n)
# 定理 19-H 的前件不可省：N2（MBON_γ 落在下平台）或 N5（裕度）不成立时，α3 写入的活动中介见证不成立
n2_ok, n5_ok = 0, 0
for trial in range(20):
    eta2 = rng.choice([F(1, 8), F(1, 4)])
    Gp = two_comp(eta2, F(-1), F(5, 2), F(3), F(1, 8))          # θ_γ = 3 ≥ n_A：MBON_γ 恒为 0（下平台），N2 不成立
    Sp = JC.run(Gp, Gp.init, ['A+', 'rest', 'A', 'rest'])
    if JC.sed(Gp, [(Sp[2], 'A')], ['g1:0', 'g1:1', 'a3:0', 'a3:1'], EPS) is None:
        n2_ok += 1
    Gq = two_comp(F(1, 512), F(-1, 8), F(5, 2), F(1, 4), F(1, 8))  # η₂|U₁₂|Σd_γ ≤ ε：N5 不成立
    Sq = JC.run(Gq, Gq.init, ['A+', 'rest', 'A', 'rest'])
    if JC.sed(Gq, [(Sq[2], 'A')], ['g1:0', 'g1:1', 'a3:0', 'a3:1'], EPS) is None:
        n5_ok += 1
check('Q7a′ 定理 19-H 的前件不可省：N2 不成立（MBON_γ 恒在下平台）的 20 例、N5 不成立（裕度 ≤ ε）的 20 例中，α3 写入的活动中介正分项台账为无者 %d、%d' %
      (n2_ok, n5_ok), n2_ok == 20 and n5_ok == 20)
# 跨宿载对照：小脑 PF→PC LTD 经 PC ⊣ DCN ⊣ IO 的净兴奋回返（定理 19-4 的自限格），重复配对下攀缘纤维活动趋于平衡 η_p/η
Cb = BM.Cereb(o0=F(1), us=F(1, 8))
cf_dir = [Cb.act(at(Cb, {'pf:0': q, 'pf:1': q}), 'A')['CF'] for q in [F(k, 8) for k in range(0, 9)]]
w_ = dict(Cb.init)
cf_seq = []
for _ in range(300):
    cf_seq.append(Cb.act(w_, 'A+')['CF'])
    w_ = Cb.step(w_, 'A+')
cf_star = Cb.eta_p / Cb.eta
~~~

check('Q7b 小脑对照成员：非强化呈现下攀缘纤维（CF）驱动随 LTD 深度单调不增（%s → %s），为定理 19-4 的自限格（压抑型、净回返符号 +）；'  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;'重复配对 300 拍后 CF = %s，趋于平衡 η\_p/η = %s（自调节平衡），深度趋于 %s' % (dec(cf\_dir\[0\]), dec(cf\_dir\[-1\]), dec(cf\_seq\[-1\], 6), fs(cf\_star), dec(w\_\['pf:0'\], 6)),  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;all(cf\_dir\[i + 1\] \<= cf\_dir\[i\] for i in range(8)) and cf\_dir\[-1\] \< cf\_dir\[0\] and abs(cf\_seq\[-1\] - cf\_star) \< F(1, 10 \*\* 6))

sw\_cb = JC.sed(Cb, \[(w\_q, 'A+')\], \['pf:0', 'pf:1'\], EPS)

check('Q7c 小脑对照的台账（深度 1/4 处的配对拍）：活动中介正分项 SED\_W %s、自增强 SED⁺ %s——与果蝇 γ1pedc→α3 的跨区室续写（SED⁺ 成立）同属 𝒩₃F 而方向相反' %

~~~python
# ======================================================================
sec('Q8　二值类 𝒦_B 上的定理 19-5、19-6、19-7（k17_core 的一拍转移直接计算）')

def kb_set(M, cols_depth):
    st = M.init_state()
    for (i, j), s_ in cols_depth.items():
        st[0][(i, j)] = M.b[(i, j)] - s_
    return st

def kb_step(M, st, letter, inj=None):
    I = [F(0)] * M.n_out
    for j, v in (inj or {}).items():
        I[j] = F(v)
    st2, g, vt, ev = KC.step(M, st, letter, I)
    return st2, g

def kb_depth(M, st, i, j):
    return M.b[(i, j)] - st[0][(i, j)]

# 定理 19-5：抑制性自环（Φ_sr）上的使用依赖保护
Msr8 = KM.selfrenew()
hi8 = Msr8.hi
sstar = (2 * hi8 - Msr8.theta[0]) / 2
dm8 = Msr8.Am - Msr8.Ap
rho8 = Msr8.rho[(0, 0)]
L8 = min(hi8, (1 - rho8) * sstar + dm8)
KP = {m: (1 - rho8) ** m * L8 > sstar for m in range(1, 8)}
rng = random.Random(19801)
pbad8, pn8 = 0, 0
for trial in range(240):
    m = rng.choice([m_ for m_ in KP if KP[m_]])
    s0 = rng.choice([sstar + F(1, 64), F(3, 16), F(1, 4), F(3, 8)])
    st = kb_set(Msr8, {(0, 0): s0, (1, 0): s0})
    for _ in range(12):
        st, g = kb_step(Msr8, st, 'A', {1: rng.choice([0, 0, F(1, 2)])})
        pn8 += 1
        pbad8 += not (g[1] == 1 and kb_depth(Msr8, st, 0, 0) >= L8)
        for _ in range(rng.randrange(0, m + 1)):
            st, g = kb_step(Msr8, st, rng.choice(['none', 'B']))
        pbad8 += not kb_depth(Msr8, st, 0, 0) > sstar
def f_m(s_, m):
    return (1 - rho8) ** m * min(hi8, (1 - rho8) * s_ + dm8)
per = {}
for m in (2, 6):
    st = kb_set(Msr8, {(0, 0): F(1, 4), (1, 0): F(1, 4)})
    fires = []
    for cyc in range(30):
        st, g = kb_step(Msr8, st, 'A')
        fires.append(g[1])
        for _ in range(m):
            st, g = kb_step(Msr8, st, 'none')
    per[m] = (f_m(sstar, m) > sstar, all(fires), fires[-1])
check('Q8a 定理 19-5（Φ_sr：u < 0、c_A + u < θ_D ≤ c_A）：s* = %s、L = %s；K_P(m) 成立的 m = %s；%d 拍随机输入史（相邻 CS+ 间静置 ≤ m、注入任意非负）上「每一 CS+ 拍 D 发放且拍后 s ≥ L、下一 CS+ 前 s > s*」不符 %d；'
      '周期协议 m = 2：f_m(s*) > s* %s、30 周期恒续写 %s；m = 6：f_m(s*) > s* %s、末周期仍续写 %s' %
      (fs(sstar), fs(L8), [m for m in KP if KP[m]], pn8, pbad8, per[2][0], per[2][1], per[6][0], per[6][2]),
      pbad8 == 0 and per[2][0] and per[2][1] and not per[6][0] and per[6][2] == 0)
# 定理 19-6：兴奋性自环（Φ_α）上的压抑迹应：无保护、I 型自限、II 型总写入
Ma8 = KM.alpha1('I')
sst_a = (2 * Ma8.hi - Ma8.theta[0]) / 2
dm_a = Ma8.Am - Ma8.Ap
rho_a = Ma8.rho[(0, 0)]
slim = min(Ma8.hi, (1 - rho_a) * sst_a + dm_a)
rng = random.Random(19802)
b1, b2, b3, b4, n6 = 0, 0, 0, 0, 0
for trial in range(300):
    s0 = rng.choice([F(k, 32) for k in range(0, 17)])
    st = kb_set(Ma8, {(0, 0): s0, (1, 0): s0})
    st2, g = kb_step(Ma8, st, 'A')
    b1 += g[1] != 0 or kb_depth(Ma8, st2, 0, 0) != (1 - rho_a) * s0
    st3, g3 = kb_step(Ma8, st, 'A', {1: F(1, 2)})
    b2 += (g3[1] == 1) != (s0 <= sst_a)
    st4, g4 = kb_step(Ma8, st, 'A', {1: F(1)})
    b3 += g4[1] != 1
    n6 += 1
st = Ma8.init_state()
for k in range(200):
    st, g = kb_step(Ma8, st, rng.choice(['A', 'A', 'none']), {1: rng.choice([0, F(1, 2)])})
    b4 += kb_depth(Ma8, st, 0, 0) > slim
check('Q8b 定理 19-6（Φ_α：u > 0）：%d 个深度上（一）无注入 CS+ 拍 D 不发放、只弛豫，不符 %d；（二）I 型（J = 1/2）强化 CS+ 写入 ⟺ s ≤ s* = %s，不符 %d；（四）II 型（J = 1）总写入，不符 %d；200 拍随机训练史上 s ≤ s_lim = %s，不符 %d' %
      (n6, b1, fs(sst_a), b2, b3, fs(slim), b4), b1 == b2 == b3 == b4 == 0)
# 定理 19-7：跨区室门控——再暴露的 MVP2 发放 ⟺ k ≥ k*，PPL1-α3 门控 = 1 − g_M1
rng = random.Random(19803)
b7, n7, inf7 = 0, 0, 0
for r1 in (F(0), F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)):
    M7 = KM.gamma_alpha3(rho1=r1)
    s1s = (2 * M7.hi - M7.theta[0]) / 2
    for sig in (F(1, 16), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)):
        kst = next((k for k in range(60) if sig * (1 - r1) ** k <= s1s), None)
        inf7 += kst is None
        st = kb_set(M7, {(0, 0): sig, (1, 0): sig})
        for k in range(40):
            _, g = kb_step(M7, st, 'A')
            n7 += 1
            want = kst is not None and k >= kst
            b7 += (g[0] == 1) != want or g[3] != 1 - g[0]
            st, _ = kb_step(M7, st, 'none')
check('Q8c 定理 19-7（Φ_γα3^0：X1、X2）：s₁* = 1/8；5 个 ρ₁ × 3 个训练末深度 σ × 40 个静置拍数上「MVP2 对再暴露 CS+ 发放 ⟺ k ≥ k* := min{k : σ(1 − ρ₁)^k ≤ s₁*}」与「g_PPL1-α3 = 1 − g_MVP2」不符 %d（%d 拍；k* = ∞ 的组 %d，即 ρ₁ = 0 且 σ > s₁*）' %
      (b7, n7, inf7), b7 == 0 and inf7 > 0)

sec('Q9　定量预测的模型内核验（正文第十节各预测的定量形式；模型层〔核〕，不是实证）')
# Q1 剂量—反应的截距 = 翻转水平：分级带符号两区室成员上，再暴露写入对 MBON-γ1 读数为直线，其零点 m₀ 与延迟扫描的翻转水平 m* 相同
rng = random.Random(19101)
q1_bad, q1_n = 0, 0
for trial in range(200):
    eta2 = rng.choice([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)])
    U12 = rng.choice([F(-1, 2), F(-1), F(-2)])
    th1 = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 4)])
    beta2 = rng.choice([F(1), F(3, 2), F(2)])
    sgn2 = rng.choice([F(1, 4), F(1, 2)])
    rho1 = rng.choice([F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)])
    Gs2 = two_comp(eta2, U12, beta2, th1, rho1, sgn2=sgn2)
    pts = []
    for sq in (F(1, 2), F(3, 4)):
        wq = at(Gs2, {'g1:0': sq, 'g1:1': sq})
        yq = Gs2.act(wq, 'A')
        if yq['MBON_g1'] > 0 and yq['DAN_a3'] > 0:
            pts.append((yq['MBON_g1'], JC.total_comp(Gs2, wq, 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0')))
    if len(pts) < 2:
        continue
    (m1, W1), (m2, W2) = pts
    m0 = m1 - W1 * (m2 - m1) / (W2 - W1)
    m_star = (beta2 - sgn2) / (-U12)
    sigma = F(7, 8)
    kf = None
    for k in range(400):
        sk = sigma * (1 - rho1) ** k
        if JC.total_comp(Gs2, at(Gs2, {'g1:0': sk, 'g1:1': sk}), 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0') <= 0:
            kf = k
            break
    q1_n += 1
    if m0 != m_star:
        q1_bad += 1
    elif kf is not None and kf > 0:
        mb = lambda k: Gs2.act(at(Gs2, {'g1:0': sigma * (1 - rho1) ** k, 'g1:1': sigma * (1 - rho1) ** k}), 'A')['MBON_g1']
        if not (mb(kf - 1) < m0 <= mb(kf)):
            q1_bad += 1
check('Q9a 预测 P19-1/P19-2 的一致关系：%d 个分级带符号成员上，剂量—反应直线（写入对 MBON-γ1 读数）的零点 m₀ 与 m* = (β₂ − θ*)/|U₁₂| 相同，且延迟扫描的翻转拍正落在 MBON-γ1 越过 m₀ 之处（不符 %d）' %
      (q1_n, q1_bad), q1_bad == 0 and q1_n > 100)
# Q2 翻转延迟的平移律：γ1 初始深度加倍，翻转延迟增加 n₂ − 1 或 n₂ 拍，n₂ = min{n : (1 − ρ₁)^n ≤ 1/2}（连续时间极限 Δt = τ_γ1·ln 2）
rng = random.Random(19102)
q2_bad, q2_n = 0, 0
for trial in range(300):
    rho1 = rng.choice([F(1, 32), F(1, 16), F(1, 10), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)])
    s_m = rng.choice([F(1, 64), F(1, 32), F(1, 16), F(1, 10)])
    sigma = rng.choice([F(k, 64) for k in range(5, 33)])
    if not (sigma > s_m and 2 * sigma <= 1):
        continue
    kst = lambda sg: next(k for k in range(10 ** 4) if sg * (1 - rho1) ** k <= s_m)
    n2 = next(n for n in range(10 ** 4) if (1 - rho1) ** n <= F(1, 2))
    dk = kst(2 * sigma) - kst(sigma)
    q2_n += 1
    if not (n2 - 1 <= dk <= n2):
        q2_bad += 1
check('Q9b 翻转延迟的平移律（定理 19-7′ 的推论）：%d 例中 k*(2σ) − k*(σ) ∈ {n₂ − 1, n₂}（n₂ 为 γ1 迹应半衰的拍数）不符 %d' % (q2_n, q2_bad),
      q2_bad == 0 and q2_n > 100)
# Q3 早测干扰量：〔十八〕FLY 上，延迟 k 拍的一次 CS+ 早测使此后 α3 深度改变 η(−a + b·Σ_{K(A)} d_γ1(k))，符号在 n*(D) 处翻转
q3_bad, q3_n, q3_signs = 0, 0, set()
Hf = XM.make_fly()
for D in [F(k, 20) for k in range(8, 21)]:
    for k in range(0, 12):
        d0 = XM.fly_zero()
        for i in XM.ODOR['A']:
            d0[(i, 'g1')] = D
            d0[(i, 'a3')] = F(1, 2)
        _, dT, _ = XC.run(Hf, d0, None, ['R'] * k + ['A'] + ['R'] * 5)
        _, dN, _ = XC.run(Hf, d0, None, ['R'] * (k + 6))
        pred = XM.ETA * (-XM.A_INN + XM.B_DIS * 2 * D * XM.R1 ** k)
        q3_n += 1
        if dT[(0, 'a3')] - dN[(0, 'a3')] != pred or ((pred > 0) != (k < nstar(D))):
            q3_bad += 1
        q3_signs.add(pred > 0)
check('Q9c 早测干扰量（〔十八〕定理 18-3（四）的定量形式）：%d 组（γ1 深度 D × 早测延迟 k）中，有早测组与无早测组的 α3 末态之差 = η(−a + b·n_A·D·r₁^k) 且在 k = n*(D) 处由正转负（不符 %d）' %
      (q3_n, q3_bad), q3_bad == 0 and q3_signs == {True, False})
# Q4 巩固窗 DAN 之差的量值：训练组 − 假训练组 = −u·b·n_A·D（线性区）
q4_bad, q4_n = 0, 0
for k_, Cm in cons.items():
    for D in [F(k, 8) for k in range(1, 9)]:
        wt = at(Cm, {'a:0': D, 'a:1': D})
        dt_, dp_ = Cm.act(wt, 'bg')['DAN_w'], Cm.act(dict(Cm.init), 'bg')['DAN_w']
        raw_t = Cm.kappa + Cm.u * Cm.act(wt, 'bg')['MBON_a']
        raw_p = Cm.kappa + Cm.u * Cm.act(dict(Cm.init), 'bg')['MBON_a']
        if k_ != 'perm' and (raw_t < 0 or raw_p < 0):
            continue
        q4_n += 1
        want = F(0) if k_ == 'perm' else -Cm.u * Cm.bgv * 2 * D
        if dt_ - dp_ != want:
            q4_bad += 1
check('Q9d 巩固窗 DAN 之差的量值（定理 19-10（三）的定量形式）：%d 例中「训练组 − 假训练组」= −u·b·n_A·D（放行型为 0）不符 %d——与训练深度成正比、与背景 KC 活动成正比' %
      (q4_n, q4_bad), q4_bad == 0 and q4_n >= 16)
# Q5 训练强度—长时记忆的非单调（兴奋性回返读法下）：窗长 L 拍、静置 N 拍后的深度
#     LTM(D) = D·ρ₀^{t+1}·ρ₁^{L−1−t+N}（t = min{t < L : D·ρ₀^t ≤ D_c}），窗内无此 t 时 D·ρ₀^{L+N}；D_c = (κ + u·b·N_KC − θ_c)/(u·b·n_A)
Dc_formula = (exc_hi.kappa + exc_hi.u * exc_hi.bgv * 4 - exc_hi.theta_c) / (exc_hi.u * exc_hi.bgv * 2)
q5_bad, ltm = 0, {}
for L in (1, 2, 3):
    for D in [F(k, 8) for k in range(1, 9)]:
        got = retain(exc_hi, at(exc_hi, {'a:0': D, 'a:1': D}), L, 20)
        t = next((t_ for t_ in range(L) if D * exc_hi.rho0 ** t_ <= Dc_formula), None)
        want = D * exc_hi.rho0 ** (L + 20) if t is None else D * exc_hi.rho0 ** (t + 1) * exc_hi.rho1 ** (L - 1 - t + 20)
        ltm[(L, D)] = got
        if got != want:
            q5_bad += 1
nonmono = ltm[(1, F(5, 8))] < ltm[(1, F(1, 2))] and ltm[(2, F(5, 8))] < ltm[(2, F(1, 2))]
~~~

check('Q9e 训练强度—长时记忆非单调（定理 19-10（四）的定量形式）：D\_c = (κ + u·b·N − θ\_c)/(u·b·n\_A) = %s；窗长 L = 1、2、3 拍下 24 例与闭式 LTM(D) = D·ρ₀^{t+1}·ρ₁^{L−1−t+N} 不符 %d；'

~~~text
      'L = 1 时 LTM(1/2)/LTM(5/8) = %s，L = 2 时 = %s——越过 D_c 即跃降（深迹应先退浅到 D_c 以下再被巩固；锯齿个数受标记门与 D ≤ 1 截断，不随窗长逐拍增加，见 Q11l）' %
      (fs(Dc_formula), q5_bad, dec(ltm[(1, F(1, 2))] / ltm[(1, F(5, 8))], 1), dec(ltm[(2, F(1, 2))] / ltm[(2, F(5, 8))], 2)),
      q5_bad == 0 and nonmono and Dc_formula == F(1, 2))
# Q6 α3 压抑的气味特异与交叠分级：突触迹应读法下测试气味的 MBON-α3 驱动之降 = o·D（o 为与 CS+ 共享的 KC 数）；
#     MBON 内在兴奋性读法（竞争理论）下降量与 o 无关
Gov = BM.Graded3F(['a3'], 8, {'A': (0, 1, 2, 3), 'X0': (4, 5, 6, 7), 'X1': (3, 4, 5, 6), 'X2': (2, 3, 4, 5), 'X3': (1, 2, 3, 4), 'X4': (0, 1, 2, 3)},
                  {'a3': F(0)}, {'a3': F(0)}, {}, {'a3': F(1)}, {'a3': F(1, 4)}, {'a3': F(0)}, name='Graded3F_交叠')
D6 = F(3, 5)
wD = at(Gov, {'a3:%d' % i: D6 for i in (0, 1, 2, 3)})
red = [Gov.act(Gov.init, 'X%d' % o)['MBON_a3'] - Gov.act(wD, 'X%d' % o)['MBON_a3'] for o in range(5)]
intrinsic = [F(4) * D6 / 4 * 4 for o in range(5)]  # 内在兴奋性读法：对一切气味按同一增益降低（与 o 无关）
~~~

check('Q9f 交叠分级（区分「突触迹应」与「MBON 内在兴奋性」）：测试气味与 CS+ 共享 o = 0..4 个 KC 时 MBON-α3 驱动之降为 %s（= o·D，线性）；内在兴奋性读法预言各 o 相同' %

~~~json
      [fs(v) for v in red], red == [o * D6 for o in range(5)] and len(set(intrinsic)) == 1)
# Q7 α3 自身的兴奋性回返（所附模型 MBON-α3 → PPL1-α3 为正）⇒ α3 自限：再暴露写入随 α3 已有深度以斜率 −η₂·u₂₂·n_A 下降
q7_bad, q7_n = 0, 0
for u22 in (F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)):
    for eta2 in (F(1, 8), F(1, 4)):
        Gm = BM.Graded3F(['g1', 'a3'], 4, {'A': (0, 1), 'B': (2, 3)}, {'g1': F(0), 'a3': F(0)}, {'g1': F(0), 'a3': F(1)},
                         {('g1', 'a3'): F(-1), ('a3', 'a3'): u22}, {'g1': F(1)}, {'g1': F(1, 2), 'a3': eta2}, {'g1': F(1, 8), 'a3': F(0)})
        for sa, sb in ((F(0), F(1, 4)), (F(1, 4), F(1, 2))):
            wa_ = at(Gm, {'g1:0': F(1, 2), 'g1:1': F(1, 2), 'a3:0': sa, 'a3:1': sa})
            wb_ = at(Gm, {'g1:0': F(1, 2), 'g1:1': F(1, 2), 'a3:0': sb, 'a3:1': sb})
            ca_ = JC.total_comp(Gm, wa_, 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0')
            cb_ = JC.total_comp(Gm, wb_, 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0')
            q7_n += 1
            if Gm.act(wb_, 'A')['DAN_a3'] > 0 and (cb_ - ca_) != -eta2 * u22 * 2 * (sb - sa):
                q7_bad += 1
check('Q9g α3 自限（定理 19-4 用于所附模型的 MBON-α3 → PPL1-α3 正权）：%d 例中再暴露写入对 α3 已有深度的斜率 = −η₂·u₂₂·n_A（不符 %d）——同一 γ1 压抑下，α3 记忆越深，早期再暴露的加强越小' %
      (q7_n, q7_bad), q7_bad == 0)
# Q8 去抑制续写的条件：同区室抑制性回返下，非强化再暴露使迹应净加深 ⟺ η·DAN(s) > ρ·s（且未饱和）；DAN(s) − DAN(0) = |u|·n_A·s（线性区）
rng = random.Random(19108)
q8_bad, q8_n, q8_yes = 0, 0, 0
for trial in range(300):
    u = rng.choice([F(-1, 4), F(-1, 2), F(-1)])
    be = rng.choice([F(1), F(3, 2), F(2), F(5, 2)])
    eta = rng.choice([F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)])
    rho = rng.choice([F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)])
    Gm = BM.Graded3F(['g1'], 4, {'A': (0, 1)}, {'g1': F(0)}, {'g1': be}, {('g1', 'g1'): u}, {'g1': F(1)}, {'g1': eta}, {'g1': rho})
    sq = rng.choice([F(k, 16) for k in range(1, 16)])
    wq = at(Gm, {'g1:0': sq, 'g1:1': sq})
    dan = Gm.act(wq, 'A')['DAN_g1']
    deeper = Gm.step(wq, 'A')['g1:0'] > sq
    q8_n += 1
    q8_yes += deeper
    lin_ok = (be + u * 2 < 0) or (dan - Gm.act(Gm.init, 'A')['DAN_g1'] == -u * 2 * sq)
    if deeper != (eta * dan > rho * sq) or not lin_ok:
        q8_bad += 1
check('Q9h 去抑制续写的条件（定理 19-5 的分级形式）：%d 例中「非强化再暴露使压抑净加深」⟺ η·DAN(s) > ρ·s 不符 %d（续写 %d 例）；DAN 的去抑制增量 = |u|·n_A·s' %
      (q8_n, q8_bad, q8_yes), q8_bad == 0 and 0 < q8_yes < q8_n)
# ======================================================================
sec('Q10　边界例（一）：资格诊断 19-H、区分定理 19-D、巩固 19-10、跨区室门控 19-7′ 与局部限定')
# ---- 定理 19-H：（一）HDyn 有效差异、（二）TraceSet 的元素证书与组织分量、（三）SED_W 另须取向 W > ε、（四）U = 0 另一对照域
def ex_signed(beta_a=F(5, 2)):
    return BM.Graded3F(['g', 'a'], 1, {'A': (0,)}, {'g': 0, 'a': 0}, {'g': 0, 'a': 1}, {('g', 'a'): F(-1, 2)}, {'g': 1, 'a': beta_a},
                       {'g': F(1, 2), 'a': F(1, 4)}, {'g': 0, 'a': 0}, signed={'a': 1})

Hs = ex_signed()
sg_ = ['A+', 'rest', 'A']
Ss = JC.run(Hs, Hs.init, sg_)
act_, ctl_ = Ss[2], dict(Ss[2], **{'g:0': F(0)})
W_act = JC.comp_of(Hs, act_, Hs.act(act_, 'A'), 'A', 'W_a', 'a:0')
br_ = JC.branches(Hs, act_, ctl_, 'A', 'W_a', 'a:0')
cert_ = JC.hdyn_cert(Hs, Ss, sg_, Hs.X, EPS)[0]
sed_ = JC.sed(Hs, [(act_, 'A')], Hs.X, EPS)

def h19_parts(host, states, sigs, k, T_gamma, x, eps):
    """定理 19-H 的四部分判值。返回 dict：
    一：拍 k 上 α 写入的定义 6′ 有效差异（对照只改 T_gamma 回基线；同拍分项 |W| > ε；两支）；
    一′：目标 x 在拍 k 的拍前态已越 ε（已留之位，N1′）；
    三：SED_W 另须取向 W > ε 且 A 支有效。"""
    w = states[k]
    s = sigs[k]
    y = host.act(w, s)
    W = JC.comp_of(host, w, y, s, 'W_a', x) if any(p == 'W_a' for (p, xx, c) in host.comps(w, y, s) if xx == x) else ZERO_
    w2 = dict(w, **{g: F(0) for g in T_gamma})
    A_, D_, tot_ = JC.branches(host, w, w2, s, 'W_a', x)
    one = abs(W) > eps and abs(tot_) > eps and all(w[g] > eps for g in T_gamma)
    return {'W': W, 'A': A_, 'D': D_, 'tot': tot_, '一': one, '一′': w[x] > eps, '三': one and W > eps and abs(A_) > eps}

ZERO_ = F(0)
pr = h19_parts(Hs, Ss, sg_, 2, ['g:0'], 'a:0', EPS)
~~~

check('Q10a 定理 19-H：例 X2（带符号、负写入）：W = %s、对照 W = %s、A = %s、D = %s；判值：（一）有效差异 %s，（三）SED\_W %s（取向 W \< 0），〔十七〕程序 hdyn\_cert = %s、sed = %s——两款拆开后一致' %

~~~text
      (fs(W_act), fs(JC.comp_of(Hs, ctl_, Hs.act(ctl_, 'A'), 'A', 'W_a', 'a:0')), fs(br_[0]), fs(br_[1]), pr['一'], pr['三'], cert_, sed_),
      pr['一'] and not pr['三'] and cert_ == '1' and sed_ is None and W_act == F(-1, 16) and br_ == (F(-1, 16), 0, F(-1, 16)))
# 带符号支的随机覆盖：（一）恒成立；（三）当且仅当 W > ε
rng = random.Random(19201)
b10b_bad, b10b_pos, b10b_neg, b10b_sub, b10b_n1 = 0, 0, 0, 0, 0
for trial in range(200):
    eta2 = rng.choice([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)])
    U12 = rng.choice([F(-1, 2), F(-1)])
    sgn2 = rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(1), F(3, 2), F(2)])
    beta2 = rng.choice([F(2), F(5, 2), F(3)])
    Gm = two_comp(eta2, U12, beta2, F(1, 4), F(1, 8), sgn2=sgn2)
    S_ = JC.run(Gm, Gm.init, ['A+', 'rest', 'A', 'rest'])
    y_ = Gm.act(S_[2], 'A')
    W = JC.comp_of(Gm, S_[2], y_, 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0')
    w2 = dict(S_[2], **{'g1:0': F(0), 'g1:1': F(0)})
    A_, D_, tot_ = JC.branches(Gm, S_[2], w2, 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0')
    lin = Gm.act(S_[2], 'A')['DAN_a3'] > 0 and Gm.act(w2, 'A')['DAN_a3'] > 0 and Gm.act(w2, 'A')['MBON_g1'] > 0
    if not lin:
        continue
    one = abs(W) > EPS and abs(tot_) > EPS
    sw = JC.sed(Gm, [(S_[2], 'A')], ['g1:0', 'g1:1', 'a3:0', 'a3:1'], EPS)
    if (sw is not None) != (one and W > EPS) or D_ != 0 or tot_ != eta2 * U12 * (S_[2]['g1:0'] + S_[2]['g1:1']):
        b10b_bad += 1
    b10b_pos += one and W > EPS
    b10b_neg += one and W < -EPS
    b10b_sub += not one
    b10b_n1 += one and S_[2]['a3:0'] <= EPS
check('Q10b 定理 19-H 带符号支覆盖（Q7a 的带符号补充）：线性区成员上 Δ_tot = η₂U₁₂Σd_γ、D 支恒为 0；（一）当且仅当 |W| > ε（正写入 %d、负写入 %d、同拍分项不越 ε %d）；SED_W 当且仅当（一）且 W > ε（不符 %d）；'
      '（一）成立而 α 拍前未越 ε（N1′ 不成立，19-H 不签）%d 例' % (b10b_pos, b10b_neg, b10b_sub, b10b_bad, b10b_n1),
      b10b_bad == 0 and b10b_pos > 0 and b10b_neg > 0)
# （二）同一 T 的元素证书（自留存下界 ≥ τ_W、K2 相对 CS+ 探针、K3 相对配对写入）、组织分量（联合设定精确枚举）、载体在 B
Gm = two_comp(F(1, 4), F(-1), F(5, 2), F(1, 4), F(1, 8))
T_ = ['g1:0', 'g1:1', 'a3:0', 'a3:1']
wT_ = JC.run(Gm, Gm.init, ['A+', 'rest'])[-1]
tauW = 6
cert2 = {}
for x in T_:
    w_ = dict(wT_)
    seq_ = []
    for t in range(40):
        seq_.append(w_[x] - Gm.ground[x])
        w_ = Gm.step(w_, 'rest')
    lb_, st_ = JC.retention(seq_, EPS)
    k2 = not JC.order_conflicts([(q, abs(Gm.act(at(Gm, {x: q}), 'A')['MBON_' + x.split(':')[0]] - Gm.act(Gm.init, 'A')['MBON_' + x.split(':')[0]]))
                                   for q in [F(k, 8) for k in range(9)]])
    k3 = all(Gm.step(at(Gm, {x: q}), 'A+')[x] >= q for q in [F(k, 8) for k in range(9)])
    cert2[x] = (lb_ >= tauW, k2, k3)
hyT, cpT = JC.org_graph_exact(Gm, wT_, T_, ['A'], EPS)
carrier = set(T_) <= set(Gm.X)
~~~

check('Q10c 定理 19-H（二）元素证书逐位单列（τ\_W = %d 拍）：%s；组织分量（CS+ 探针、联合设定精确枚举）%s；载体在 B（T ⊆ 申报结构位）%s——不以形成或越阈代替' %

~~~text
      (tauW, {x: v for x, v in cert2.items()}, cpT, carrier),
      all(all(v) for v in cert2.values()) and any(set(T_) <= set(c) for c in cpT) and carrier)
# 例 X3（α 已退尽）：训练后 α = 1/2、再暴露前 0、再暴露后 3/16；hdyn_cert 仍判 1，但前件 N1′（目标为该拍已留之位）不成立，19-H 不签

                 {'g': F(1, 2), 'a': F(1, 4)}, {'g': 0, 'a': 1})
Sv = JC.run(Hv, Hv.init, sg_)
pv = h19_parts(Hv, Sv, sg_, 2, ['g:0'], 'a:0', EPS)
~~~

check('Q10e 定理 19-H（四）U = 0 对照域：ρ\_γ = 1/8 时证书 %s（见证过程 %s，γ 弛豫直读）；ρ\_γ = 0 时训练后窗内证书「%s」——不签 Fld，记 ⊥（不记 0）' %

~~~text
      (c0r[0], c0r[1][2] if c0r[0] == '1' else None, c0n[0]),
      c0r[0] == '1' and c0r[1][2] == 'R_g1' and c0n[0] != '1')
# ---- 定理 19-D（区分式的承重定理）：放行型与回返型候选的两臂差
rng = random.Random(19202)
d_bad, d_n = 0, 0
for trial in range(400):
    N = rng.choice([4, 6, 8])
    b = [rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)]) for _ in range(N)]
    if sum(b) == 0:
        continue
    dS = [rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 8)]) for _ in range(N)]
    dT = [min(F(1), x + rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)])) for x in dS]
    db = sum((bi * (t - s_) for bi, t, s_ in zip(b, dT, dS)), F(0))
    if db <= 0:
        continue
    kappa = rng.choice([F(1), F(2), F(3)])
    for u in (F(-1, 2), F(-1), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)):
        Rc = BM.WindowCand('R', kappa, u, b, F(0))
        Pc = BM.WindowCand('P', kappa, 0, b, F(0))
        if not (Rc.dan_input(dT) > 0 and Rc.dan_input(dS) > 0):
            continue
        d_n += 1
        if Pc.delta(dT, dS) != 0 or Rc.delta(dT, dS) != -u * db:
            d_bad += 1
check('Q10f 定理 19-D（一）精确两臂差：%d 例（前件 D1 训练在背景激活位上增加压抑 Δb > 0、D2 u ≠ 0、D3 两臂复合读出在线性区）中，放行型 Δ = 0、回返型 Δ = −u·Δb（符号 −sgn u）不符 %d' %
      (d_n, d_bad), d_bad == 0 and d_n > 200)
rng = random.Random(19203)
res_bad, res_n, gap_hits = 0, 0, 0
for trial in range(300):
    e = rng.choice([F(1, 100), F(1, 40), F(1, 20)])
    u_min, g = rng.choice([F(1, 4), F(1, 2)]), rng.choice([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)])
    m = u_min * g
    if not m > 2 * e:
        continue
    truth = rng.choice(['P', 'R+', 'R-'])
    u = {'P': F(0), 'R+': u_min * rng.choice([1, 2]), 'R-': -u_min * rng.choice([1, 2])}[truth]
    true_delta = -u * g
    noise = e * F(rng.randrange(-10, 11), 10)
    obs = true_delta + noise
    call = 'P' if abs(obs) <= e else ('R-' if obs >= m - e else ('R+' if obs <= -(m - e) else '不可判'))
    res_n += 1
    if call != truth:
        res_bad += 1
check('Q10g 定理 19-D（二）分辨率版本：m = u_min·g > 2e 时三输出集 {|x| ≤ e}、{x ≥ m − e}、{x ≤ −(m − e)} 互不相交；%d 次带误差（|δ| ≤ e）的判读中错判 %d、落入不可判区 0' %
      (res_n, res_bad), res_bad == 0 and res_n > 100)
plat = BM.WindowCand('R', 0, -1, (F(1, 4),) * 4, 0)
dT_, dS_ = (F(1, 2), F(1, 2), 0, 0), (0, 0, 0, 0)
P0 = BM.WindowCand('P', 0, 0, (F(1, 4),) * 4, 0)
nob = BM.WindowCand('R', 1, -1, (0, 0, 0, 0), 0)
u0 = BM.WindowCand('R', 1, 0, (F(1, 4),) * 4, 0)
m_small = F(1, 40) * F(1, 2)
~~~

~~~text
      (fs(plat.delta(dT_, dS_)), fs(P0.delta(dT_, dS_)), fs(nob.delta(dT_, dS_)), fs(u0.delta(dT_, dS_))),
      plat.delta(dT_, dS_) == 0 == P0.delta(dT_, dS_) and nob.delta(dT_, dS_) == 0 and u0.delta(dT_, dS_) == 0 and not m_small > 2 * F(1, 40))
# 19-10（四）：例 X5，兴奋性（κ = 1、u = 1/2、b = 1/4、θ_c = 1/2）D = 1 仍 CONS；D_c 存在 ⟺ κ + u·b·(N − n_A) < θ_c ≤ κ + u·b·N（D = 0 巩固而 D = 1 不巩固）
ex_rev = BM.Consol('exc', kappa=F(1), u=F(1, 2), bg=F(1, 4), theta_c=F(1, 2))
st1 = {x: F(x.startswith('a:')) for x in ex_rev.X}
cons_d1 = ex_rev.step(st1, 'bg')['c:0'] == 1
rng = random.Random(19204)
dc_bad = 0
for trial in range(200):
    kap = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)])
    uu = rng.choice([F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)])
    th = rng.choice([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(3, 8), F(1, 2), F(3, 4)])
    Cm = BM.Consol('exc', kappa=kap, u=uu, bg=F(1, 4), theta_c=th)
    fails = [D for D in [F(k, 16) for k in range(1, 17)] if JC.total_comp(Cm, at(Cm, {'a:0': D, 'a:1': D}), 'bg', 'CONS', 'c:0') == 0]
    pred_exists = kap + uu * F(1, 4) * 2 < th <= kap + uu * F(1, 4) * 4
    if bool(fails) != (kap + uu * F(1, 4) * 2 < th) or (pred_exists and (F(1) not in fails)):
        dc_bad += 1
check('Q10i 定理 19-10（四）：例 X5（兴奋性） D = 1 时 DAN_w = %s ≥ θ_c，CONS 仍置位 %s；200 例中「存在不被巩固的深度」⟺ κ + u·b·(N − n_A) < θ_c 不符 %d——单调下降不推出跨阈' %
      (fs(ex_rev.act(st1, 'bg')['DAN_w']), cons_d1, dc_bad), cons_d1 and dc_bad == 0)
th0 = BM.Consol('perm', kappa=F(1, 2), theta_c=F(0), block=True)
w_tr0 = at(th0, {'a:0': F(3, 4), 'a:1': F(3, 4)})
cons_blocked = JC.total_comp(th0, w_tr0, 'bg', 'CONS', 'c:0')
~~~

~~~text
# ---- 19-7′：上端夹限时 W > 0 而不增深；k₊（首次不再正写入）与 k₋（首次反向写入）分列；渐近零点与有尾部证明的 ∞
Hp = BM.Graded3F(['g', 'a'], 1, {'A': (0,)}, {'g': 0, 'a': 0}, {'g': 0, 'a': 1}, {('g', 'a'): F(-1, 2)}, {'g': 1, 'a': 1},
                 {'g': F(1, 2), 'a': F(1, 4)}, {'g': 0, 'a': 0}, signed={'a': F(1, 2)})

Wsat = JC.comp_of(Hp, satw, Hp.act(satw, 'A'), 'A', 'W_a', 'a:0')
~~~

check('Q10k 定理 19-7′（三）上端夹限（例 X6）：W = %s \> 0 而 α 后态 %s → %s（无增深）——「写入分项发生」不推出「实际增深」' %

~~~python
      (fs(Wsat), fs(satw['a:0']), fs(Hp.step(satw, 'A')['a:0'])), Wsat == F(1, 16) and Hp.step(satw, 'A')['a:0'] == 1)

def kplus_kminus(Gs2, sigma, rho1, K=300):
    kp = km = None
    for k in range(K):
        sk = sigma * (1 - rho1) ** k
        W = JC.total_comp(Gs2, at(Gs2, {'g1:0': sk, 'g1:1': sk}), 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0')
        if kp is None and W <= 0:
            kp = k
        if km is None and W < 0:
            km = k
    return kp, km

# 首拍恰为零：取 θ* 使某拍 W = 0
Gz = two_comp(F(1, 4), F(-1), F(1), F(0), F(1, 2), sgn2=F(1, 2))  # m* = 1/2；MBON = 2(1 − s)
kp_z, km_z = kplus_kminus(Gz, F(3, 4), F(1, 2))
# 渐近零点：m* = MBON(0) = n_A − θ，s_m = 0，W > 0 恒成立而趋于 0（尾部证明：σ(1 − ρ)^k > 0）
Ga = two_comp(F(1, 4), F(-1), F(5, 2), F(0), F(1, 2), sgn2=F(1, 2))  # m* = 2 = MBON(0)
kp_a, km_a = kplus_kminus(Ga, F(3, 4), F(1, 2), 60)
~~~

~~~text
# P19-1 的匹配无再暴露臂：训练强度改变 α 终态；再暴露臂与匹配臂之差 = 同拍写入 W（未夹限、α 无弛豫）
p1_bad = 0
for nA in (1, 2, 3):
    Gm = two_comp(F(1, 16), F(-1), F(5, 2), F(1, 4), F(1, 8))
    pre = JC.run(Gm, Gm.init, ['A+'] * nA + ['rest'])[-1]
    Wp = JC.total_comp(Gm, pre, 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0')
    re_ = JC.run(Gm, pre, ['A'] + ['rest'] * 6)[-1]
    no_ = JC.run(Gm, pre, ['rest'] * 7)[-1]
    if re_['a3:0'] - no_['a3:0'] != Wp:
        p1_bad += 1
~~~

~~~text
# Q11c 0 < |u| < u_min 仍是回返：Δ ≠ 0，但可落在 O_P 或未判区（超出保证区，不是 P）

      weak, all(F(x[1]) != 0 for x in weak) and any(x[2] == 'P' for x in weak) and any(x[2] == '未判' for x in weak))
# Q11d 共同窗统计量：窗内各格深度按共同弛豫变化，非负权重，P 给 0，R_u 给 −u·Σ w_j Δb_j，|Δ̄| ≥ u_min·g
rng = random.Random(19301)
wbad, wn = 0, 0
for trial in range(300):
    N_ = rng.choice([2, 3, 4])
    bb = tuple(rng.choice([F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)]) for _ in range(N_))
    dS_ = tuple(rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 8)]) for _ in range(N_))
    dT_ = tuple(min(F(1), s_ + rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)])) for s_ in dS_)
    rr = rng.choice([F(1), F(7, 8), F(3, 4)])
    J = rng.choice([1, 2, 3])
    wts = [F(rng.randrange(1, 4)) for _ in range(J)]
    wts = [x / sum(wts) for x in wts]
    kap = rng.choice([F(1), F(2), F(3)])
    thM = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 8)])
    for kind, u_ in [('P', 0), ('R', rng.choice([F(-1), F(-1, 2), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)]))]:
        C_ = BM.WindowCand(kind, kap, u_, bb, thM)
        gridT = [tuple(d * rr ** j for d in dT_) for j in range(J)]
        gridS = [tuple(d * rr ** j for d in dS_) for j in range(J)]
        lin = all(C_.mbon(d) > 0 and C_.dan_input(d) > 0 for d in gridT + gridS) if kind == 'R' else True
        dbj = [sum(bi * (t - s_) for bi, t, s_ in zip(bb, gT, gS)) for gT, gS in zip(gridT, gridS)]
        if not lin or min(dbj) <= 0:
            continue
        wn += 1
        bar = sum(wj * C_.delta(gT, gS) for wj, gT, gS in zip(wts, gridT, gridS))
        want = 0 if kind == 'P' else -u_ * sum(wj * x for wj, x in zip(wts, dbj))
        if bar != want or (kind == 'R' and abs(bar) < abs(u_) * min(dbj)):
            wbad += 1
check('Q11d 共同窗统计量（事前选定时间格与非负权重、两臂共用、窗内共同弛豫）：%d 例中 P 给 Δ̄ = 0、R_u 给 Δ̄ = −u·Σ w_j Δb_j 且 |Δ̄| ≥ |u|·min_j Δb_j，不符 %d' % (wn, wbad),
      wbad == 0 and wn > 100)
# Q11e 「无误判」只在误差界成立且候选集覆盖时；m ≤ 2e 只是不保证分离，不证明必同读

for dl in resp:
    for k in range(-4, 5):
        obs = dl + e_b * F(k, 4)
        z = 'P' if abs(obs) <= e_b else ('R₋' if obs >= m_b - e_b else ('R₊' if obs <= -(m_b - e_b) else '未判'))
        mis_in.append(z)
true_z = ['P'] * 9 + ['R₋'] * 9 + ['R₊'] * 9
viol = resp[1] - 7 * e_b
z_viol = 'P' if abs(viol) <= e_b else ('R₋' if viol >= m_b - e_b else '未判')
big_e = F(1, 8)
sep_mem = BM.WindowCand('R', 1, -1, b2_, F(1, 4)).delta(dT2, dS2)
~~~

check('Q11e 误差界与覆盖：界内（\|δ\| ≤ e）27 次判读错判 %d；误差超界（δ = −7e）时 R₋ 读作「%s」——「无误判」只在界成立时；e = 1/8 使 m ≤ 2e（不保证分离），而该冻结 R₋ 成员 Δ = %s 仍与 P 的 0 不同——m ≤ 2e 不证明必同读' %

~~~python
      (sum(a != b for a, b in zip(mis_in, true_z)), z_viol, fs(sep_mem)),

# ---- 零点、首拍与迟读
def mk_host(alpha_rho=0):
    return BM.Graded3F(['g', 'a'], 1, {'A': (0,)}, {'g': 0, 'a': 0}, {'g': 0, 'a': 1}, {('g', 'a'): -1},
                       {'g': 1, 'a': 1}, {'g': F(1, 2), 'a': F(1, 2)}, {'g': F(1, 2), 'a': alpha_rho}, signed={'a': F(1, 2)}, bg=1)
hp_ = mk_host()
s0_ = {'g:0': F(3, 4), 'a:0': F(1, 2)}
rows_ = []
st_ = dict(s0_)
for k in range(2):
    rows_.append((hp_.act(st_, 'A')['MBON_g'], JC.comp_of(hp_, st_, hp_.act(st_, 'A'), 'A', 'W_a', 'a:0')))
    st_ = hp_.step(st_, 'rest')
mstar_ = F(1, 2)
kp_ = next(k for k, (m_, W_) in enumerate(rows_) if W_ <= 0)
km_ = next(k for k, (m_, W_) in enumerate(rows_) if W_ < 0)
iv_ = (rows_[kp_ - 1][0], rows_[kp_][0])
# 随机成员：m(k₊−1) < m* ≤ m(k₊)、m(k₋−1) ≤ m* < m(k₋)
rng = random.Random(19302)
ibad, in_ = 0, 0
for trial in range(600):
    eta2 = rng.choice([F(1, 8), F(1, 4)])
    U12 = rng.choice([F(-1, 2), F(-1)])
    th1 = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 4)])
    beta2 = rng.choice([F(2), F(5, 2), F(3)])
    rho1 = rng.choice([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)])
    sg2 = rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(1)])
    Gm = two_comp(eta2, U12, beta2, th1, rho1, sgn2=sg2)
    sig = rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(3, 4), F(1)])
    wk_ = at(Gm, {'g1:0': sig, 'g1:1': sig})
    ms, Ws = [], []
    for k in range(40):
        ms.append(Gm.act(wk_, 'A')['MBON_g1'])
        Ws.append(JC.total_comp(Gm, wk_, 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0'))
        wk_ = Gm.step(wk_, 'rest')
    mst = (beta2 - sg2) / (-U12)
    if not all(beta2 + U12 * m_ > 0 for m_ in ms):
        continue
    kp = next((k for k in range(40) if Ws[k] <= 0), None)
    km = next((k for k in range(40) if Ws[k] < 0), None)
    if kp is None or kp == 0:
        continue
    in_ += 1
    ok = ms[kp - 1] < mst <= ms[kp] and (km is None or (km >= kp and ms[km - 1] <= mst < ms[km]))
    ibad += not ok
check('Q11f P19-2 的夹逼区间：例 X11 m* = 1/2，(m, W) = (%s, %s)、(%s, %s)，k₊ = k₋ = %d，只得 %s < m* < %s（零点未采到，不签 m₀ = m(t_flip)）；%d 个随机成员上 m(k₊−1) < m* ≤ m(k₊) 与 m(k₋−1) ≤ m* < m(k₋) 不符 %d' %
      (fs(rows_[0][0]), fs(rows_[0][1]), fs(rows_[1][0]), fs(rows_[1][1]), kp_, fs(iv_[0]), fs(iv_[1]), in_, ibad),
      rows_ == [(F(1, 4), F(1, 8)), (F(5, 8), F(-1, 16))] and kp_ == km_ == 1 and iv_[0] < mstar_ < iv_[1] and all(m_ != mstar_ for m_, _ in rows_) and ibad == 0 and in_ > 50)
# Q11g 平移律的取整形式：k₊(σ) = ⌈ln(σ/s_m)/ln(1/(1 − ρ))⌉，k₊(cσ) − k₊(σ) ∈ {⌊L⌋, ⌈L⌉}；全以有理不等式精确判定
def kp_iter(sig, sm, q):
    k = 0
    while sig * q ** k > sm:
        k += 1
    return k

def ceil_log(r, q):  # min{n ≥ 0 : q^{-n} ≥ r}，即 ⌈ln r / ln(1/q)⌉（r ≥ 1）
    n = 0
    while (1 / q) ** n < r:
        n += 1
    return n

def floor_log(r, q):  # max{n ≥ 0 : q^{-n} ≤ r}
    n = 0
    while (1 / q) ** (n + 1) <= r:
        n += 1
    return n

rev_g = [kp_iter(s_, F(1, 4), F(1, 4)) for s_ in (F(1, 2), F(1))]
tbad, tn = 0, 0
for rho_ in (F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(3, 4)):
    q_ = 1 - rho_
    for sm in (F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(3, 8)):
        for sig in (F(k, 16) for k in range(1, 17)):
            if not sig > sm:
                continue
            for c_ in (F(5, 4), F(3, 2), F(2), F(3), F(4)):
                if c_ * sig > 1:
                    continue
                tn += 1
                k1, k2 = kp_iter(sig, sm, q_), kp_iter(c_ * sig, sm, q_)
                if k1 != ceil_log(sig / sm, q_) or (k2 - k1) not in (floor_log(c_, q_), ceil_log(c_, q_)):
                    tbad += 1
# 模型对拍：带符号 two_comp 的 k₊ 与 s_m = 1 − (m* + θ_γ)/n_A 的取整式
mb_bad = 0
~~~

for (eta2, U12, beta2, th1, rho1, sg2) in \[(F(1, 4), F(-1), F(5, 2), F(1, 4), F(1, 8), F(1)), (F(1, 8), F(-1), F(2), F(0), F(1, 4), F(1)),

~~~python
                                            (F(1, 4), F(-1), F(3), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(3, 2))]:
    Gm = two_comp(eta2, U12, beta2, th1, rho1, sgn2=sg2)
    mst = (beta2 - sg2) / (-U12)
    sm = 1 - (mst + th1) / 2
    for sig in (F(3, 4), F(1)):
        wk_ = at(Gm, {'g1:0': sig, 'g1:1': sig})
        kk = 0
        while JC.total_comp(Gm, wk_, 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0') > 0 and kk < 500:
            wk_ = Gm.step(wk_, 'rest')
            kk += 1
        mb_bad += not (0 < sm < 1) or kk != (0 if sig <= sm else ceil_log(sig / sm, 1 - rho1))
check('Q11g 推论 19-7″ 的取整形式：例 X12 ρ = 3/4、s_m = 1/4 时 σ = 1/2 与 1 的首达拍 %s（L = 1/2 < 1 拍，延迟相同仍符合）；%d 组（ρ、s_m、σ、c）中 k₊ = ⌈x⌉ 与差 ∈ {⌊L⌋, ⌈L⌉} 不符 %d；带符号 two_comp 上模型首达与取整式不符 %d' %
      (rev_g, tn, tbad, mb_bad), rev_g == [1, 1] and tbad == 0 and tn > 100 and mb_bad == 0)
# Q11h 尾证条件与「恒在零点」对「从正侧趋零」
Gt = two_comp(F(1, 4), F(-1), F(3), F(0), F(1, 8), sgn2=F(1))   # m* = 2 = MBON 上界（n_A = 2、θ_γ = 0），s_m = 0
def Wseq(Gm, sig, n):
    wk_ = at(Gm, {'g1:0': sig, 'g1:1': sig})
    out = []
    for _ in range(n):
        out.append(JC.total_comp(Gm, wk_, 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0'))
        wk_ = Gm.step(wk_, 'rest')
    return out

G0z = two_comp(F(1, 4), F(-1), F(3), F(0), F(0), sgn2=F(3, 2))  # ρ_γ = 0，m* = 3/2，s_m = 1/4
zero_line = Wseq(G0z, F(1, 4), 60)
~~~

~~~text
# Q11i 迟读的差传播：例 X13一拍安静弛豫 1/8 → 1/16；一般式 Σ_s W_s∏_{j>s} r_j（无夹限）；触夹限时须用实际转移

rng = random.Random(19303)
pbad, pn, pclamp = 0, 0, 0
for trial in range(200):
    ra = rng.choice([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)])
    Gd = BM.Graded3F(['g', 'a'], 1, {'A': (0,)}, {'g': 0, 'a': 0}, {'g': 0, 'a': 1}, {('g', 'a'): -1},
                     {'g': 1, 'a': 1}, {'g': F(1, 2), 'a': F(1, 8)}, {'g': rng.choice([F(1, 4), F(1, 2)]), 'a': ra}, signed={'a': F(1, 2)})
    st0 = {'g:0': rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(3, 4)]), 'a:0': F(1, 2)}
    L_ = 6
    tests = sorted(rng.sample(range(L_), rng.choice([1, 2])))
    seqT = ['A' if j in tests else 'rest' for j in range(L_)]
    wT, wN = dict(st0), dict(st0)
    Ws_ = {}
    clamped = False
    for j, sgl in enumerate(seqT):
        if sgl == 'A':
            Ws_[j] = JC.comp_of(Gd, wT, Gd.act(wT, 'A'), 'A', 'W_a', 'a:0')
            raw = wT['a:0'] + Ws_[j] - ra * wT['a:0']
            clamped = clamped or not (0 <= raw <= 1)
        wT, wN = Gd.step(wT, sgl), Gd.step(wN, 'rest')
    if clamped:
        pclamp += 1
        continue
    pred = sum(Ws_[j] * (1 - ra) ** (L_ - 1 - j) for j in Ws_)
    pn += 1
    pbad += (wT['a:0'] - wN['a:0']) != pred
~~~

~~~text
# ---- 定理 19-10：DAN 门、标记门、可置位门分层
crev = BM.Consol('exc', kappa=1, u=F(1, 2), theta_c=F(1, 2), tag=F(3, 4))
srev = dict(crev.init, **{'a:0': F(1, 2), 'a:1': F(1, 2)})
tag_case = (crev.act(srev, 'bg')['DAN_w'], crev.step(srev, 'bg')['c:0'])
thm_wc = BM.WindowCand('R', F(1, 4), 1, (F(1, 4),) * 4, F(1, 2)).dan((1, 1, 0, 0))
thm_cs = BM.Consol('exc', kappa=F(1, 4), u=F(1), bg=F(1, 4), theta_c=F(1, 2), theta_M=F(1, 2))
thm_cd = thm_cs.act(at(thm_cs, {'a:0': F(1), 'a:1': F(1)}), 'bg')['DAN_w']
old_floor = F(1, 4) + F(1) * F(1, 4) * (4 - 2)
# 例 X5 在两种构造下：K 设深 与 全设深
c1r = BM.Consol('exc', kappa=F(1), u=F(1, 2), bg=F(1, 4), theta_c=F(1, 2))
dan_K = c1r.act(at(c1r, {'a:0': F(1), 'a:1': F(1)}), 'bg')['DAN_w']
dan_all = c1r.act({x: F(x.startswith('a:')) for x in c1r.X}, 'bg')['DAN_w']
# 置位集的完整等价（K 设深、一拍、c = 0）：CONS(D) ⟺ D > τ_tag ∧（κ ≥ θ_c ∨ D ≤ D_c）
jbad, jn, jex = 0, 0, 0
for kap in (F(0), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)):
    for uu in (F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)):
        for bgv in (F(1, 4), F(1, 2)):
            for thM in (F(0), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)):
                for th in (F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(3, 4)):
                    for tg in (F(1, 20), F(1, 4), F(3, 4)):
                        Cm = BM.Consol('exc', kappa=kap, u=uu, bg=bgv, theta_c=th, tag=tg, theta_M=thM)
                        Dc = (kap + uu * (bgv * 4 - thM) - th) / (uu * bgv * 2)
                        Ds = sorted(set([F(k, 16) for k in range(17)] + ([Dc] if 0 <= Dc <= 1 else []) + ([tg] if tg <= 1 else [])))
                        got_set = []
                        for D in Ds:
                            jn += 1
                            got = JC.total_comp(Cm, at(Cm, {'a:0': D, 'a:1': D}), 'bg', 'CONS', 'c:0') == 1
                            want = D > tg and (kap >= th or D <= Dc)
                            jbad += got != want
                            got_set.append(got)
                        Y1 = Cm.act(at(Cm, {'a:0': F(1), 'a:1': F(1)}), 'bg')['DAN_w']
                        exists = any((D > tg) and not g_ for D, g_ in zip(Ds, got_set))
                        jex += exists != (tg < 1 and Y1 < th)
~~~

~~~python
# Q11l 窗末留存（定理 19-10（六））：首置位拍含标记门；L 加一拍不必多一个锯齿；置位不影响任一弛豫拍时无严格跃降
ce = BM.Consol('exc', kappa=F(0), u=F(1, 2), theta_c=F(3, 8), tag=F(1, 4))
Dc_e = (ce.kappa + ce.u * (ce.bgv * 4 - ce.theta_M) - ce.theta_c) / (ce.u * ce.bgv * 2)
def ltm_pred(Cm, D, L, Np, Dc):
    t = next((j for j in range(L) if Cm.tag < D * Cm.rho0 ** j <= Dc), None)
    return D * Cm.rho0 ** (L + Np) if t is None else D * Cm.rho0 ** (t + 1) * Cm.rho1 ** (L - 1 - t + Np)
lbad = 0
jumps = {}
Dgrid = [F(k, 32) for k in range(1, 33)]
for L in (1, 2, 3, 4, 5):
    vals = []
    for D in Dgrid:
        got = retain(ce, at(ce, {'a:0': D, 'a:1': D}), L, 10)
        lbad += got != ltm_pred(ce, D, L, 10, Dc_e)
        vals.append(got)
    jumps[L] = sum(1 for a_, b_ in zip(vals, vals[1:]) if b_ < a_)
lastbeat = (retain(ce, at(ce, {'a:0': F(1, 2), 'a:1': F(1, 2)}), 1, 0), F(1, 2) * ce.rho0)
~~~

~~~python
# Q11v 19-H（四）：两臂都先训练、保留拍前历史，见证限于训练后的取证窗
def cert_window(host, hist, T, k0):
    states = JC.run(host, host.init, hist)
    for k in range(k0, len(hist)):
        sub_states, sub_sigs = states[:k + 1], hist[:k + 1]
        Uav = JC.avail_at(host, states, k, T, EPS)
        if not Uav:
            continue
        w = states[k]
        s = hist[k]
        y = host.act(w, s)
        for (p, x, _c) in host.comps(w, y, s):
            if x not in T or abs(JC.comp_of(host, w, y, s, p, x)) <= EPS:
                continue
            st, _wt = JC.hc_status(host, w, s, p, x, Uav, EPS)
            if st == '有效差异已获证':
                return '1', (k, s, p)
    return '未获证', None
hv_ = ['A+', 'rest', 'A', 'rest']
cv_r = cert_window(G0r, hv_, T_, 1)
cv_n = cert_window(G0n, hv_, T_, 1)
~~~
